 Catalog number : I00041
Uniform title: Āgamagrantha
Manuscript : IFP manuscript bundle RE30718
Description:
RE30718 Āgamagranthaḥ. This bundle has collected different topics from various agamic texts. A significant portion is drawn from Śrīmadaghoraśivācāryaviracita Kriyākramadyotikā, covering topics such as Dvārādipratiṣṭhāvidhi, Vināyakotsavādibhaktotsavāntavidhi, Bṛhattālādiṛkṣotsavāntavidhi and Prāyaścittavidhi. Adding to that, a section includes excerpts from the Svāyambhuvāgama detailing the Śivarātrividhi and the Caryāpāda of Suprabhedāgama elaborating on Śaivotpatti (origin of Śaivism), the Pañcākṣaravidhi, and the Snapanavidhi from Siddhāntaśekhara.

The bundle contains 119 folios, and there are 5-7 lines in each folio. The size of the folio is 16x2 cm. This manuscript provides an extensive view of Āgamic rituals and practices, offering insights into both everyday and festival-specific worship. Its diverse content from multiple Āgamic sources and related commentaries makes it an indispensable reference for scholars and practitioners of Śaiva Āgamas.
Notes:

prastaro'nyasya hṛdayaṃ vedi skandho galo galaḥ ।
śiraśśiraḥśikhācūliprāsādo dehavānayam ॥

śukanāsyordhve prāgvedyā (l.3) bhittigarbhe hṛtpadmavāddhṛdā । kumbhaṃ saṃsthāpya nirodhayet । tataḥ kuṇḍasamīpaṃ gatvā । prāyaścittārthamastreṇa hutvā । yāgaṃ (l.4) visṛjet । tato dadyāt guroḥ kartā mūlapādona-dakṣiṇām ।

atha cūlikāpratiṣṭhā –
pūrvavat sūryadvārapālapūjā (l.5) mantratarpaṇāntaṃ karmaṃ(karma) vidhāya । śikhipādayuktacūlikāṃ saṃsnāpyāgnivāsanamaṇṭape samānīya । śayyāyāṃ pūrvaśiraskaṃ vinsya । vṛttabhāge – śivatattvarudraṃ śaktyantamaṣṭa......ge vidyātattvaṃ viṣṇuṃ sadāśivāntaṃ caturaśre tvātmatattvaṃ brahmāṇaṃ māyāntaṃ (p.11a, l.1) vinyasya । kṣmādimūrtiḥ śarvādimūrtīśāśca pratikhaṇḍaṃ krameṇa vinyasya । nirudhya । kuṇḍe śaktinyāsapurassaraṃ pratibhāgaṃ pūrvavaddhṛtva bhatvā japtvā sarvaṃ pūrvavadācaret । (l.2)

atha sthūpipratiṣṭhāvidhiḥ –
prāsāde kāritasnāne karṇordhve śikhāntaṃ śivatattvaṃ rudraṃ kaṇṭhe । vidyātattvaṃ viṣṇuṃ sadāśivāntaṃ jaṅghāyāṃ (l.3) brahmāṇaṃ māyāntaṃ pūrvavat mūrtiṃ mūrtīśāśca pratibhāgaṃ sṛṣṭikrameṇa vinyasya । pūrvavaddhṛtvā । gatvā । japtvā sarvaṃ pūrvavadācaret । tataḥ cūlikā(l.4)dhāre ratnādipañcakaṃ mūlena vinyasya । culikāṃ saṃsthāpya । tatkālocitā phalaiḥ nālikeraphalaṃ caikaṃ nāraṅga(l.5)dvitayaṃ tathā । ḍāḍimilikucaṃ caiva kadalīpanasā mṛjāḥ । ekaikaghaṭānāpūrya taiḥ prāsādasnānaṃ vidadhyāt । (l.6) tato daṇḍaṃ saṃsnāpya । dhvajamastreṇa saṃprokṣya । kavacenāvakuṇṭhya । prāsādottaravedikāśayayāyāṃ viniveśya । pūrvava(p.11b, f.8a, l.1)dubhayorātmavidyātattvaṃ daṇḍe dhvaje śivatattvaṃ seśvaraṃ mūrti-mūrtīśāṃśca sṛṣṭyā vinyasya । nirudhya । homaṃ kṛtvā । gatvā । spṛṣṭyā(ṣṭvā) japtvā sarvaṃ pūrvavadviśodhya । (l.2) daṇḍe śivam । sakaladhvaje niṣkalaṃ śivamaṅgāni ca vinyasya । dhvajadaṇḍādhāre ṣaḍutthāsanaṃ datvādhāraśaktiṃ vinyasya । mūrtibhūtadaṇḍaṃ pratiṣṭhāpyāghorāstraṃ (l.3) prapūjayet । tadanu dhvajaṃ saṃyojya । tatra pāśupatāstraṃ saṃpūjya । vidyāṅgamantrai-rgandhādi datvā । praṇamya । prāsādaṃ sarvatattvaṃ dhyātvā । (l.4)
anantaṃ kālarudraṃ ca vibhāvya ca pādāmbuje ।
kūśmāṇḍahāḍakau pīṭhe pātālanarakaiḥ saha ॥
bhuvanairlokapālaiśca śatarudrādibhi(bhiḥ) vratam । (l.5)
brahmāṇḍakamiti dhyātvā jaṅghāyāṃ ca vibhāvayet ॥
vāritejo'nilavyomapañcāṣṭakasamanvitam ।
garbhāvaraṇasaṃjñaṃ ca buddhiṃ yonyaṣṭakānvitam ॥ (l.6)
yonyaṣṭakasamāyuktaṃ nāsāvidhi guṇakra(tra)yam ।
ghaṭasthaṃ puruṣaṃ siṃhe vṛṣe ca paribhāvayet ॥
mañjari vedikāyāṃ ca vidyādikacatuṣṭayam ।
kaṇṭhe *māyāṃ sarudraṃ ca vidyāṃ cāmaladhārake ॥* (l.7)
kalaśe ceśvaraṃ binduvidyeśvarasamanvitam ।
jaṭācūḍaṃ ca taṃ vidyāt sthalaṃ candrārdharūpakam ॥
śaktitrayaṃ ca *tatraiva daṇḍe nādaṃ vibhāvya ca ।* (p.12a, l.1)
dhvaje kuṇḍaliniśaktimiti dhāva vibhāvayet ॥
jagamyādhāma sandhāya liṅgapiṇḍikayoryathā ।

	iti dhyātvā । śivaṃ ca vyāpakatvena vinyasya । (l.2) kuṇḍasamīpaṃ gatvā । prāyaścittaṃ śivenāghorāstreṇa japahomau vidhāya । yāgaṃ visṛjet । guruṃ bhojayet । kartā svarṇācchādanabhojanaiḥ ॥

atha jīrṇoddhāravidhiḥ – (l.3)
	prāsādasya bahirīśāno(ne) uttāraṇe vā dvāratoraṇādimaṇṭapaṃ vidhāya । tatrāpi dvārapūjādisthaṇḍila.... vārcanamantratarpaṇāntaṃ karmaṃ(karma) kṛtvā (l.4) vāstudevāśca saṃpūjyāhutitrayaṃ datvā । sahasramaghorāstreṇa liṅgacalanārthaṃ hutvā । tato digbaliṃ bahirdatvācamya sakalīkṛtya liṅginā (l.5) ........tānāṃ ca bhojanādinā tṛpti........ । sahāyontaḥ praviśyā(śya) śivaṃ vijñāpayeddoṣa-duṣṭaliṅgamidaṃ prabho । asya coddhāraṇe (l.6) śakti(ktiḥ) syādastviti bhavadvacaḥ । tasmādatra vidhānena māmadhiṣṭhe maheśvara evamadhyeṣya eyehi yehi vyāpakeśvara । hṛdayāya namaḥ । (l.7) vyāpakeśvaraśirase svāhā । vyāpakeśvaraśikhāyai vauṣaṭ । vyāpakeśvarakavacāya hum । vyāpakeśvaranetrebhyo vauṣaṭ । vyāpa(p.12b, f.9, l.1)keśvarāstrāya phaṭ । evamaṅgāni sahitaṃ saṃpūjya । liṅgasthaṃ sattvaṃ śrāvayet । satvaḥ ko'pi ...yaḥ kaśca liṅgamāśritya tiṣṭhati । liṅgaṃ tyatvā(ktvā) śivajñāto yatrodanna pragacchata । (l.2) vidyāvidyeśvarairyuktaḥ śambhurajñaṃ na viṣyati । evamuktvāstreṇārghyaṃ datvā । visarjayet । tataḥ pāśupatāstreṇa pratibhāgaṃ śataṃ sahasraṃ hutvā śāntyambhasā (l.3) prokṣya । kuśai(śaiḥ) spṛṣṭvā । japtvā । pratilomyenārghyaṃ datvā । tattvatattveśvarā(rāḥ) mūrtimūrtīśāśca visarjayet । kriyāśaktiṃ pīṭhasthamantrāpi liṅga(l.4)bhāgatrayamantrān ṣaḍadhvājñānaśakti.....visṛjya । tat caṇḍeśaṃ ca visṛjyāstreṇa khanitreṇa khātvā sañjalanavṛttaṃ svarṇapāśādinā liṅgaṃ piṇḍikāṃ (l.5) brahmaśilāṃ ca badhvā । vṛṣaskandhe samāropya । śivamastva(stvi)ti gṛhṇan janaiḥ sārdhaṃ bahirnirgatvā gāḍhajale kṣipet । tataḥ śintya? rthaṃ tri madhunātta? dūrvāyā śataṃ (l.6) hutvā । dikptīnāṃ tri(tṛ)ptyarthaṃ tattadigīśamantrai(ntraiḥ) hutvā । prāsādavāstuśuddhyarthamastreṇa śataṃ hutvā । pāśupadāstreṇa prāsādaṃ saṃrakṣet । tatra pūrvavat (l.7) pramāṇasiddhaliṅgādi saṃpādya sthāpayet । eṣa eva vidhiḥ kāryo jīrṇadhāmasamuddhṛtau । khaḍge mantragaṇān nyasya । kārayonmandirāntaram । (p.13a, l.1) śambarāt pūjayeddhāmasidhyantaṃ khaḍgasaṃyutān samācare(ret) liṅgarakṣāṃ pūrvamānena kārayet । saṅkocaraṇaṃ? proktaṃ vistāre tu dhanakṣayam । taddravyaśreṣṭhadravyaṃ (l.2) vā kāraye(yet) tatpramāṇataḥ ।

atha svāyambhuvādipratiṣaṭhā -
svāyambhuvādipratiṣṭhāyāṃ pīṭhasyaiva tu saṃsmṛtiḥ ।
tattvatattveśamūrtīśavrā?sahomādipūrvavat ॥
svato liṅga....sya *devatvāt stapanādipunarmatam ।* (l.3)
saṃhitānyāsanaṃ pūjāṃ śāntaye haṃsapañcaret ॥
svāyambhuvakabāṇaliṅgaṃ prāgdevaiḥ samskṛtaṃ yataḥ ।
tatra notkirellakṣma nāsya *ta...thādikalpanā ॥* (l.4)
bhogyādyarthaṃ gatirnātra caturthī caṇḍakalpanām ।
rohacitsannidhiḥ kāryā samskāro bāṇaliṅgavat ॥
svayambhubāṇa*liṅgaṃ vā sarveṣāṃ bhuktimuktidam ।* (l.5)
sahasraguṇamanyasmāt phalaṃ sthāpanapūjanāt ॥
susaṃsthānaṃ visaṃsthānaṃ sarvasiddhikaraṃ nṛṇām ।
pratiṣṭhā*vidhiruktoyamatha sthāpanamucyate ॥* (l.6)
calaliṅge pṛthakpīṭhe bhakteśaiḥ pañcabhiḥ stri(tri)bhiḥ ।
dvau bhāgau bhāgamekaṃ vā vinyaset piṇḍikāvaṭe ॥ (l.7)
tadardhaṃ vinyaset pīṭhe yathāśāstra(straṃ) samācaret ।
liṅganāhasamapīṭhavistaraṃ vistarasya śaravedatuṅgakam ॥
vistarārdhamatha gomukhānta*samakaṇṭhanāhakam ।* (p.13b, l.1)
tatrāśābhavetryaṃśo dvyaṃśo vātha svaśambaraiḥ ॥
pūrvavat samskṛte bāṇe cale pīṭhe susamskṛte ।
kramāt *jñānakriyāśaktiṃ nyasedityādi pūrvavat ॥* (l.2)
iti sthāpanamākhyāta(taṃ) sthitisthāpanamucyate ।

calaliṅgapratiṣṭhai –
sapīṭhe calaliṅge ca prakṛtyā sā ṭīkādike । (l.3)
prāgvat sarvavidhiḥ kintu liṅgapiṇḍikayoriha ॥
abhedāt samskṛtiḥ kārya(ryaṃ) maṇḍape vā gṛhe'thavā ।
kiñcit brahmaśilāratnaprabhṛteśca niveśanam ॥ (l.4)
yojanaṃ piṇḍikāyāśca manasā parikalpayet ।
cale'pi samskṛtiḥ prāgvat bāṇaliṅge ca rohake ॥
liṅgamastakavistāraṃ *pūjābhāgasamaṃ nayet ।* (l.5)
nāhaṃ na triguṇaṃ kuryāt nāhavakapīṭhavismṛtam ॥
prajāśadviguṇenaiva pīṭhasyonnatimācaret ।
vṛttaṃ vā caturaśraṃ vā *madhye kaṇṭhasamanvitam ॥* (l.6)
dviguṇaṃ liṅganāhaṃ sya(hamasya) kaṇṭhanāgaṃ samācaret ।
taduccamapi kurvīta liṅgoccadviguṇaṃ budhaḥ ॥
liṅgadairghyasamaṃ *kuryāt pranālaṃ pīṭhabāhyataḥ ।* (p.14a, l.1)
maṇṭape vā gṛhe vātha sarvapīṭhāśraye ca te ॥
prāguktasthirabāṇādinyāsanyāye'tra ca smṛtaḥ ।
dhātujaṃ ratnajaṃ vāpi sa yo'nyatha *viyoni vā ॥* (l.2)
sthāpayet sthiravalliṅgaṃ dhāturatnādimānuṣam ।
prayogayogabāhulyāt kriyā caiva kriyocyate ॥

	pūrvoktamaṇṭape gṛhādau dīkṣā(l.3)maṇṭape yā pīṭhabalayuktaliṅge sthāpanaṃ kuryāt । tatra liṅgapiṇḍike pañcagavyādibhiḥ pūrvavat saṃsnāpya । candanādibhirvilipya । pīṭhikādau niveśya । (l.4) bāṇaliṅge samhitāmantranyāsapūrvaṃ taiḥ saṃpūjya । sahasramardhaṃ vā juhuyāt । rohe nadisamurthe(dre) vā liṅge jīvanyāsaṃ vidhāya । saṃhitānyāsa(l.5)homau kuryāt । yadvā jīvanyāsaśca tattvatattveśādinyāsapūrvakatvāt pūrvavat ṣaṭtriṃśatattvāni sādhipāni śivavidyātmatattvāni rudraviṣṇu(l.6)brahmāṇaśca vinyasya paścimādidikṣu pṛthivyā(vya)ptejo vāyvākāśākhyā mūrtistadīśvarī vāmā jyeṣṭhā kriyā jñānīcchākhyā ca vinyasya । oṃ (l.7) hrīṃ kriyāśaktaye namaḥ । iti tanule jīvanyāsaṃ vidhāya । pūrvavat saṃrakṣya । hutvā । japtvā । sarvaṃ kṛtvā । liṅge jñānaśaktiṃ pīṭhe kriyāśaktiṃ vinyasya । (p.14b, f.11a, l.1) lagne brahmaśilā(yāṃ) ratnādikaniveśanam । piṇḍikāyojanaṃ ca vidhāya । pīṭhaliṅgayoḥ bhagaliṅgasamāyogaṃ śaktiśivamantrābhyāṃ vidhāya । pīṭhe ādhāraśaktyādi(l.2)śaktyantāsanamantrānvinyasya liṅge mūrtiṃ vidyādehaṃ netraṃ ca vinyasya । mūlenāvāhyā(hya) pūrvavat pañcopacāreṇa japāntaṃ saṃpūjyā-mukeśvarā iti (l.3) nāma datvā । candrāt tārakaṃ yāvadatra sannidhirbhaveti vijñāpya । praṇamya । mūlamantromāmantrābhyāṃ tilaiḥ pāyasena mṛtyuñcitā śataṃ pṛthak hutvā । (l.4) prāyaścittārthaṃ ca mūlena śataṃ pūrṇāsahitaṃ juhuyāt । yajamāno yāvajjīvaṃ pūjayāmīti saṃkalpya । gurupūjāṃ kṛtvā । pratidina(l.5)muktavat pūjayet ।

atha kṣaṇikaliṅgapūjāvidhiḥ –
	mṛdbhasmakuśamṛtpiṣṭagulakhaṇḍādijanmanāṃ tatkālakānāṃ liṅgānāṃ vikṣaṇādyabhimantraṇaiḥ viśodhya । (l.6) vidhinā nityamarcanādaihakaṃ phalaṃ prāpnute vatsarāntena visṛjyā(jya) saṃpravāhayet ।

atha citraliṅgādipratiṣṭhāvidhiḥ –
	paṭapustaka*lekhyānāṃ darpaṇe snānamācaret ।* (l.7)
	dhāraṇābhirviśuddhi syāt sthāpane lekhyacitrayoḥ ॥
	netrodghāṭanamantre'ṣṭamāsanādi*prakalpanam ।* (p.15a, l.1)
	teṣāmadhasthādasparśāt puṣpaiḥ pūjā nimbubhiḥ ॥
	dhūmantra(ntraṃ) na doṣāya śilāratnādimānasam ।
	sthitasthāpanamākhyātamāsthāpanamathocyate ॥ (l.2)
	vyaktārcāsvapi pūrvoktavidhiścaṇḍeśvaraṃ vinā ।
	ku(kū)rmādyaṃ ki(ka)thitaṃ mayacca ratnādyādipañcakam ॥
	prakṣipet pīṭhagartāyāṃ tacca brahmaśilāṃ vinā ।
	ṣaḍbhirvibhaṃ? *jite garbhe tyatvā(ktvā) bhāgaṃ ca pṛṣṭhataḥ ॥* (l.3)
	sthāpanaṃ pañcabhāgo'yaṃ yadi yā vasubhājite ।
	sthāpanaṃ saptame bhāge pratimāsu sukhāvahaḥ ॥ (l.4)

	pūrvavanmaṇṭape mantratṛptyantaṃ karma kṛtvā devīṃ pūrvavanmṛdādibhiḥ snāpya । netronmīlanaṃ kṛtvā । punaḥ saṃsnāpya । śayyāyāmadhiropya । nidrā(l.5)kumbhaṃ nidhāya । saṃpūjya । naivedyādi-gṛhopakaraṇāntaṃ saṃpādya । pādadeśe sthitvā । kṣityādiśaktyāntāni sādhipāni (l.6) vinyasya । jānusthāne kaṇṭhasthāne ca trikhaṇḍadarśanārthaṃ puṣpamālāpratisaraṃ datvā । kaṇṭhordhve śivatattvamicchāśaktiṃ śaktyantaṃ jānmūrdhve? vidyātattvaṃ (p.15b, f.12a, l.1) jñāniṃ sadāśivāntaṃ jānvadhasthādātmatattvaṃ māyāntaṃ kriyāśaktiṃ vinyasya । trikhaṇḍaṃ kṣmāvahniyajamānāt jalavāyvibu(bhu)khāni ca । dhārikā divyamatyugrā (l.2) jyotsnā cetā balotkaṭā । dhātriṃ vibhāvya siśca vinyaset । yadvā lalāṭāntamākāśamūrtimicchāṃ hṛdayāntaṃ vāyumūrtiṃ jānuṃ nābhyantamagnimūrtikriyā (l.3) jānvantaṃ jalamūrtijyeṣṭhāṃ pādāntaṃ pṛthvīmūrtiṃ vāmaṃ vinyasya । jānugalaśirovadhi tritattvaṃ seśvarīśca vinyaset । oṃ hrīṃ sauṃ ma....gau....(l.4)rudradayitetyanena yadvā kavargādyaṃ svarai raudraṃ cāturyaṃ svarasaṃjñake vyāpake navamāyārṇamādyapadamiti kramāt । nāda(l.5)mūṣmāttriyahaṅkādyaṃ prāṇaśca vyāpakena ca । māyābhaja?miti prāntaḥ । padūmadhyamamādiśekakritiyamūṣmamadhye ca sparśe cādyamataḥ pa.... । graha(l.6)saṃkhyaṃ vyāpake ca māyābījaṃ mahāmanurityanena jīvanyāsaṃ vidhāya । tiladarbhādibhiḥ prākāratrayaṃ dhya? vidhāya । pīṭhe māyā śuddhavidyā (p.16a, l.1) sa deśāntaṃ trikhaṇḍasthaṃ tattvatrayamīśvarībhiḥ saha vinyasya । oṃ raṃ hrīṃ kra(kri)yāśaktaye namaḥ । iti vinyasya । niruśca(dhya) । mantrasaṃhitāśca pūrvavat santarpya । devīsamīpaṃ gatvā । spṛṣṭvā । (l.2) kuṇḍe'pi pūrvavadādhāraśaktiṃ vinyasya । tattvatattveśvarāṃ mūrtimūrtīśvarīśca japtvā । sandhāya । sarvaṃ pūrvavat pratikhaṇḍaṃ kṛtvā । sulagne garbhaṃ praviśya । pūrvaṃ madhyaṃ parīkṣya । (l.3) pīṭhikāṃ oṃ sauṃ kriyāśaktaye namaḥ । iti vinyasya । tatra ratnādipañcakaṃ vinyasya । aindrāgne dhityādimantrairāsanaṃ pūjya । tatra gaurīmūrtiṃ śakya(ktya)ntau (l.4) sṛṣṭyā vinyasya śrīkaṇṭhanyāsaṃ ca kṛtvā । oṃ sauṃ kriyāśaktaye namaḥ । iti pīṭhe । oṃ hrīṃ jñānaśaktaye namaḥ । iti gaurīmūrtiṃ vinyasya । tanmūlena (l.5) parāśaktimāvāhya । saṃsthāpyāmukeśvarīti nama kṛtvā saṃpūjayet । mantrā yathā – oṃ hāṃ ām ādhāraśaktaye namaḥ । īṃ kandāya namaḥ । ūṃ (l.6) nālāya namaḥ । ṛṃ dharmāya namaḥ । ṝṃ jñānāya namaḥ । ḷṃ vairāgyāya namaḥ । ḹm aiśvaryāya namaḥ । eṃ adharmāya namaḥ । aiṃ ajñānāya namaḥ । oṃ aum (p.16b, l.1) raudrāya namaḥ । aum anaiśvaryāya namaḥ । am adhacchadanāya namaḥ । aḥ ūrdhvachadanāya namaḥ । paṃ padmāya namaḥ । raṃ haṃ kesarebhyo namaḥ । saṃ karṇikāyai namaḥ । (l.2) kṣaṃ puṣkarebhyo namaḥ । hrām icchāyai namaḥ । hrīṃ dūtyai namaḥ । hrūṃ kriyāyai namaḥ । hreṃ vāce namaḥ । hraiṃ vāgīśvaryai namaḥ । hroṃ (l.3) jvālinyai namaḥ । hrauṃ vāmāyai namaḥ । iti navaśakti(kta)yaḥ । gāṃ gauryāsanāya namaḥ । gauṃ gaurimūrtaye namaḥ । hrīṃ sauḥ mahāgaurī rudradayite namaḥ । (l.4) yadvā taṃ mūlamahāvidyāpañcadaśākṣaryā vāvāhya । gāṃ hṛdayāya namaḥ । gaḥ astrāya phaṭ । athavā hrūṃ hū iti hrāṃ lakṣitāyai namaḥ । (l.5) hrīṃ kāminyai namaḥ । sauḥ kāmamālinyai namaḥ । iti gaurisakhīnāmevaṃ japāntaṃ pañcopacāreṇa pañcāvaraṇasaptāvaraṇanavāvaraṇaṃ (l.6) vā saṃpūjya । prāyaścittahomaṃ kṛtvā । caṇḍaṃ vināyakathāpūrvaṃ caturthadivasāvadhi vidadhyāt ।

atha sūryapratiṣṭhāvidhiḥ – (p.17a, l.1)
vidyāntāsanaśayyāyā kṛtasnānādikaṃ ravi(viṃ) tu vinyasettatra tattvāni seśvaratrayamuktavat । māyā sadeśaśatya(ktya)ntaṃ vivātāgnyaṃ līlā nyaset । sadāśiveśvarau (l.2) rudraṃ viṣṇuṃ brahmāṇa-mityapi । oṃ hrāṃ hrīṃ saḥ śivasūryāya namaḥ । iti jīvanyāsam । māyā vidyā sadeśāntaṃ pīṭhe traikhaṇḍakalpanam । śaktyā ca sarvamomukhyā (l.3) saṃsthāpya । vidhivattataḥ । sthāṇunā vidhivat sūryaṃ śaktyantaṃ sthāpayedguruḥ ।

atha vighneśapratiṣṭhāvidhiḥ –
vidyāntāsanaśayyāyāṃ kṛtasnānaṃ gaṇādhipam । (l.4)
nyastvā tattvatrayaṃ seśaṃ māyā vidyā sadeśikam ॥
jānukaṇṭhaśirontaṃ tatkhavātāgnyaṃ sisā nyaset ।
sadāśiveśvarau rudraṃ viṣṇu*brahmāṇamityapi ॥* (l.5)
kṣmāvahniyajamānārkajalavātendukhāni ca ।
āmodaśca pramodaśca sumukho durmukhastathā ॥
avighno vighnarājaśca *bhakṣyādhipañcahastakaḥ ।* (l.6)
ete'ṣṭamūrtayo khyātā manmenākṣarodbhavāḥ ॥
aṣṭāviṃśativarṇākhyamatha ṣaḍvarṇakāṣṭakam । (p.17b, f.14a, l.1)
tanmūlena samāvāhya pūjayedvidhipūrvakam ॥
māyā vidyeśvarāntaṃ tatpīṭhe traikhaṇḍakalpanam ।

atha skandapratiṣṭhāvidhiḥ –
māyāntāsanaśayyāyāṃ *kṛtasnānādikaṃ guham ।* (l.2)
ātmavidyāśivākhyaṃ ca jānukaṇṭhaśirovithi ॥
aṇḍayiśuddhavidyāntaṃ nyastvā mūrtyādipañcakam ।
aṣṭapakṣe *tadā proktā kṣmādau vidyeśvarādhipāḥ ॥* (l.3)
ṣaṇmukhaḥ śaktipāṇiśca kārtikeyo guhastathā ।
skando mayūravāhaśca senāni ca *viśākhakaḥ ॥* (l.4)
aṇḍadhi mohiniprāntaṃ pīṭhe traikhaṇḍakalpanam ।
vāme va dakṣapārśve ca senāvallisamanvitam ॥
kalātiśirasākhyaṃ cāmanūnā*muttamottamam ।* (l.5)
pañcopacāravidhinā pañcāvaraṇapūrvakam ॥
samāvāhya susaṃpūjya kuṇḍepyevaṃ prabūjayet ।
kuṇḍaṃ ṣaḍaśra*mīśāne pāvake vā prakīrtitām ॥* (l.6)

(atha liṅgodbhavādipratiṣṭhā –)
atha liṅgodbhavādipratiṣṭhāsūcanamekena ślokenāha ।
liṅgaṃ purārinṛtteśaṃ *pramukhānapi pūrvavat ॥* (p.18a, l.1)
anuktamantrasāmānyasthāpanoditamācaret ।
pratiṣṭhāṃ kārayedva(dvi)....ptā sadāśivapadāntakam ॥
pūrvavadvediśayyāyāṃ du....*snātāṃ kramā(māt) nyaset ।* (l.2)
ātmavidyā śiva(vā)khyaṃ ca jānukarku śiro'vadhi ॥
māyā sadeśaśaktyantaṃ vinyaset kṣmādayo yathā ।
vahniyajamānārkajalava.....ndukhāni ca ॥ (l.3)
tamo mohakṣayā nidrā mṛtyurmāyāhayājarā ।
pañcadhā vātamoghorārātiravajvarātisrodhavākrakriyājñāni...cchā vā adhi(l.4)nāyikāḥ । ātmādiṣu tritatveṣu ti(tī)vramūrtiṣu vinyaset । māyā vidyā sadeśāntaṃ pīṭhe krai(trai)khaṇḍakalpanā । tithi-saṅkhyākhya(l.5)varṇena pūjayedvidhipūrvakam ।

(atha dakṣiṇāmūrtipratiṣṭhāvidhiḥ -)
dakṣiṇāmūrtipratiṣṭhāvidhiḥ sūcanam ।
kauśikaḥ kāśyapaścaiva jamadagnirvasiṣṭhikaḥ ।
saṃsthitā *jayaścaite dakṣiṇeśasya dakṣiṇe ॥* (l.6)
bhāradvājastatho'gastyo gautamaścātrireva ca ।
ṛṣayaḥ pṛthivītattve tattvānāṃ kalpaye *trayam ॥* (p.18b, f.15a, l.1)
ātmānnarātmā paramātmāna sa tatrādhipān nyaset ।
tanmūlenaiva mantreṇa dakṣiṇeśaṃ samarcayet ॥
atha brahmaḥ pratiṣṭhāsūcitamāhā(ha) brahmāṇamatha (l.2) śayyāyāṃ kṛtasnānaṃ nidhāya ca । jānukaṇṭhaśiro'ntaṃ tattritatvā vinyaset kramāt । lokapālāśca paritaḥ yadvā dhātāṣṭaka(kaṃ) nyaset । aṇḍa yimo hi (l.3) niprāntaṃ pīṭhe traikhaṇḍakalpanam । ṣaḍakṣareṇa mantreṇa sarvaṃ pūrvavadācaret ।

atha viṣṇupratiṣṭhāvidhiḥ -
vāyudevapratiṣṭhāyāṃ *jñeyā tattvatrayaṃ punaḥ ।* (l.4)
tatpīṭhe va prakṛtyantaṃ pra(prā)gvat prajñāntamamani ॥
tanmātrāṇi pradhauṃ prastatvametat triṃ(tri)kaṃ haraiḥ ।
vāsudevasthaṃ *karṣaṇapradyutnaśca trivikramaḥ ॥* (l.5)
vāmanaśrīdharaścāruhriṣīkeśastathā mataḥ ।
tathā dāmodaraśceti mūrtipā kṣmādayo'ṣṭa ca ॥
pañcapakṣe *vāsudevaḥ saṃkarṣaṇastathaiva ca । * (l.6)
pra[tya]dyumnaścāniruddhaśca śrīdharo mūrtipā smṛtāḥ ॥
śrībījena prakṛtyantaṃ vyālikaṃ piṇḍikāṃ na(nya)set । (l.7)
aṇḍabuddhipradhānāntaṃ tritatvaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ padam ॥
aṇḍāhaṃ kṛtivṛ(bu)ddhyantaṃ phalaṃ traikhaṇḍakalpanā ।
aṣṭākṣareṇa mantreṇa pūjaye*diti pūrvakaḥ ॥* (p.19a, l.1)
ardhanārīśvarasyāṇḍabuddhirmāyāvadhisthitam ।
śrītattvaṃ kalpayet pīṭhe cāṇḍabuddhikalāvidhi ॥
evaṃ harihareśo'pi sthāpanīyo *runamaiḥ? ।* (l.2)
haryardhe pūrvava(vat) jñeyaṃ kra(kri)yā mantrā yathāvidhi(dhiḥ) ॥
kṣetrapāle'ṇḍadhi śuddhavidyāntaṃ tattvakalpanā ।
pīṭhe'ṇḍabuddhimāyāntaṃ kalpayet pūrvavadvidhim ॥ (l.3)
hetughnaśastripuraghnaśca vahniśca yamajihvakaḥ ।
kalaḥ karālikaścaiva ekāṅghrirbhama ityatapi ॥
yadvā –
analaścāgnikesaśca karālacakritiyakaḥ । (l.4)
ghaṇṭāravo mahākrodha ṣaṣṭhe vai piśitāśanaḥ ॥
saptamapiṅgalākṣaśca ūrdhvakeśastathā mataḥ ।
ete ca mūrtipā(pāḥ) caiva *kṣmāgnyādyā mūrtaya(yaḥ) smṛtāḥ ॥* (l.5)
nandyādīnāṃ pratiṣṭhāyāmevaṃ vyāptiṃ prakalpayet ।
trikhaṇḍakalpanāḥ saptamātṝṇāmevameva hi ॥ (l.6)
brāhmī māheśvarī caiva kaumārī vaiṣṇavī tathā ।
vārāhī caiva māhendrī cāmuṇḍā vīrabhadrakaḥ ॥
vīravighnaśca vighneśa *pratimā kalpayetsudhīḥ ।* (p.19b, f.16a, l.1)
yaralavaśeṣasahā(ha) bījaṃ gaṃ gaṇapasya ca ॥
māyanalasamāyuktaṃ bindunādasamanvitaḥ ।
sthāpayetpratimāstatra virūpaṃ *citrito mukham ।* (l.2)
uttarābhimukhā devī sthāpayeddeśikottamaḥ ॥
pūrvābhimukhamevaṃ tu vighneśaṃ sthāpayettataḥ ।
śarabhaṃ paścime deśe *tanmantreṇa samācaret ॥* (l.3)

atha bhaktapratiṣṭhāvidhiḥ -
sā(ghā)ntamādyasvaropetaṃ binduśayyuktaṃ yathā bhavet ।
ātmamantraḥ samākhyo'haṃ sa *śaśabdo'tha vāmataḥ ॥* (l.4)
sāntenaiva ca mantreṇa na brahmāni ca kalpayet ।
brahmāṅgāni ca pādyasyādādyavarṇastadā manuḥ ॥
hrīṃkārastrīpratiṣṭhāyāṃ *tena brahmāṅgakalpanā ।* (l.5)
ātmavidyāśivākhyaṃ tu tattvatrayamudāhṛda(ta)m ॥
ātmānamantarātmānaṃ paramātmānameva cā(ca) ।
tattvatrayārthapatyena *krameṇa viniveśayet ॥* (l.6)
strīmāmapyevameva syāt kṣmādayo mūrti yo mataḥ ।
aṣṭau syuḥ pañcapakṣe tu kṣmādayaḥ *pañca eva hi ॥* (p.20a, l.1)
indrādyā mūrtipā pakṣe nivṛtyādyāstu mūrtipāḥ ।
tattatpīṭhāni vinyasya mūrtimūrtistanau nyaset ॥
akārādikṣakārāntaṃ seśaṃ mūrdhādi *vinyaset ।* (l.2)
niṣkale tu kalānyāsaṃ śrīkaṇṭhaṃ sakale nyaset ॥

atha śrīkaṇṭhanyāsam – tadyathā –
oṃ hāṃ śrīṃ hrīṃ aṃ śrīkaṇṭhāya pūrṇodaryai namaḥ । iti keśāntam । (l.3) anantāya namaḥ । virajarāyai namaḥ । lalāṭe – iṃ sūkṣmāya namaḥ । śa(śā)lmalyai namaḥ । dakṣiṇanetre – ī(īṃ) trimūrtaye namaḥ । lolākṣyai namaḥ । (l.4) vāmanetre – um amareśāya namaḥ । vartulākṣyai namaḥ । dakṣiṇaśrotre – ūm arpiśāya namaḥ । dīrghakoṇārthaye namaḥ । vāmaśrotre – (l.5) ṛṃ bhārabhūtāya namaḥ । dīrghamukhyai namaḥ । dakṣiṇanāsāpuṭe – ṝṃ nidhaneśāya namaḥ । gomukhyai namaḥ । vāmanāsāpuṭe – (l.6) ḷṃ sthāṇave dīrghajihvāyai namaḥ । dakṣiṇagaṇḍe – ḹṃ harāya namaḥ । kuṇḍodaryai namaḥ । vāmagaṇḍe – eṃ caṇḍīśāya namaḥ । (p.20b, f.17a, l.1) ūrdhvakeśinyai namaḥ । uttirā(uttaro)ṣṭhe – aiṃ bhautikāya namaḥ । vikṛtamukhyai namaḥ । adharoṣṭhe – oṃ sadyojātāya namaḥ । jvālāmukhyai namaḥ । (l.2) ūrdhvadantapaṅktau – aum anugraheśāya namaḥ । ullāsinyai namaḥ । adho dantapaṅktau – am akrūrāya namaḥ । śrīmukhyai namaḥ । cighrako? (l.3) aṃ mahāsenāya namaḥ । vidyāyai namaḥ । āsye namaḥ । kaṃ krodheśāya namaḥ । mahākālyai namaḥ । dakṣiṇabāhau – khaṃ caṇḍāya namaḥ । (l.4) sarasvatyai namaḥ । dakṣiṇakorpare – gaṃ pañcākāya namaḥ । siddhyai namaḥ । dakṣiṇamaṇibandhe – ghaṃ śivottamāya namaḥ । trailokye (l.5) namaḥ । vidyāyai namaḥ । dakṣimapāṇitale – ṅam ekarudrāya namaḥ । mantraśaktyai namaḥ । dakṣimakarāgre – cakṣurmeṣāya namaḥ । ātmaśaktyai namaḥ । (l.6) vāmabāhau – phaṇekanetrāya namaḥ । bhūtamātre namaḥ । vāmakorpare – jaṃ caturmukhāya namaḥ । lambodaryai namaḥ । (p.21a, l.1) vāmamaṇibandhe – jham ajeśāya namaḥ । bhrāmiṇyai namaḥ । vāmapāṇitale – ñaṃ candrāya namaḥ । gatakāyyai namaḥ । vāmakarāgre – ṭaṃ somāya namaḥ । khecaryai namaḥ । dakṣiṇorumūle – (l.2) ṭhaṃ lāṅgaline namaḥ । mañjaryai namaḥ । dakṣiṇajānuni – ḍaṃ dāhakāya namaḥ । rūpiṇyai namaḥ । dakṣiṇajaṅghāyām – ḍham ardhanārīśāya namaḥ । vikariṇyai namaḥ । (l.3) dakṣiṇapādatale – ṇam umākāntāya namaḥ । pā(khā)daryai namaḥ । dakṣiṇa-pādatalāgre – taṃ sthaḍiśāya namaḥ । pūtanāyai namaḥ । vāmorumūle – thaṃ ḍiṇḍu(l.4)muṇḍaye namaḥ । bhadrakālyai namaḥ । vāmajānuni – dam atraye namaḥ । gomātre namaḥ । vāmajaṅghāyām – dham īśānāya namaḥ । śaṅkhinyai namaḥ । (l.5) vāmapādatale – naṃ meṣāya namaḥ । gajinyai namaḥ । vāmapādāgre – paṃ lohitāya namaḥ । kālarātryai namaḥ । dakṣiṇapārśve – phaṃ śikhine (p.21b, f.18a, l.1) kapardinyai namaḥ । vāmapārśve – baṃ chagalaṇḍāya namaḥ । kāminyai namaḥ । pṛṣṭhavaṃśe – bhaṃ dviraṇḍāya namaḥ । vajriṇyai namaḥ । nābhau – maṃ mahākālāya namaḥ । māyāyai (l.2) namaḥ । hṛdaye – yaṃ tvagātmane namaḥ । mupāline sumukhyai namaḥ । tvaci – raktātmane huṃ jaṅgāya namaḥ । revatyai namaḥ । rudhire gocare – vāmāṃ(l.3)sātmane namaḥ । vināśine mādhavyai namaḥ । māmsagocare – vaṃ modātmane namaḥ । khaḍgīśāya namaḥ । vāruṇyai namaḥ । medasi - .... adhyātmane (l.4) bakāya namaḥ । vāmāyai namaḥ । asthiṣu – ṣaṃ majjātmane manorūpāya namaḥ । śvetādharakṣopasāriṇyai namaḥ । majje – saṃ śuklātmane vṛṣāya (l.5) sahajāyai namaḥ । śukle – haṃ prāṇātmane lakulīśāya lakṣmyai namaḥ । karṇe – laṃ liṅgātmane manorūpāya vyāpinyai namaḥ । liṅge – (l.6) kṣaṃ krodhātmane mahāmāyāyai namaḥ । iti liṅgāgre vinyasya । ma ma ṣa a ka ca kaceti mātṛkā । gaṃ sajāti ca । ma arva kavaca । i ī kṣaṃ vāmā (p.22a, l.1) krakāṅgāni ma hṛdayam । ..... śiraḥ । vaṃ śikhā । kavacam i ī । netraṃ kṣamāstram ।

jīvanyāsaṃ sva mantreṇa nyastvāṅgāni ca vinyaset ।
kramā dhṛtvā ca pūrṇānkaṃ tattvatattveśvarān (l.2) prati ॥
mūrtimūrtīśvaropetaṃ pratyekaṃ cāhutitrayam ।
viśodhya sthāpayetpīṭhe ratnādinyāsasaṃyute ॥
kṛtvāghorāṇunā homaṃ nicchidrārṇaṃ śataṃ *tathā ।* (l.3)
svanāmādyo haṃsayukto dvitīye vāpi gṛhyatām ॥
saptamasya dvitīyaṃ tu prathamasya caturdaśa ।
bindunādasamāyuktaṃ mānuṣaṃ bījamucyate ॥ (l.4)
hrasvabrahmasamākhyātaṃ dīrghānyaṅgāni kalpayet ।
rākṣasāṅgaśca piśācāṃśca surān siddhāṃstathaiva ca ॥
devānāṃ vāhanānyevaṃ (l.5) sthāpayeddeśikottamaḥ ।
kā yekasuvinayasattakrapañcaviṃsatitatvakān ॥
pratimā mūrdhvadeśe tu nidrākumbhaṃ ca vinyaset । (l.6)
iḍā ca piṅgalā caiva suṣumnā ca tathaiva ca ॥
gāndhāri hastijihvā ca pūṣā caiva yaśasvini ।
alaṃ pūṣā .....caiva *śaṅkhini daśami smṛtā ॥* (p.22b, f.19a, l.1)
etāśca nāḍayaścaiva tadrūpe vinyasettataḥ ।
prāṇāpānasamānaśca udānavyānaeva ca ॥
nāgaḥ kūrmaścakrakaro devadatto *dhanañjayaḥ ।* (l.2)
jānu karṇāpadhau tattvatrayaṃ bhūtattvagocaram ॥
ātmāntarātmā paramātmana satatrādhipāṃ nyaset ।
jīvanyāsaśca pūrvokta.......*kārayedguruḥ ॥* (l.3)
viśodhya sthāpayetpīṭhe ratnādinyāsasaṃyute ।

atha saṃprokṣaṇavidhiḥ –
vatsaraṃ vatsara.......niṣṭhita*mapūjitam ।* (l.4)
śunā kākādibhirliṅgaṃ prādho yat syā(syāt) niṣevitam ॥
tatpūjyamānaṃ loke'smiṃ mahādoṣasahasrakṛt ।
atastasya sti(sthi)tasyaivaṃ *saṃskāro'dya nigadyate ॥* (l.5)
pūrvoktavidhinā sarvaṃ kārayenmaṇṭapādikam ।
saṃsnāpya pūrvavacchastramanunā mūrtikādibhiḥ ॥
tritattvaṃ *vātha mūrtirpā(pā)ditattvatrayapurassaram ।* (l.6)
pūrvavannyasya talliṅgaṃ samārabhya prapūjayet ॥
pūrvavat pratibhāgaṃ ca hutvā śāntighaṭāmbhasā । (p.23a, l.1)
abhyukṣya homasaṅkhyābhirjaptvātha viniveśayet ॥
pūrṇānte pūrvavat buddhya śilāratnādapañcakam ।
pūrvoktamāsanaṃ nyasya tasya copari pūrvavat ॥ (l.2)
mūrtimantrānvitaṃ liṅgaṃ pīṭhamantreṇa pīṭhikam ।
buddhyā samāropya śivaṃ tattadaṅgāni vinyaset ॥
datvārghyaṃ pūrvavat pūjāṃ homaṃ kṛtvā tu deśikaḥ । (l.3)
kuryāccāturthikaṃ ka(kra)masva mantrasya visarjanam ॥
iti saṃprokṣaṇaṃ proktaṃ ṣaṭtrimāsādyapūjite ।
liṅge śuddhikaraṃ mūlapratimā bhaladāyakam ॥
evaṃ pratiṣṭhā *liṅgādiparavārāmarātimā ।* (l.4)
sakalānyānapekṣā ca kṛtā śiṣyavibodhinī ॥
dīkṣāpratiṣṭhayoraṅgaṃ lakṣaṇaṃ nottamaṃ ..... ।
kriyākrama*vibodhasya parimoṣo(kṣo) bhavediti ॥* (l.5)

śrīmannetraḥ śivāryatārakajitā tat pūrve(vra)jenāmalaṃ
	jyotiḥ śāstravidāṃ varo varavapu*nyāyādiśāstrāmbudhiḥ ।* (l.6)
vānyajñānavidhāradaḥ śivamatavyākhyāgragaṇyo kṛtaṃ
	jñānaṃ svātmagurūttameṣu ca tathā dīkṣāpratiṣṭhāvidhiḥ ॥ (p.23b, f.20a, l.1)

atha guruvaṃśakramavidhiḥ –
gokraṃ caturvidhaṃ jñeyaṃ gocarāḥ ṣoḍaśa smṛtaḥ ।
catvāro gotramunaya(yaḥ) sthānaṃ caiva catuṣṭayam ॥
ṣaṭtriṃśat bhrātarastoma *munayaḥ parikīrtitāḥ ।* (l.2)
maṭhāḥ pañcadaśa proktā vṛkṣāścatvāra īritāḥ ॥
gāyatryākhyā tu sā vikribhārati ca manonmani ।
ityākhyātaḥ śivasyocuḥ śakti*gotrāṇi kecanā(na) ॥* (l.3)
gāyatryāderadhiṣṭhe yā śivādyā gocarā iti ।
yathāvaditi manyante siddhāntajñā gurūttamāḥ ॥
gocaraṃ kulamityuktam *iti śrīkāraṇaśrateḥ ।* (l.4)
gotrānu ya kālādīnāṃ pūryāya tvā(tvāṃ) prasiddhataḥ ॥
gotraṃ śivaśikhājyotisāvitryākhyāntasthitam । (l.5)
bindunādasthūlabindunādādhiṣṭātra kalpanam ॥
śivācchaktestano bindunādādapi caturvidham ।
kulajyotirmayasthānaṃ gurūṇāṃ pañcadhā *kvacit ॥* (l.6)
śālmale śākasaṃjñe ca puṣkare kuśasaṃjñate ।
kramā(mt)sthitacatuskaṃ(ṣkaṃ) tu dvipeṣvanyeṣu kṛsnaśa ॥
ekaikasya caturvarti *bhedā ṣoḍaśagocaraḥ ।* (l.7)
prājāpatyamahīpālā kāpotā grandhikāḥ śive ॥
kuṭikāścaiva vetālā padmā haṃsāḥ śikhākule । (p.24a, l.1)
dhṛtarāṣṭrabakāḥ kaṅkā gopālākhyā jyotiṣā skatāḥ ॥
kaṭikā māṭharāścaiva nāḍikā daṇḍino pare ।
bhāradvājaḥ śivakule kuśikaśca śikhāhvaye ॥ (l.2)
kāśyapo jyotiṣi jñeyāḥ sāvitre gautamo muniḥ ।
bhāradvājasya sacchakraṃ kuśikasya va pauṇḍaram ॥
nāgaraṃ kāśyapasyoktaṃ gautamasya nagodbhavam । (l.3)
ākhyātā gotrapataya ucyante bhrātarastathā ॥
śaṅkho mukaṇḍusūnaśca pauṇḍaraḥ śubhrasaṃjñakaḥ ।
śukākhyaḥ śaunakaścaiva manurvai *kṛṣṇapiṅgalaḥ ॥* (l.4)
durvāsaśca navocyante bharadvājānujā smṛtāḥ ।
namuci dakṣasaṃjñaśca śurvalmikanandanau ॥
parāśarastathā vyāso *bodhāyanasanātanau ।* (l.5)
bhrātarakuśakasyaite navākhyo'ta śikhākule ॥
aṅgirā sthūlavasaṃjñaśca śāṇḍilyo'gastyasaṃjñakaḥ । (l.6)
āpastambośanā bhūtiśaṅkukarṇavirājakāḥ ॥
bhrātaraḥ kāśyapasyaite na vā khātāstritīyake ।
bāḍabogālla*saṃjñaśca vasiṣṭho māṭharottrikaḥ ॥* (p.24b, f.21a, l.1)
mauga(dga)lyo ṛṣyaśṛṅgaśca kātyāyana uṣaspatiḥ ।
sāvitrīgocare jātā bhrātaro gautamasya ca ॥
kailāsākhye *maṭhaḥ proktaḥ pauṇḍaraśca dvitīyakaḥ ।* (l.2)
valāhamustriti(tī)ya(yaḥ) syātkalpagrāmaścaturthakaḥ ॥
pañcami badari jñeyā devadāruvanaṃ tataḥ ।
kandarpaḥ saptamaḥ śakhā uttarārabhyakaṃ tataḥ ॥ (l.3)
de(da)kṣiṇāraṇyakaṃ tasmāt govindāṇumataḥ param ।
āmado mattamāyūro raṇabhadraṃ ca golakī ॥
kadambarodumbarāśvatthavaṭāmaṭhavibhūṣaṇāḥ ।
sāmānyameta*tsarveṣāmasmatsantatirucyate ॥* (l.4)
akṣubdhakuṇḍalīśakteradhiṣṭhātuḥ śivāt parāt ।
āvirbhūtaṃ śivakulaṃ gāyatrīśaktyadhiṣṭhitam ॥
tasamānmantramaheśānā *gaṇadevāditaḥ kramāt ।* (l.5)
nāḍicakrātmavijñānaṃ vijayogādilakṣaṇam ॥
avatīrṇaṃ śivakulaṃ drāvāsasimahāmunau ।
tenaivānugrahitā nā......ṣyādinā*manugraham ॥* (l.6)

śrīmadānde śivajñānaṃ vibodhakaṃ tasmāt paiṅgalanāmāṃ trigurusantatitaḥ kramāt । paitāmahākhye manuje saṃkrāntā mūrtistataḥ (l.7) catvāvo gocarā jātā । prājāpatyādisaṃsthitaḥ । ahiṃsāguruśiśriṣā । śaucasvādhyāyasamyamaiḥ samyāsteyaguṇai(l.8)rlakṣyāt । prajāpatyāṃ tu gocarāt ।

gurusantatirutpannā viśiṣṭaśivamantragā ।
tasyāṃ śrīvyāpakaśivo *laṭaperyo gurūttamaḥ ॥* (p.25a, l.1)
āmadākhyamaṭheśānaḥ sa pūrvāso yathā bhavet ।
tacchiṣyo gauḍajaśrīmānāsat sarveśapaṇḍitaḥ ॥
pattane nilalā*ṭā....se nivāsamakarodguruḥ ।* (l.2)
tato hṛllāṭauttuṅgaṃ śivo vidhye'rpatīśvaraḥ ॥
kalyāṇanagarī vāsī guruḥ vardhatikṛtsudhīḥ ।
sarvavidyādhipo *yasya kanīyānasya deśikaḥ ॥* (l.3)
sarvāgamārthanirṇetu(tuḥ) śrī bhojanṛpate garuḥ ।
tatassomaśivastajja(rja)staddeśi yo vratīśvaraḥ ॥
tataḥ pūrṇaśivā*cāryo vārāṇasyāṃ nṛpācitaḥ ।* (l.4)
gṛhasthaścobhudeśīya āryottuṅgaśivastataḥ ॥
vidyeśānaśivastasmādāryāvetau pra(vra?)tīśvarau । (l.5)
coladeśodbhavastasmādvidyāntaśivasaṃjñataḥ ॥
vārāṇasinapāścāryo grahi vidyāṃ na pāragaḥ ।
tato brahmaśivācāryastapasviṭalāṭa?*puṃgavaḥ ॥* (l.6)
tato mūrtiśivastādṛk vijayākhyapure sthitaḥ ।
māyāpuryāṃ gṛhī tajjastitaḥ sarvātmadeśikaḥ ॥
cacchiṣyo gauḍavṛṣabho *śrīkamaṭhaśivadeśikaḥ ।* (p.25b, f.22a, l.1)
śrīmadabhrasabheśānādidīkṣārtī nāgatastu yaḥ ॥
śrīvikramādicoleśā svagurutve nyaveśayat ।
tato dhyānaśivaśrīmānabhavadgauḍa*puṃgavaḥ ॥* (l.2)
aṣṭāviṃśatitantrāṇāṃ vyākhyāna(nā)ni praṇo guruḥ ।
coleśvarācito bhūtvā yaḥ sabheśamapūjayet ॥

	tasmāt babhūva (l.3) sakalāgamapāradravā śuddhāśayo hṛdayaśaṅkaranāmadheyaḥ । asmāt pitāmaha pitṛvyasutāgra eṣa śaivādhirājā iti (l.4) suprathīto dharaṇyāṃ mātulo'smat pitustasya kaniṣṭhaḥ parameśvaraḥ śrīmadhyānaśivenātha anugṛhya guruḥ kṛtaḥ ।

taṃ vade śivata.....*cakṣuṣyahaṃ vedādividyotkaṭaṃ* (l.5)
	pratyekṣaikaśivākaṃ śaktimuditaṃ sākṣādanugrāhakam ।
atreranvayaratnamīḍyamamalaṃ kāñcī*nivāsaśriyaṃ* (l.6)
	svārjhaspṛk parameśvarābhidhamamumamadbandhavicchedakam ॥

tadākhyayā kṣitau patyā taṃ so'nujagrāha māṃ sudhīḥ ।
aghoraśivanāmānaṃ saṃjñāya samanvitam ॥
āgneyatantraṃ bahudhā saṃkṣipya śarajanmane ।
śrīkaṇṭhanātho bhagavānavocat । (l.2) karuṇānidhiḥ teṣu kālottarākhye tu ṣusaṃgṛheṣu śatatrayam ।
sārdhaṃ tu vipratiryenaya trayodaśabhiḥ śataiḥ ।
atrānuktaṃ tu yatkiñcit *sūcitaṃ na prakāśitam ।* (l.3)
vistīrṇasaṃgrahe sarvaṃ suhitāyāmapekṣya ca ॥
anuṣṭi(ṣṭhi)taṃ guruśreṣṭhairasmadīyaiḥ purātanaiḥ । (l.4)
uttuṅgaśivapaddhatyāṃ nityaṃ naimittikaṃ punaḥ ॥
siddhāntasārapaddhatyāṃ sopadeśamanuṣṭhitam ।
sarvātmaśivapādebhya asma*dguruvāntikāt ॥* (l.5)
etannityādikaṃ karma sopavartikamādarāt ।
śra(śru)tvā kriyākramasyaiva dyotikāracitā mayā ॥
nāda........*yeśakasyābde vartamāne kalau yuge ।* (p.26b, f.23a, l.1)
likhitā granthasāhasraistibhiḥ pañcaśatairapi ॥
॥ iti parameśvaraparanāmadheya śrīmadaghoraśivācāryaviracitāyāṃ kriyākramadyotikāyāṃ dvārādipratiṣṭhāvidhiḥ samāptaḥ ॥

atha mahotsavavidhiḥ – hariḥ om । (l.3)
prathamaṃ vṛṣayāgaṃ ca dhvajārohadvitīyakam ।
bṛhanālatṛtīyaṃ ca caturthaṃ cāṅkurārpaṇam ॥
yāgaśālārcanaṃ caivāstra.........*taḥ param ।* (l.4)
saptamaṃ balidānaṃ ca yānakramamathāṣṭamam ॥
navamaṃ pariveṣaṃ ca daśa nīrājanakramam ।
ekādaśaṃ kautukaṃ ca dvādaśaṃ tīrthasaṃgraham ॥ (l.5)
svarṇotsavaṃ trayodaśyāṃ caturdaśa ca tīrthakam ।
avarohaṃ pañcadaśā ṣoḍaśaṃ snapanaṃ bhavet ॥
vaivāhika(kaṃ) *saptadaśa maktotsavamataḥparam ।* (p.27a, l.1)

	ityādibhedenotsavaṃ jñātvā śaivādidaivikāntabhedottamottamanavāhotsavavidhirvidhīyyate । tadyathā – (l.2)
	tatpūrvarātrāvadhivāsanamārabhet । tatra ācāryādayaḥ – ārohaṇādyavarohaṇaparyantamekabhuktādhaśśayana-tailābhyañjanastrīsampatparānna(l.3)bhojanādirāhityā striṣapaṇasnāthinaḥ vapanaṃ kṛtvā । hataṃ vāsaḥ paridhāya saṅkalpya pūrvamiṣṭadevatāṃ namaskṛtyevamācaret । prathamadivasa(l.4)mārabhya navāhāntadvivārabaliḥ kāryaḥ । daśāhe prātarbaliṃ datvā taddine cūrṇotsavaṃ tīrthaṃ cārabhet । navāhotva......(l.5)rohaṇapūrvameva kāryam । mārgaśīrṣake ādrā(rdrā)ntam । puṣye puṣyāntaṃ māghe māghāntaṃ phālgune uttaraphalgunyāntaṃ caitre cittā(trā)ntaṃ .....(p.27b, f.24a, l.1)śā(vikhā)khe viśākhāntaṃ jyeṣṭhe jyeṣṭhāntaṃ mūlāntaṃ vā āṣāḍhe uttarāṣāḍhāntam । pūrvāṣāḍāntaṃ vā śrāvaṇe śravaṇāntaṃ bhādrapade pūrvābhādrānta(l.2)māśvije aśvinyāntam । kārtike kṛttikāntamevaṃ māsaṛkṣāṇi । samudratīre parvaṇi । nadyādau parvayukte vā । māghe ṣaṣṭhyanta(l.3)jyeṣṭhakṛṣṇāṣṭamyantaṃ vā । ācāryabhūpayajamānadevigrāmādinakṣatreṣu vā karturabhīṣṭanakṣatre vā tīrthaṃ kārayet । samudre (l.4) pūrvāhṇe vā puṣkarādiṣvaparāhṇai(hṇe) nadyāṃ madhyadine tīrthaṃ kārayet । nakṣatrādhikye sāyāhne tīrthaṃ kārayet । (l.5) ṛkṣadvaye tithidvayai(ye) vā ekasminmāse ekasminmāse saṃprāpte pare viṣvayanasaṃkrāntyādiyogayuktaṃ cet । pūrvaṃ grāhyam । ārohaṇāditīrthānta(l.6)mekamāse śreṣṭham । viṣvantamayanāntaṃ grahaṇāntaṃ śivatīrthamācaret । śaivādhikyaśivossa(tsa)ve prātaḥkāle avabhṛtam । niśi prātadhvaja (p.28a, vināyakotsavam, l.1) iti niśi sāyaṃ divā prātaḥ dhvajārohaṇaṃ kārayet ।

dhvaje dhvajaṃ na kartavyā kartavyaṃ vṛṣabhadhvajam ।
anyeṣāṃ tu dhvajārabdhe mahādoṣaḥ prajāyate ॥ (l.2)
ekavāstuni grāmādau vā devotsavamadhenuḥ ।

	kalyāṇaṃ na kārayet । kṛtaṃ codrājño rāṣṭrasya ca doṣo bhavati । utsavārambhapūrve vāhanādirathāntaṃ (l.3) navīkṛtya devadhāmādivīthyantam । kadalīstambhapūgastamba(bha)kanālikeralikucādi tatkālocitaphalapallavapuṣpamālādarpaṇadvāratoraṇa(l.4)vitānadhvajādibhi(bhiḥ) prapādiviśrāmamaṇṭapāntamalaṅkṛtya । sammārjanajalasecanādi kārayitvā bhūśuddhiṃ kārayet । bhadrakālyutsavānte (l.5)

(vināyakotsavam -)
vighneśvarasya navāha-saptāha-triyāha-mekāhaṃ vā utsavaṃ kārayet । tadyathā – vināyakaskandavīraśāstapañcaviṃśatiberaśivāstra(l.6)bhastacaṇḍāvigrahādiśca viśeṣataḥ । pañcagavyapurassaraṃ saṃpūjya । sarvālaṅkārairalaṅkṛtyācāryaṛtvikbhūpajayamānaḥ brāhma........(p.28b, f.25, l.1)rtikaiḥ saha śubhalagne utsavārthaṃ vighneśapūjāpurassaraṃ mahādevaśabdaviśiṣṭaṃ puṇyāhaṃ vācayitvā । snapanapurassaraṃ vighneśaṃ saṃpūjya । ......... (l.2) bhakṣyabhojyādinaivedyaṃ datvā । brāhmaṇān bhojayitvā । avighnamastviti prārthya । devālaye karmaratān sarvāndīnāndhakṛpaṇanāthān bhojanā(l.3)cchādanaiḥ santoṣyāstrarājaṃ caṇḍeśvaraṃ ca vināyakapūrvaṃ rathe vā śibikādau vāropya chatracāmarādinānādhvāsahitaṃ (l.4) gandhapuṣpadhūpadīpanānāvādyavedaghoṣasahitaṃ puraṃ pradakṣiṇīkṛtvā snānamaṇṭape niveśayet । tataḥ śi.........brahmaṇa(l.5)vaddārusaṃgrahaṇaṃ kuryāt । bilvakhādiradevadārucampakārjunāśokaśirīṣaveṇurityādivṛkṣāṇāmekamahamagraṃ mūlaṃ parīkṣya । śivaṃ (l.6) saṃpūjya । baliṃ vidhāya । nirudhya । paraśunā cindāt(chindyāt) । pūrve cottare caiśānyāṃ patitaṃ cecchobhanam anyathā tu santyajet । tadaśrīkṛtyā(p.29a, l.1)dāyā(ya) śilpināmadhāre cāropyānīya । gavyena snāpya kautukamagre badhvā । punaḥ puṇyāhaṃ vācayitvāsthānamaṇṭape nidhāya । tailādinā vimṛjya (l.2) sarveṣāṃ tāmbūladakṣiṇāṃ ca dāpayet । tatra sthaṇḍilaṃ vidhāya । madhye daṇḍaṃ vinyasya । vastrādyairalaṅkṛtya । mālādibhirvibhūṣya । dhūpayitvā । vedāśra(l.3)pasvaśravṛttabhāgeṣu śivabhāgāditaḥ tatvatrayaṃ seśvaraṃ saṃpūjya । praṇavenotthāpya । tatsthāne niveśya sāmānyapratiṣṭho-ktavat pratiṣṭhāpaye(l.4)diti spū(sthū)pyantaṃ cūlikāntam । śukanāntaṃ vā yaṣṭitrayaphalakātraya-mupadaṇḍadvayaghaṇṭāsahitamaṣṭādhika-śatadarbhakūrca-darbha......ṣṭi(l.5)sahitaṃ valayasahitaṃ kārayediti dārusaṃgrahaṇadaṇḍapratiṣṭhāvidhiḥ ।

(mṛtsaṃgrahaṇavidhiḥ -)
nadītīrādisthale vighneśvarādiberasahitaṃ nānāvādyavedaghoṣa(l.6)sahitaṃ gatvā pūrvoktavanmṛtsaṃgrahaṇaṃ kṛtvā । taṃ mṛdaṃ paricārakairmāheśvarairudragaṇikādibhirgajādyairvāhayitvā puraṃ pradakṣiṇīkṛtya । (p.29b, f.26a, l.1) prāsādaṃ praviśyāṅkurārpaṇavedikāyāṃ nikṣipya । vighneśaṃ modakairapūpairikṣudaṇḍanālikerakapitthamātulaṅgakadalyādī........(l.2)locitaphalaiśchaṇakamudgatāmbūlādi nivedya । sarvopacāraiḥ pūjayediti mṛtsaṃgrahaṇavidhiḥ । dhvajārohaṇapūrvedyuḥ rātrā........(l.3)vāṅkuraṃ tri(tṛ)tīyamiti pūrvoktavadaṅkuraṃ kuryādityaṅkurārpaṇavidhiḥ samāptaḥ ॥

atha vṛṣabhayāgavidhiḥ –
dhvajārohaṇapūrvedyuḥ rātrā..........(l.4)vāsanakrayāṃ kārayet । tataḥ śivaṃ saṃpūjya । vijñāpya । vitānādyairvṛṣasthānamalaṅkṛtya gururuttarābhimukho bhūtvā । sthirāsanaḥ (l.5) pūrvoktavadaṅga-nyāsādikaṃ kṛtvārghyaṃ ...sādhya vṛṣeśaṃ pūjya mudgānnaṃ nivedya । tasyāgre śālyādibhi(bhiḥ) sthaṇḍilaṃ vidhāya taṃ madhye padmamālikhya (l.6) kuśaiḥ puṣpaiḥ paristīrya saṃsthāpya madhyakumbhe vṛṣabhaṃ ṣaḍutthāsanapūrvaṃ saṃpūjya । pūrvādīśāntadikṣu – ukṣāya gopataye namaḥ । (p.30a, l.1) śṛṅgiṇe – nandine namaḥ । vṛṣapataye namaḥ । vṛṣāṇine śaṅkukarṇāya mahodarāya namaḥ । ityāsanādimūlamantrairabhyarcya । yadvā – lokapālān saṃpūjya । dhvajapaṭa(l.2)ma.....pannavahastādikaṃ pañcabhāgaikavistāraṃ vistārasamaṃ puccaṃ(cchaṃ) puccā(cchā)rdhaṃ tadardhaṃ vā śiraḥ । sitaṃ sūkṣmaṃ ślakṣaṇaṃ(kṣṇaṃ) kāṣāyādisambhavaṃ tattribhāgaṃ kṛtvā (l.3) madhyabhāge vṛṣabhaṃ tribhāgaikabhāge vā samālikhya śvetena raktena śūrpakaṇṭhakhurāṇi pītena pucchamakṣikaṇiśvetakṛṣṇena kārayitvā । tat purastāt (l.4) pūrṇakumbhaṃ paścāddīpaśrīvatsādikaśeṣamaṅgalarūpāṇi vilikhya dhvajayāgamārabhe । athavā daṇḍadīrghaṃ paṭānāha vistāraṃ (l.5) ca pūrvavadvinyasya । puṇyāhaṃ vācayitvā pañcagavyādibhiḥ saṃprokṣyā(kṣya) vṛṣe calāsanaṃ saṃpūjya । tasyāgre sthaṇḍile'gni- (l.6)....ryoktamārgeṇa pūrṇāntaṃ pūrvavadvidhāya beraśuddhyānantaramanyatra sthaṇḍiladvayaṃ saṃpādya dhvajapaṭaṃ madhye nyastvā hemakulīkāyā (p.30b, f.27a, l.1) sūryasomamantrābhyāmakṣimocanaṃ vṛṣagāyatryā vā vidhāya ।
tīkṣṇaśṛṅgāya vidmahe vedapādāya dhīmahī । tanno vṛṣabhaḥ pracodayāt । iti kṛtvā (l.2) godarśanādisarvaṃ vidhāya । mṛdbhiḥ pañcagavyena śivāmbhasā ca prokṣya । darpaṇe gavyādibhi(bhiḥ) snāpya । jale'dhivāsayet । jalāduttārya (l.3) yāgaśālāyā dhvajapaṭamānīya । sthaṇḍile saṃsthāpya । ṣaḍutthāsanaṃ saṃpūjya । mūrtidhyānapurassaraṃ saṃkalpya । vāsasācchādya । (l.4) brahmaṇi vinyasya । nidrākumbhaṃ saṃsthāpya । ukṣādīn parito vinyasya । pūjayet । vṛṣamūle sthitvā mūrdhādipādaparyantaṃ catustriṃśa-tta....ni (l.5) nyaset । tadyathā – sadāśivatattvāya namaḥ । sadāśivatattvādhipataye brahmā(hma)ṇe namaḥ । ityārabhya pṛthivītattvādhipāya śrīkaṇṭhāya namaḥ । (l.6) ityantaṃ saṃpūjya । trikhaṇḍaṃ parikalpya । puṣpamālāpratisaraṃ datvā । pūrvavat bhāgatrayaṃ saṃkalpya । pratibhāgaṃ pūrvādikrameṇa kṣmāvanhi(hni) (p.31a, l.1) …mmānāt jalyātendukhāni ca । iti mūrtayaḥ । ukṣaṃ gopatiśṛṅginandivṛkṣa(ṣa)parirviṣāṇi śaṅkukarṇamahodarā mūrtipān vinyasya । jīvanyāsāntaṃ mūlena kṛtvā । (l.2) sthāpanādyupacāraiḥ saṃpūjya । bhasmadarbhatilaiḥ prākāratrayaṃ bahirvidadhyāt । lokapālanadhyeṣya kuṇḍe ādhāraśaktaye namaḥ । ityagnau saṃpūjya । (l.3) āhutitrayaṃ datvātmatattvavidyātattvaśivatattvabrahmaviṣṇuśivebhyo namaḥ । iti saṃpūjya । pratyekamāhutitrayaṃ datvā । kṣmādimūrtistadhipebhyo namaḥ । saṃpūjya । (l.4) pratyekamāhutitrayaṃ datvā । brahmāṅgāni ca pratyekaṃ śatamardhaṃ vā hutvā । pūrṇāṃ datvā । guruḥ śāntikumbhahastaḥ vṛṣabhasamīpaṃ gatvā । śāntikumbhajalenā(na) (l.5) prokṣya । tanmūlena kuśamūla-madhyāgrairbhāgatrayaṃ saṃkalpya । saṃspṛśya । japtvā । varmaṇāvakuṇṭhyā(ṇṭhya) । nirudhya । tattvādhipānājñāṃ śrāvaṃ ca (l.6) jñānaśaktiṃ vinyasya । nirudhya । homamai(homai)rvedādighoṣairvā nṛttagītamahotsavaiḥ bhaktito ekarātro vā kuryā(ryāt) । vṛṣayāga(l.7)śivāsanam । digbaliṃ ca nivedya guruḥ । pūrvavat svapediti vṛṣādhivāsanavidhiḥ samāptaḥ ॥

	ayane'dya snādikarmaṃ(karma) kṛtvā । saṃkalpya । sakalīkṛtyā(tya) । (p.31b, f.28a, l.1) sāmānyārghyaṃ saṃsādhya । nityavadātmapūjāvidhiḥ sarvaṃ vidhāya । paṭaṃ kumbhādīnyathākrameṇābhyarcya । vahnāvapi samidājyacarubhiraṣṭottaraśataṃ hutvā । pūrṇāṃ datvā । nirudhya । maṇṭapādiṣu (l.2) maṇḍalatrayaṃ kṛtvā । paricāramāhūyāstreṇa saṃprokṣyā(kṣya) soṣṇīṣamuttarīyaṃ ca dhārayitvā । paṭaṃ kumbhādisahita-muddhṛtyāstrādicaṇḍeśāntaṃ sarvātodyasamāyuktaṃ (l.3) śikhadundubhinirghoṣairvedādighoṣaiḥ saha grāmapradakṣiṇaṃ kṛtvālayaṃ praviśya । paṭamekasminkumbhamekasmin sthaṇḍilatraye nidhāya । puṇyāhaṃ (l.4) vācayitvā । pañcagavyārghyodakena saha prokṣya । vṛṣabhaṃ kumbhajalena prokṣya । ukṣādighaṭairapi gandhādyairalaṅkṛtya । haviṃ caṇakādi nivedyāstrarājā(l.5)mapi tatkumbhajalenābhiṣicyālaṅkṛtya nivedya japaṃ samarpyāstrarājasya rajanīcūrṇaṃ datvā । svayamapi dhṛtvā rājñāṃ bhaktānāṃ paricārakāṇāṃ sarveṣāṃ ca rajanīcūrṇaṃ (l.6) dāpayet । iti śubham ॥

(dhvajārohaṇavidhiḥ -)
tato dhvajadaṇḍamalaṅkṛtya gāyatryā daṇḍāyāmabhyarcayet । devaṃ rathādau samāropya śūlamagre agrata ācāryaḥ । pṛṣṭhato yaja(l.7)mānaḥ bhaktādisarvajanaiḥ sa(sā)rdhaṃ sarvātodyasamāyuktaṃ maṅgalāṅkurasahitaṃ nānāvidhacchatracāmaradīpasaṃyuktaṃ yātrādānasahitaṃ diśādidikṣu (p.32a, l.1) gītanṛttabalidānasahitaṃ puraṃ pradakṣiṇīkṛtyālayaṃ praviśya । dhvajasthāne samānīya puṇyāhaṃ vācayitvā । savedikamabhyukṣya śaktyādiśaktiparyantaṃ vedikāyāmabhyarcya । (l.2) ekaṃ ca pādamānaṃ tu triṇetraṃ romāvalirājitadantarajjuṃ śvetāṅgarūpaṃ śukanāsikāntamārūḍhapadmāsanadaṇḍarūpamiti dhyātvā । daṇḍe sadāśiva-(l.3)māvāhya । yaṣṭiphalakāsu śaktitrayamupadaṇḍe sūrayasomau rajjau takṣādidaivatyaṃ paṭe vāyudhvaje vṛṣabhaṃ krameṇābhyarcya । (l.4) mudgānnaṃ pāyasānnaṃ vā vṛṣāya nivedya । dhūpadīpau datvā । brāhmaṇānā(nāṃ) sauvarṇadhānyaphalatāmbūlādi datvā । paṭarajjuṃna(jjunā) saṃveṣṭya । (l.5) kariṣyamāṇamutsavaṃ kra(kri)yatā(tām) iti śivaṃ vijñāpya । bṛhattālapaṭhanaṃ kṛtvā । vighnanirasanārthamasaṅkhyaṃ nālikerabaliṃ datvā । (l.6) sumuhūrte nānāvādyasahitaṃ deśiko daṇḍamūle sthitvāropitaparicārakamarghyeṇa prokṣya । svāyambhuvamādau spṛṣṭvā mūrtipaiḥ saha (p.32b, f.29a, l.1) maghramāropayet । krameṇa veṣṭanaṃ kṛtvā daṇḍamūle bandhayet । ataḥprabhṛti tīrthāntaṃ vṛṣabhaṃ trikālaṃ ca dvikālaṃ cā(ca) pūjayet । paścāt (l.2) kuṇḍāntikaṃ gatvā । prāyaścittaṃ hutvā । vṛṣayāgāgniṃ visṛjayet । digvidikṣu śuddhanṛttaṃ kāryamiti dhvajārohaṇavidhiḥ ॥

	tatpiṇḍaḥ prāśayennāri । (l.3) aputri putrimāpnuyāt । vyādhighnaṃ sarvadoṣaghnaṃ vṛṣocciṣṭaṃ śikhi dhvayitre tat kriyate rāṣṭre dhvajayiṣṭiniveśanam ।
na kālamṛtyu*statrāstinā lakṣmi pāpakṛtsvapi ।* (l.4)
nopasaṃparbhayaṃ tatra no pāro go na vibhrama ॥
viparītaśca matayo yogino(nā)mapi bhūyasā । (l.5)
svakālavaṣi(rṣi) parjanyaḥ subhikṣaṃ ...mu vijayī nṛpāḥ ॥
śāntāni sarvabhūtāni payahvanyaḥ payobhṛtaḥ ।
kṛtaghno brahmabhāgagoghno dṛṣṭvā dhvajaniveśanam ॥ (l.6)
prāpnoti pāpanirmokṣaṃ kimu kartuḥ kalatrayam ।
kṛte mahādhvajārohe tatkoṭiguṇitaṃ phalam ॥

atha bṛhattālavidhiḥ – (p.33a, l.1)
uktamutsavapaddhatyāṃ sarvadevapriyārthakam ।
sāyarakṣāvasāne ca bherīṃ santāḍyate yadā ॥
devāgre tu dhvejāgre vā devatāhvānakra(kri)yām ।

	samārabhettatra sthaṇḍila(l.2)trayaṃ kṛtvā । ekasmin śūlaṃ tatpurato paṭaṃ tadagre bherīṃ vinyasya । dakṣiṇavyānadaṇḍaṃ vinyaset । tatastriśūlamūle tattvabhuvanavarṇapadamantrakalā vinyaset । tadyathā – (l.3) madhyapatre śivatattvāya namaḥ । śivatattvādhipāya rudrāya namaḥ । vāmapatre – vidyātattvāya namaḥ । vidyātattvādhipāya viṣṇave namaḥ । dakṣiṇapatre – ātmatattvāya (l.4) namaḥ । ātmatattvādhipataye brahmaṇe namaḥ । palakāyām – pārvatyai namaḥ । kumbhadeśe – senānyai namaḥ । tadāse(dāse) – vighnarājāya namaḥ । daṇḍāgre – (l.5) madanāya namaḥ । daṇḍamadhye – bhāskarāya namaḥ । daṇḍamūle – caṇḍeśvarāya namaḥ । āgneyādisaptadaleṣu – brāhmyai namaḥ । māheśvaryai namaḥ । (l.6) kaumāryai namaḥ । vaiṣṇavyai namaḥ । vārāhyai namaḥ । māhendryai namaḥ । cāmuṇḍāyai namaḥ । paścimadalamadhye – jyeṣṭhāyai namaḥ । (p.33b, f.30a, l.1) kuberadalamadhye – kātyāyinyai namaḥ । śūlamūle – ekādaśarudrādvādaśādityā avasavaḥ । aśvinīdevatāḥ aṣṭādaśagaṇāścāvāhyābhyarcya (l.2) tānāhūyāśiṣāścoktvā bherīṃ santāḍya naṃ? kārayet । tato bherīṃ vicitravastreṇāveṣṭya । madhye rudraṃ saptakīleṣu mātraśca kīle nava(l.3)grahāṃścarmeṣu vāsukīm । prahare – ṣaṇmukhaḥ । krameṇābhyarcya । gandhādibhiralaṅkṛtya । bherītāḍanaṃ kuryāt । ācāryaḥ śāstraṃ bindurūpaṃ (l.4) dhyātvā । darbheṇa trivāraṃ tāḍayet । taṃ nādaprāṇināṃ śravaṇapathaṃ cedapetakilvikṣā bhavanti । pañcādyakamāṃ prayayajña (l.5) sūtramuttarīyaṃ ca gandhamālyādidhārayitvā taddhaste puṣpaṃ datvā । so'pyācāryānujñayā bheryāṃ puṣpaṃ nikṣipya । vādyataṃ skandhe samāropya । (l.6) nanditālena tāḍayet । tataḥ śivādisarvadevāścāṣṭādaśagaṇāṃśca indrādidikpatiṃśca samudrāt parvatāt dvi(dvī)pāśca pātālāṃśca (p.43a, l.1) sakalānāhūya tāḍayet । vṛṣabhasthānamāgatya būjā bali datvā । nṛttaṃ puṣpāñjaliṃ rāgaṃ śuddhavarāṭi । gītādibhiḥ santoṣya । paścānmadhyādidevatā (l.2) snānārthamastrarājasomāskandadevīvināyakaskandanandicaṇḍabhaktavigrahādisahitaṃ sarvamaṅgalaiḥ saha brahmasthānamāśritya tanmaṇḍalasthāne puṇyāhaprokṣaṇādi(l.3)sarvaṃ vidhāyāsanamūrtimabhyarcya haste lājapuṣpāṇi gṛhītvā । musulamudrayā brahmāṇaṃ dhyātvāvāhyābhyarcya gandhapuṣpadhūpasahitaṃ naivedya ....(l.4)baliṃ datvānantaraṃ brahmatālaṃ megharāgaṃ daṇḍapādamiti nṛttagītavādyādibhiḥ santoṣyānantaramindradiśimāśritya tanmaṇḍale pūrvavadāsanādi(l.5)baliṃ datvā samatālaṃ gāndhāri । ūrdhvapādamityādibhiḥ santoṣya । evamevāgneyadiśi kollitālamathāvaraṇam । lilitamityādibhiḥ (l.6) santoṣya । yāmyadiśi – bhṛṅgitālam । kauśikam । bhujaṅgatrāsamityādibhiḥ kṛtvā । naiṛte – mallatālam । nāṭṭabhāṣā । bhujaṅgamityādinā santoṣya । (p.34b, f.31a, yāgaśālā, l.1) vāruṇe ca – navatālam । śrīkāmaram । kuñcitamityādi vāyavyamāśritya । balitālaṃ tarkeśi । bhrāmariti । uttarepyevaṃ tarkeśi tarkarāgam ālāḍamiti kṛtvā (l.2) īśāne – śālāpāṇi । pratyalibhūmityādibhiḥ santoṣya । indrādikrameṇa pradakṣaṇīkṛtyālayaṃ pravaśya । dhvajamūle liṅgapīṭhe vā śeṣaṃ kṣipediti (l.3) bṛhattālavidhiḥ ॥

(yāgaśālārcanā -) ?
	tataḥ pūrvoktaprakāreṇa maṅgalāṅkuraṃ nivṛtyānantaraṃ yāgaśālārcanā । devāgre vā īśānāgneyasauma....(l.4)vāruṇe vā । pañcahastādimaṇṭape paścimadvāraṃ vidhāya । tanmadhye yathoktavedikādarpaṇodara-dupa?vedikāsahitaṃ kāryā । tadbahiḥ (l.5) pūrvādīśāntaḥ krameṇa kuṇḍānyaśrayonyardhacandraṃ trikoṇavṛttaṣaṭkoṇapadmāṣṭakoṇakuṇḍāni । indreśānayormadhye vṛttaṃ (l.6) vā caturaśraṃ vā yadvā pañcapakṣe । vṛttaṃ pradhānamīśāne caturdikṣu caturaśrāṇi kuṇḍāni । athavā ekaṃ vṛttacaturaśraṃ ca kṛtvā (p.35a, l.1) gomayenopalipya vitānadhvajadarbhamālādidvāratoraṇamaṅgalāṣṭakam । darpaṇaṃ pūrṇakumbhaṃ cā(ca) + dīpo devāṣṭamaṅgalamiti ca । vajraśaktiṃ ca daṇḍaṃ ca + cakraṃ (l.2) cedidamāyudham । etat sahitaṃ sṛksṛvādikārakasamūhayuktaṃ vidhāyārohaṇānantaraṃ yāgaśālārcanāvidhiḥ ॥

(astrayāgavidhiḥ -)
ācāryo ṛtvigbhiḥ saha sāyaṃ sandhyāṃ (l.3) viśeṣasandhyāṃ ca nirvartyālayaṃ praviśya । devāgre sthaṇḍilaṃ vidhāyāstreṇa saṃprokṣya । kumbhān susnigdhān sūḍānna vā hemādimṛṇmayāntā(l.4)......kān sakūrcān sāpidhānān suvastrapallavasasūtrasahitān sahiraṇyaratnādisaṃyuktānyathoktalakṣaṇānastreṇa vāriṇā (l.5) prakṣālya । dhūpayitvā । hṛdā gandhatoyenāpūrya sthaṇḍilopari madhye śivakumbhamuttare vardhanīṃ parito'ṣṭaghaṭān saṃsthāpya । (l.6) mūrtimanatreṇa kumbhaṃ śaktyā vardhanīṃ cānantādiśikhaṇḍyantāt kalaśeṣu saṃpūjya । tatparitaḥ nandyādyāyudhāntaṃ ca paricārakamāhūya śivakumbha(p.35b, f.32a, l.1)vardhanyau ca krameṇa dhārayitvā । vajrādicakrāntaṃ śaṅkhadundubhinirghoṣairvedādighoṣaiṣca vimānaṃ pradakṣaṇīkṛtyā(tya) yāgaśālāṃ praveśya । (l.2) vedikāmadhye – aṣṭadroṇaśālibhistadardhataṇḍulaistadardhatilalājaiḥ saptadhānyaiśca paristīrya । tanmadhye śivakumbhavardhanyau vidyeśvaraghaṭāśca (l.3) saṃsthāpya । dharmādisūryāntaṃ tattatsthāne saṃsthāpya । ṛtvigbhiryajamānena saha gururvināyakapūjāpurassaraṃ puṇyāhaṃ vācayitvā । saura(l.4)pūjāṃ nityavat kṛtvā । visṛjyaiva । samācamya । sakalīkṛtya । sāmānyārghyaṃ saṃsādhya । pūrvādidvārāṇi – saṃpūjya । pratidvāraṃ dvau dvau nandyādi(l.5)caṇḍāntaṃ pūrvavat saṃpūjya । paścime - nityadvārapālānārādhya । naiṛte – vāstunāthaṃ saṃpūjya । brahmasthānadadakṣiṇato dunmukhaḥ (l.6) samupaveśya । bhūtaśuddhyādiviśeṣārghyaṃ ca kṛtvātmānaṃ gandhādyuṣṇīṣāntai(ntaiḥ) mūrtipaiḥ sahālaṅkṛtya । pañcagavyavidhivadvidhāya pañcagavya(p.36a, l.1) ..... jalairmaṇṭapaśuddhiṃ kṛtvā । śivakumbhavardhanyau pūrvavajjapāntaṃ saṃpūjya । lokapālān vāhanāyudhaṃ saṃyuktāṃ dhyātvā । saṃpūjya । mahālakṣmīṃ gaṇapatiṃ (l.2) gurupaṅktiścābhyarcya । sarvān śivājñāṃ śrāvayet । tataḥ sthaṇḍilā-ddakṣiṇatopaviśya । śivakumbhe – śaktyādiśaktiparyantamāsanaṃ saṃpūjya । daṇḍabhaṅgi (l.3) muṇḍabhaṅagi-vaktrabhaṅgi-kalābhaṅgyā ca nyastvā । sarvādhvopetaṃ śivaṃ pūrvoktavat dhyātvāvāhya । vardhanyāṃ śrīkaṇṭhanyāsaṃ vidhāya ।

dvibhujāṃ cāruvadanāṃ sustanāṃ ca suyauvanām ।
dukūlavasanāṃ śāntāṃ kinnāt prahasitānanām ॥
sarvābharaṇasaṃyuktā*māsane hanayat sthitām ।* (l.5)
karaṇḍamakuṭopetāṃ dhyāyeddevīmanukramāt ॥

	iti dhyātvā tanmūlenāvāhya । tau pañcāvaraṇapañcopacāraiḥ saṃpūjya । naivedya । japaṃ ca samarpya । stutvā । prārthyāgneyādikoṇeṣu dharmādi(p.36b, f.33a, agnikāryam, rakṣābandhanavidhi, l.1)saṃpūjya । śivājñāṃ śrāvayitvā kuṇḍāntikaṃ vrajeyuḥ । kuṇḍasamskārādipūrṇāntaṃ vidhāyāgniṃ navadhā pañcadhā vā vibhajya । pūrvādikuṇḍeṣu (l.2) nikṣipya । tattacchivaṃ sāṅgamabhyarcya । caruhavanaprāyaścittāntamajapaiḥ saha juhuyuḥ । tattatkuṇḍeṣu śilāṃ viśeṣataḥ saṃpūjya । (l.3) aśvatthodumbaraplakṣavaṭā(ṭāḥ) pūrvādidikṣu ca śamīkhādiramāyūraśrīvṛkṣāgnādikoṇataḥ । pradhāne tu palāśaḥ syāt । sa ca sarvatra sammataḥ । (l.4) carulājasarṣapa-yavatilamudgamāṣaśimbāḍakacaṇakā kulutha(ttha)sarṣapāṃśca hutvā । dravyānte mūlenāṣṭottaraśataṃ sahasramardhaṃ (l.5) vā pūrṇāsahitaṃ juhuyuḥ । śrotravandanaṃ kṛtvā । vahni nirudhya । homarakṣāṃ mūlaliṅgādiborāṇāṃ ca dāpayitvā । svayaṃ ca ṛtvigbhiḥ saha dhṛtvā tatraiśāne mārute vā śūlaṃ samānīya । sthāpayitvā । pratidinaṃ sāyaṃ prātarevameva kārayedityastrayāgavidhiḥ samāptaḥ ॥ (p.37a, l.1)

atha rakṣābandhanavidhiḥ -
	tataḥ svarṇarajitakārpāsebhyo'nyatamaṃ rakṣāsūtraṃ tithiraudranavātmakaṃ ṣoḍaśāṅgulāyāmaṃ liṅgasya tannāhasamaṃ piṇḍikāyāṃ nālasamaṃ stha.....(l.2)kāyāṃ taṇḍulalājapuṣpairāpūrya tanmadhye padmamālikhya tadupari vinyasya । paritaḥ bhasmarajanīdūrvāgandhapuṣpaphalasvarṇatāmbūlādi datvā । tatsūtraṃ tryambake......(l.3)bhasmanā sammṛjya । puṇyāhaṃ vācayitvā । ṣaḍutthāsanapūrvamabhyarcya । dhūpadīpau datvālayaṃ pradakṣiṇī-kṛtyāntaḥpraviśya śivaṃ saṃpūjya । dhūpadīpau datvā । ......(l.4)sukīṃ dhyātvā । liṅgamadhye mūlena dhṛtvā । piṇḍikāyāṃ tālabhāge tanmūlena bandhayitvā । triyambakena bhasma samarpya । tadupari rajani datvā ...... (l.5) gandhādibhirabhyarcyāstrarājasya madhyapatre bandhayitvāstrādibhaktāntaberāṇāṃ triyambakena sahṛdā dakṣahaste bandhayet । gauryā.......(l.6)distrīberāṇāṃ ca gaurīmūlamantreṇa vāmahaste kautukaṃ bandhayet । svasya ca ṛtvijāṃ ca balivāhakasya bherītāḍasya ca daśāyudhānāṃ (l.7) ca kautukaṃ bandhayitvā । tadupari bhasmarajani samarpya । dhvajadaṇḍamadhye kautukaṃ dhṛtvā । sapuṣpaṃ grandhiṣu rajanyā saha sudṛḍhaṃ bandhayet । (p.37b, l.1) sarveṣāṃ rajanicūrṇaṃ svarṇaphalatāmbūlādi dāpayet । iti rakṣābandhanavidhiḥ ॥

tato baliḥ –
mahotsave sāyaṃ prātaḥ brahmasthānādi(l.2)dhvajāntaṃ vā vṛṣādibalipīṭhāntaṃ veti bali dadyāt । tadyathā – brahmasthānādisthāneṣu saptapramāṇadīrghaṃ tadardhavistāraṃ dvādaśāṅgulamaṣṭāṅgulaṃ (l.3) vā ghanameghalā-trayayutamekaikapīṭhakarṇikāṣṭadalayuktaśilāmayaṃ vāthavā iṣṭakāmayaṃ sāradārumayaṃ vā salakṣaṇam ūṭhāni (l.4) nirmayā lepya pañcacūrṇaistattaddigalaṅkṛtyā(tya) droṇaṃ vā tadardhataṇḍulaṃ vā vipacya । balipātre vādāyāstreṇa saṃprokṣya । (l.5) tadanyatsthālikāyāṃ tadardhasamānīyā-nnaliṅgaṃ salakṣaṇaṃ kurvīta dhyāyet ।

dakṣiṇe śūlasaṃyuktaṃ raudradṛṣṭisamanvitam । (l.6)
nāgābharaṇasaṃyuktamūrdhvakeśamathokṣi ca ॥
etat pāśupataṃ divyaṃ liṅgamadhye prapūjayet ।

	yadvā – pūrvavat śivaṃ saṃpūjyācāryaḥ (l.7) saha ri(ṛ)tvik daśāyudhāstrarājayuktaḥ bhasmādyuṣṇīṣāntairalaṅkṛtaḥ paricārakasahito vicitradhvajachatracāmarādi(l.8) sahito nānāvādyaghaṇṭādyā brahmaghoṣaiśca nṛttagītagāyakasahito baliṃ dadyāt ।

navāhotsave – viśeṣa(p.38a, l.1)balidevān krameṇocyate । prathame'hani – vighnarājāya svāhā । dvitīye'hani – bhūtebhya(yaḥ) svāhā । tṛtīye ṛṣibhyaḥ । caturthe indrāya । pañcame'hani (l.2) brahmaṇe । ṣaṣṭhe viṣṇu(ṇa)ve । saptame rudrāya । aṣṭame īśvarāya । navame'hani sadāśivāya svāhā । ityeṣu divaseṣu havirliṅga(l.3)pūjānte kāryam । śuddhānnamājyayuktaṃ dadhipla(plu)taṃ kukkuḍāṇḍapramāṇaṃ kṣipet ।

viśeṣabaliḥ –
gosthāne – (l.4) rudrāya kumbhodarāya । kuśeṣu – laṅkāyai । nandiṣu – gaṅgāyai । taṭāke – kṣetramūrtaye । sabhāsthāne – sarasvatyai । (l.5) darbhāyai – kośe । mahālakṣmi – gopure । bhīmāya svāhetyevaṃ sāyaṃ prātarbaliṃ diśet । brahmasthānādibaliṃ pīṭhāntaṃ (l.6) daṇḍāntaṃ vā layaṃ praviśya balipīṭhe śeṣaṃ kṣipet । annaliṅgaṃ jale visṛjya । pādau prakṣālyācamyā(mya) । (l.7) śuddhabhasmanoddhūlya praveśayediti balibhramaṇavidhiḥ ॥

atha yānakramavidhiḥ -
	pūrvoktavadvidhiḥ samalaṅkṛtyā(tya) rathe (p.38b, l.1) raṅge gheṭake śibikādau vā nānālaṅkārapuṣpaprabhāsamāyuktaṃ devaṃ śaktyāsahitamūlasthāne samānīyāvāhya । dhūpadīpau (l.2) datvā । puṣpāñjalinābhyarcya । śaktyādibhaktāntaberasahitaṃ pradakṣiṇamupakramet । agrato nānālaṅkārasahitaṃ bheryādivādyasahitaṃ (l.3) śailādīn tadantarāle nandivināyakasubrahmaṇyau madhye devyā saha somāskandamagrataḥ pṛṣṭhato vā bhaktabherāśca tatpaścāccaṇḍeśvaraṃ ca deśikaḥ (l.4) sa bhāge sthitvā mūlamaghoraṃ vā saṅkhyaṃ japet । agrato nṛpaḥ । pṛṣṭhato vedādighoṣayuktāstatpaścāt cāturvarṇodbhavā māheśvaraśca (l.5) anugacchantaḥ aśvameghaphalaṃ prāpnuvanti । evaṃ devaṃ santoṣya । prathame'hani prātaḥ rāgam । rātrau haṃsam । dvitīye'hani sūryasomayantram । (l.6) tṛtīye bhūtayantram । rātrau siṃham । caturthe nāgam । rātrau vṛṣabhapradakṣiṇāntarāle pariveṣaṇaṃ kuryāt । tadyathā – udyānādiṣvaṅkaṇe (p.39a, l.1) vedastutiphaṇāveṇugāyakanṛttakarudragaṇikāsahitaṃ phalādyupahāraistāmbūlādibhiśca pade pade devaṃ santoṣyālayaṃ praveśayet । (l.2) pañcame'hani āndolikāśikharayuktarāvaṇayantram । ṣaṣṭhe'hani śibikāyāṃ gheṭake vā samāropya । cūrṇotsavaṃ vakṣyamāṇavat kṛtvā । kuṅkumakusuma(l.3)haridrādijalaiśca devān saṃkṣya kuṅkumapaṅkairārmevanālipya sarvān prokṣayet । rātri gajam । saptame ratham । aṣṭame aśvāropaṇaṃ (l.4) kṛtvā । yānakāle aśvārūḍākantādyāyudhadhattā dhanuḥ kheṭakayuktāḥ sarvālaṅkārasahitarudragaṇikāḥ parito vrajeyuḥ । atrāntare (l.5) duṣṭanigrahaśiṣṭaparipālanaṃ kṛtvā । sakaladharmādharmāḥ śrāvayitvā । layaṃ praveśayet । navāhe puruṣayantrayuktāndolika(l.6)sagheṭake । rātrau kṛṣṇagandham । daśame'hani ratnasihmāsane devaṃ samāropya purādikaṃ pradakṣiṇīkṛtya । harmyāgre dhvajāgre vā (p.39b, f.36a, atha nīrājanavidhiḥ, l.1) vikṛtaṃ taṃ paribhrāmya taccatuṣpatha(thaṃ) prakṣipet । anantaraṃ guruṇā suvāsinībhi(bhiḥ) rudragaṇikābhirvā nīrāñjanaṃ kārayet । tato devāya ghaṭadīpaṃ datvānantaraṃ (l.2) ghaṭadīpasahitam ārātrikanīrāñjanahastayuktā rudragaṇikā nṛttagītādibhiḥ sakalavādyaiḥ saha pradakṣiṇaṃ kramāt । kṛtvā śailādinandivināyakaskandadevī-bhakta(l.3)caṇḍeśvaraiḥ saha prātaḥ pradakṣiṇīkṛtyālayaṃ vrajeyuḥ । tato maṅgalaṃ gāyatribhiḥ sarvavādyaiḥ saha somāskandamālayaṃ praveśayitvā (l.4) sthānamaṇṭapādau saṃsthāpya । pracchannapaṭaṃ kṛtvā । mahāhaviṃ nivedya । sarvopacāraiḥ saṃpūjya । śramaharaṇāntaṃ (l.5) nālikerekṣusāramudgatatkālocitaphalāni nivedya । brāhmaṇān gandhapuṣpairalaṅkṛtya । bhojayet । śivabhaktādīnāndhādiścā(l.6)vicāreṇa bhojayet । athavānyaprakāreṇa yānaṃ kuryāditi yānakramavidhiḥ samāptaḥ ॥

atha nīrājanavidhiḥ – (p.40a, l.1)
nīrāñjanārthaṃ svarṇaraji(ja)tatāmrakāmsyapātrāṇi vā vitastivistāramoṣṭamekāṅgulaṃ pañca saṃgṛhya । maṇḍalopari vinyasya । gandhapuṣpādināgnibījena (l.2) brahmabhirabhyarcya । catuḥ samskāraiḥ samskṛtya । śūlamudrāṃ dhenumudrāṃ pradarśya । svastisūktaiḥ maṅgalavācakaiḥ sarpiṣṭaḥ pātrai(traiḥ) dīpamādāya । śirasi (l.3) nidhāya । pradakṣiṇīkṛtya । devasyāgre maṇḍalake saṃsthāpya । devasya śiraḥprabhṛti pādāntaṃ tridhā tridhā brahmabhiḥ paribhrāmya । darpaṇacchatracāmaravyajana(l.4)bhasmādi samarpya । mūlena śivāya puṣpāñjaliṃ datvā । praṇamya । gaṇikābhiḥ pūrvavadvāhayitvā vādyādibhi(bhiḥ) saha pīṭhāgre vṛṣāgre vā (l.5) kṣipet । pādaprakṣālanādikaṃ kṛtvālayaṃ praviśediti nīrājanavidhiḥ ॥

atha tīrthasaṃgrahaṇavidhiḥ –
	hariḥ om । (l.6) atha tīrthasya pūrvasmin dine tīrthaṃ saṃgrahet । rakṣāṃ pūrvavat kṛtvāśiṣo vācayitvā । maṇṭapamadhye pūrvavat sthaṇḍilamekaṃ kṛtvā । (p.40b, f.37a, cūrṇotsavam, l.1) tanmadhye manonmanikarakaṃ tatparito'ṣṭakaraḥ saṃsthāpya ।
madhye –
siddharābhāṃ triṇetrāmamṛtaśaśikalāṃ śekhariraktayasmāṃ
	pīnottuṅga*pravṛttastanabharaṇamitā yauvanārambhaṇām ।* (l.2)
nānālaṅkārayuktāṃ sarasijanilayaṃ bījasaṃkrāntamūrtīṃ
	devīṃ pāśāṅkuśāḍhyāmabhayavarakarāṃ *viśvayoniṃ namāmi ॥* (l.3) iti dhyātvā ।

	saṃpūjya । sa pūrvādyaṣṭaghaṭeṣu । oṃ hāṃ gaṅgāyai namaḥ । yamunāyai namaḥ । narmadāyai (l.4) namaḥ । sarasvatyai namaḥ । sindhuve namaḥ । godāvaryai namaḥ । kāveryai namaḥ । tāmraparṇyai namaḥ । ityāvāhanādisarvopacāraiḥ (l.5) saṃpūjya । śirasāvāhayitvāstra-samanvitam । sarvātodyasamāyuktaṃ puraṃ pradakṣiṇīkṛtya । nandyāditīrtheṣu snānārthaṃ saṃpraveśaye(l.6)diti tīrthasaṃgrahaṇavidhiḥ ॥

atha cūrṇotsavavidhiḥ –
	tīrthasaṃgrahadineṣu balinayānāt paraṃ cūrṇamārabhet । (p.41a, l.1) prāsādāgre sthaṇḍiladvaye padmamālikhya । puṇyāhaṃ vācayitvā । darbhaiḥ paristīrya । ekasmin śūlaṃ saṃsthāpaya । plakṣodumbarāśvatthavaṭādivṛkṣeṇa (l.2) ulūkhalaṃ musalaṃ khādireṇa salakṣaṇaṃ kṛtvā । pare saṃsathāpyāstreṇa saṃprokṣya । catuḥ samskāraiḥ samskṛtya । lūkhalaṃ musalaṃ ca vastreṇāveṣṭya । brahma(l.3)daivatyamiti lūkhalāstrayo madhye rudrakumbhaṃ parito lokapālāṣṭakalaśān saṃpūjya । yadvā – śivaṃ vābhyarcya lūkhalaparitaḥ pūrvāddaleṣu (l.4) dūrvā saha devīkoṣṭhaṃ rajanitailaghṛtatāmbūlaphalagandhapuṣpabhasma saṃsathāpya । catuḥ samskāraiḥ samskṛtya । lūkhale (l.5) pṛthivi meruṃ musale saṃpūjaya । tat lūkhale prakṣipya । padamantreṇā(ṇa) vā bhaktastotreṇa vā musalāghātena gaṇikābhiḥ (l.6) cūrṇaṃ tailena miśrīkṛtya sahiraṇyamācāryahaste cūrṇaṃ gṛhītvā । mūlaliṅge bhasmanā saha dāpayitvā kuṅkumoṣṇīṣaṃ (p.41b, f.38a, tīrtham, l.1) dhṛtvā । tatpaṅkairālipya । nivedya । dhūpadīpau datvā । sarveṣāṃ berāṇāṃ ca tatpākenālipya । padmamudrāṃ pradarśya । svayaṃ ca dhṛtvā । (l.2) sarveṣāṃ ca uṣṇīṣarajanītāmbūlādi datvā । purādikaṃ triśūlena saha pradakṣiṇīkṛtyālayaṃ praviśet । athavāstrakumbhaṃ vajrādikalaśāṃśca (l.3) saṃpūjayediti cūrṇotsavavidhiḥ ॥

(tīrthasnānavidhiḥ -)
	paścāt pūravapadaṃ karaṃ kṛtvā । tīrthasnānamācaret । sarveṣāmaspṛṣṭai(l.4)spṛṣṭadoṣanivṛttyarthaṃ tīrthaprasādasiddhyarthaṃ sarvadevastu yāvahasamāda? phalasiddhyarthaṃ sālokādipadaprāptyarthaṃ tīrtha(l.5)tīre śakvāsahāstrarājasomāskandādyaṅkurachatracāmarādinānāvādyavedaghoṣastutibhiḥ sārdhantikakumbhasahitaṃ (l.6) vihitapraghādike devān saṃsthāpya । tadagre sthaṇḍile manonmani gaṅgādyaṣṭakumbhāṃścāghatīrya-pālikāsa.....saṃ (p.42a, l.1) sthāpya viśeṣataḥ saṃpūjya aiśānyāṃ durgāmāvāhya triśūlasannidhau trayastriṃśaddevatāśca(ścā)ṣṭādaśagaṇāśca lokapālāścāhvānaṃ kṛtvāstrādi(l.2)devatānāṃ kautukaṃ visṛjya । tīrthamadhye prakṣipya । pālikaiḥ saha śivādyastraparyantaṃ tattadghaṭairabhiṣicya sarvajanaiḥ saha snānamācaret । (l.3) tata ācāryaḥ dīrthadevānudvāsyālayaṃ praviśya । sarveṣāṃ devānāṃ sva sva kumbhamabhiṣicya । naivedya dhūpadīpādi datvā(l.4)layaṃ praviśyāsthānasthāḥ sarve brahmaghoṣasahita ācāryahaste āślakṣatai(taiḥ) datvā deśika āślakṣatairāśīrvādapurassaraṃ (l.5) sva sva sthānaṃ praveśayediti tīrthasnānavidhiḥ ।

atha ghaṭasnānavidhiḥ –
	harotsavasya pūrṇārthajanānāsthitikāraṇārthameva prasādasiddhyarthamindrādidevatāsāmīpyasiddhyarthaghaṭasnānavidhiḥ kārayet । kuṇḍeṣu viśeṣeṇa homaṃ vidhāya । kuṇḍeṣu pūrṇāhutiṃ jihuyuḥ । (l.2) tattaddiśaṃ pradhāne saṃyojya । śivamabhyarcya । punaḥ pūrṇāṃ datvā । śivakumbhādīn viśeṣeṇābhyarcya । saṃyojya । dhūpadīpaṃ datvā । ācāryādibhyo yathoktadakṣiṇā (l.3) datvā । paricārakādibhyo vastrahemādi datvā । pūrvavat kumbhānuddhṛtyālayaṃ pradakṣiṇīkṛtya । garbhagṛhe saṃsthāpya । ṣaḍadhvanyāsapūrvaṃ mūla(l.4)liṅge'bhiṣecayet । vardhanīṃ pīṭhe vidyeśakalaśān tattaddiksthāne'bhiṣicya devīṃ śrīkaṇṭhanyāsapūrvaṃ kalaśenābhiṣicya mahāhaviṃ (l.5) nivedya । dhūpadīpaṃ datvāgnisadanaṃ yayāt । śivāgnau sva śirasā mūlenāghorāsinā śatādhikaṃ hṛtvā । pūrṇāṃ datvāgniṃ yāgeśe saṃyojaya (l.6) gurūpadiṣṭaprakāreṇa kumbhavardhanyādipañcāvaraṇadevatāṃśca tatsthāne saṃyojya । tamayi(pi) visarjayet । prabhṛ(bhū)tahaviṣaṃ datvā । bimbaṃ (p.43a, l.1) prati snānaṃ kārayediti ghaṭasnānavidhiḥ ॥


duṣṭanigrahaśiṣṭaparipālanārthaṃ dīrghadivasapūrvāhṇādike rātrau vā bṛhattālapaṭhanapūrvamaparāhṇaṃ kuryā(ryāt) । devamuttamottamaprakāreṇa (l.2) dhvajasthaṃ vṛṣabhe saṃyojya । vṛṣabhamapi śivāgre sthitavigrahe samāropyāvaraṇadevāṃśca tadāvaraṇe saṃyojya । yathāvidhi pūjayet । taddine maunabaliḥ ।

(maunabaliḥ -)
	homānte (l.3) havirbhāgadevatānāmityuktatvāt tannimittaṃ maunabalimārabhet । ācāryaḥ somāskandasamīpaṃ gatvā । śaktyādiśaktyantaṃ saṃpūjya । daṇḍabhaṅgimuṇḍabhaṅgiekatriṃśatkalānyāsaṃ (l.4) śrīkaṇṭhanyāsaṃ ca kṛtvā dhyāyet ।

dakṣālambitavāmanidratapadaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ varaṃ cābhayam ।
ṭaṅkaṃ cāpi dhṛtaṃ varaṃ ca kaṭakaṃ bibhrāṇayā vāmake ॥
vāmālambitanidratānyapadayo *devyāyutaṃ madhyata(taḥ) ।* (l.5)
skandenābjakaradvayena sahitaṃ somā guheśaṃ bhaje ॥

	iti sāṅgamāvaraṇapañcakopataṃ saṃpūjya । vakṣyamāṇavaddevīṃ dhyātvābhyarcya । jagataḥ (l.6) śubhaṃ prārthya । tasmāllabdhānujño maunavratadharo bandhā(baddhā)ñjalipuṭo bhūtvā । annaliṅgasamanvita-mastrādidevīn yāne samāropya । gandhapuṣpādisaṃyuktā (l.7) khaḍgakheṭakāstrādisaṃyuktā bahurūpaṃ hṛdistaranto maunavratadharā vādyaghoṣaṃ vinā grāsārdhakakukkuṭāṇḍamātrā vā brahmasthānādisandhiṣu pīṭheṣu (p.43b, f.40a, l.1) tattatsthāne vādyaghoṣasahitadhvajāvarohaṇapūrvaṃ baliṃ vidhāyā(ya) । pūrvavat sandhidevān yathākrameṇa visṛjya । pradakṣiṇīkṛtyālayaṃ praviśyāvarūhya । pūrvasthale vinyasya । (l.2) sarvānapi cānnaliṅgajale visarjayedityavarohaṇavidhiḥ ॥

atha caṇḍotsavavidhiḥ –
paścāccaṇḍamūrtiṃ yāgālaye samānīya । tatkumbha(mbhe)nābhiṣicya । yathāvidhi saṃpūjya । nirmālyādina(nā) (l.3) vibhūṣya । prāyaścittamagnau śataṃ sahasramardhaṃ hutvā । tamapi visṛjyālayaṃ naurya?trikoṇabhaktajanaiḥsārdhaṃ pradakṣiṇīkṛtya । tatsthāne niveśayediti caṇḍotsava(l.4)vidhiḥ ॥

(ācāryotsavavidhaḥ -)
ācāryaṃ pūjayetpaścāt dakṣiṇāṃ ca pradāpayet । gobhūmiratnahemaratnādivastrāntāni pradāpayet । ācāryamanasā tṛptiḥ । tasmāttṛptiḥ paraśśivaḥ । (l.5) iti tamapi ṛtvikbhiḥ saha śivavat saṃpūjya । rathe śibikādau vā saṃsthāpya । nānāvādyaiḥ nṛttagītādibhirvedādighoṣairbhaktastutibhiścatracāmarādinānāvidhadīpaiḥ (l.6) saha bhūpayajamānasarvajanaiśca puraṃ pradakṣiṇīkṛtya । yānena svagṛhaṃ praveśayedi-tyācāryotsavavidhiḥ ॥

(rathārohaṇavidhiḥ -)
saptame vā mahārathārohaṇaṃ kāryam । tat (l.7) pūrvarātrau rakṣābandhanaṃ kṛtvā prabhāte snapanādyabhiṣekaṃ nityapūjāvasāne vidhāya । śayadhāvidhi? । saṃpūjyāgnikāryoktamārgeṇa caruhavana-(p.44a, l.1)prāyaścittāntaṃ pūrvavat kṛtvā । ājyaṃ mūlena । samidīśena । carumaghoreṇa । hṛdā lājam । vāmena tilam । kavacena gulam । netreṇa bilvam । yathāśakti hutvā । īśāna(nena) dravyāṇi (l.2) dhānyādi hṛdā । vyāhṛtibhiśca hutvā । pūrṇāṃ datvā । yāgeśaṃ nirudhya । rakṣāṃ samarpya । pūrṇakumbhaṃ tilapātraṃ savatsāṃ gāṃ pradarśya । brāhmaṇebhyo dadyāt । vighneśaṃ (l.3) saṃpūjya । modakaṃ nivedya । devatānāṃ pāyasaṃ nivedya । paścādraupyakaṭāhe kuṅkumāgarukarpūracandanaharidrādyairvā sanniyojya । (l.4) kaṭāhe pāṭalamutpalajātipunnāgacampakādyāḥ paṅke nikṣipya । kuṅkumavastrādinā kaṭāhaṃ pariveṣṭya । śivāgre sthaṇḍile vinyasya । (l.5) kaṭāhe kāmabījenābhyarcyārdrapaṅkaṃ mūlenābhyarcya । samskṛtya । dhūpadīpau datvā । mūlaliṅgādiberāṇāṃ ca taddatvā deśika(kaḥ) svayaṃ ca ṛtvigbhiḥ (l.6) sahārdrapaṅkaṃ kuṅkumoṣṇīṣaṃ ca dhṛtvā । bhūpayajamānabhaktādisarvajanānāmādra(rdra)paṅkamuṣṇīṣaṃ dadet । paścādrathaṃ pañcagavyapuṇyāhajalena saṃprokṣya । (p.44b, f.41a, l.1) rathaṃ catuḥ samskāraiḥ samskṛtya । tasmin devaṃ samāropya । sthāpayitvā । rathe sarvān devān prapūjayet । cakreṣu – oṃ hāṃ sūryāya namaḥ । candramase namaḥ । (l.2) paritaḥ – haraye namaḥ । vajriṇe namaḥ । ekapraipe – dvidaṇḍakāya namaḥ । ityabhyarcya । patākāsu – śatarudrān । gātreṣu – dharmādīn । sthalikāyām – sadāśivāya namaḥ । (l.3) sandhiṣu – dakṣiṇāmūrtaye namaḥ । viṣṇave namaḥ । brahmaṇe namaḥ । skandāya namaḥ । ityabhyarcya । rathaprānte – lokapālān । rajjuṣu – uragebhyo namaḥ । ityevaṃ (l.4) krameṇābhyarcya । vicitrapaṭavastrādyairalaṅkṛtya । digvidikṣu dvārapārśve ca – saiṃhakadalīpūgastambakanālikeravicitrapaṭādinā suśobhitaṃ (l.5) kṛtvā brahmāṇaṃ sārathyaṃ kalpayitvā । sacchatre pāśupatāstramāvāhya । dhvaje – vṛṣabhamabhyarcya । dhyāyayet ।

pañcavarṇo mahādīptamabhayaṃ varadānvitam । (l.6)
ṣaṭpadaṃ caikakukṣiṃ ca śūlaṃ pāśaṃ caturbhujam ॥
ekavaktraṃ triṇetraṃ ca ṣaṭsthalaṃ maṅgalānvitam ।
pādaṃ cakrasamāyuktaṃ bhu(bhū)tādhāraṃ carmāmpsikam ॥ (p.45a, l.1)
pauḍa(dha)?sthalaṃ sujānvākhyamuparisthalamūrukam ।
kukṣi devāsanaṃ proktaṃ hṛdayaṃ sthambhavargakam ॥
kūṭaṃ skandhadvayaṃ proktaṃ kaṇṭhaṃ karṇamitīritam ।
śiraśśiraśśikhāṃ cūli *sārathyaṃ vīthikalpitam ॥* (l.2)
rathadhyānamidaṃ proktaṃ sarvamaṅgalakāraṇam ।

	iti dhyātvā । saṃpūjayet । evaṃ devī-skanda-vināyakacaṇḍeśvarāśca tattadrathe samāropya mṛpaṅkaṃ? hṛdālipya । (l.3) kaṅkamoṣṇīṣaṃ kavacena dhṛtvā । phalāpūpādi nivedya । raktapuṣpairabhyarcya । dānāni datvācāryādibhaktānnānāvastrahemaphalatāmbūlādi (l.4) datvā । sarvān santoṣya । sumuhūrte śubhāmśe caralagne svastipūrvaṃ samastavādyayuktaṃ puraṃ pradakṣiṇīkṛtyā(tya) rathādavatā(l.5)ryālayaṃ praviśya । snapanapūrvā(rvaṃ) snāpayitvā । śilpādirathaniyantṝṇāṃ vastrādi datvā । mahāhavirnivedya । sarveṣāṃ dāpayediti rathārohaṇa(l.6)vidhiḥ samāptaḥ ॥


ataḥparaṃ kṛṣṇagandhavidhiḥ -
	iṣṭakāmyārthasiddhyarthasuputrāvyāptihetukam ।
	brahmahatyādidoṣaghnaṃ sarvaśatrunibarhaṇam ॥ (p.45b, f.42a, l.1)
	yo vā ko vā nṛttamūrtidarśanāt phalamaśnute ।

	tadanu – nṛttamūrtimāsthānamaṇṭape samānīyāṣṭamadināpararātrau rakṣāṃ pūrvavat kṛtvā । phalatāmbūlādiyuktahaste (l.2) kṛtvā । citravastrādinā ubhāvapyalaṅkṛtya । naivedyādi datvālayaṃ pradakṣiṇīkṛtya । maṇṭape saṃsthāpya । vinodadarśanena rātriśeṣaṃ vyapohayet । apare(l.3)dyurutsavāntaṃ kārayitvā । sāyāhne devāgre sthaṇḍiladvitayaṃ vidhāya । padmamālikhya । darbhaiḥ puṣpaiḥ paristīrya । tanmadhye gālāgaruṃ (l.4) saṃsthāpya । vināyakapūjāpurassaraṃ puṇyāhaṃ vācayitvā । śāntikumbhaṃ vidhivat dvidhā saṃpūjyāgnikāryoktamārgeṇa kuṇḍasamskārādi(l.5)pūrṇāntaṃ vidhāya । tasmi(smin) maheśaṃ saṃpūjya । sa(śa)milājasarṣapayavatilādīn pratyekaṃ tanmūlenāṣṭottaraśataṃ hutvā । bilvotpalakadalī(l.6)panasādiśca । madhutrayāṇi ca hutvā । dravyānte vyāhṛtī hṛtvā । pūrṇāṃ datvā । devaṃ saṃsnāpya । alaṅkṛtya । sthalikāyāṃ taṇḍulenāpūritāyāṃ (p.46a, l.1) kṛṣṇagandhaṃ nidhāya । vastreṇāveṣṭya । śantikumbhajalenā(na) prokṣya । karpūrādyaiḥ sugandhibhi(bhiḥ) miśrīkṛtya । tamastreṇa śodhya । varmaṇāvakuṇṭhya । brahmāṅgairabhimantryā(ntrya) (l.2) mūlena saṃpūjya । dhenumudrāṃ pradarśya । pādyācamanārghyaṃ dhūpadīpādi datvā । sampātābhihutaṃ kṛtvā । pūrṇāṃ vidhāyāgniṃ visṛjyālayaṃ śaṅkhadundubhi-vādyaghoṣa(l.3)sahitaṃ pradakṣiṇīkṛtyā(tya) garbhagṛhaṃ praviśya । mūlaliṅge samarpyānantaraṃ maheśāgre saṃsthāpya । śaktyādiśaktyantāsane daṇḍamuṇḍabhaṅgyārcaṇa(na)kartri...(l.4)śatkalātmalakaṃ śrīkaṇṭhaṃ ca nyastvā ।

dhyāyet –
saṅkīrṇajajaṭahinduhaimakurarimuṇḍāsthidevāpagā
	śvetāt dūmarājapiñcakadharaṃ vaiyāghra*kṛtyambaram ।* (l.5)
sarpaṃ savyakare'bhayaṃ ḍamarukaṃ vāmaṃ prasāryānyakaṃ
	vāmerdagniṃ dadhataṃ śayānaharitā bhasmārapṛṣṭhasthitam ॥

vakrīkṛtya svadakṣiṇaṃ ca *caraṇaṃ coddhṛtya vāmam ।* (l.6)
prabhamāmadhyasthaṃ dhvajakiṅkiṇimaṇikalākoṭisphuratpādukam ॥
dakṣena kratukuṇḍalaṃ śrutipuṭe vāme tu vaktrānvitam ॥ (p.46b, f.43a, gaurīkalānayāsavidhi, l.1)
bhasmāliptamumāyutaṃ triṇayanaṃ vande naṭeśaṃ śivam ।

	ityāvāhya । devīṃ ca pañcaviṃśatikalātmakaṃ śrīkaṇṭhaṃ ca nyastvā । dhyāyet – oṃ himāyai namaḥ । mūrdhni । vidyāyai namaḥ । (l.2) vaktre । mṛtyuve namaḥ । hṛdaye । śuddhāyai namaḥ । karṇe । jarāyai namaḥ । dakṣiṇabāhu । medhāyai namaḥ । vāmabāhu । svadhāyai namaḥ । jaḍa(ṭha)re । svastyai namaḥ । nābhau । (l.3) pālinyai namaḥ । yonau । jvālinyai namaḥ । guhye । kṛṣṇāyai namaḥ । dakṣiṇorau । smṛtyai namaḥ । vāmorau । kṣamāyai namaḥ । dakṣiṇajānau । matyai namaḥ । (l.4) vāmajānau । prajāyai namaḥ । dakṣiṇajaṅghe । cintāyai namaḥ । vāmajaṅaghe । dhṛtyai namaḥ । pṛṣṭhe । krayāyai namaḥ । dakṣiṇapārśve । kṣāntyai namaḥ । vāmapārśve । (l.5) śāntyai namaḥ । vāmapāde । bhrāntyai namaḥ । dakṣiṇapāde । dayāyai namaḥ । dakṣiṇahaste । smṛtyai namaḥ । vāmahaste । dvityai? namaḥ । dakṣiṇastanau । sandhyāyai (l.6) namaḥ । vāmastanau । vidyādehāya namaḥ । hṛdaye । netratrayāya namaḥ । netre । oṃ hrīṃ hṛdayāya namaḥ । hṛdaye । śirase namaḥ । śirasi । śikhāyai namaḥ । (p.47a, l.1) śikhāyām । kavacāyai namaḥ । kavacam । astrāyai namaḥ iti karosphāṭanam । prarta(pūrvokta)prakāreṇa śrīkaṇṭhaṃ nyastvā ।
dhyāyet -
śyāmāṃ dvinetrāṃ dvibhujāttribhaṅgī savyāpasavyasthitakuñcitāṅghrim । (l.2)
savyotpalāvākaṭakānvitā vā hastāvalambā parameśvarī syāt ॥

	ityubhāvapi saṃpūjya । dhūpadīpau datvā । paiṣṭyena pāyasena phalāpūpādinā saha (l.3) nivedya tāmbūlaṃ datvā । kṛṣṇagandhālepanaṃ kāryam । lalāṭe ghoreṇa śikhayā bāhvau । sarvatra sadyena vā dāpayet । paścāt sadāśivādimūrtīnāṃ dāpayet । (l.4) sūtraberāṇāṃ lalāṭe galatanmūlena hṛdā vā dhārayet । paścāddeśikādibhaktajanāntānāṃ kṛṣṇagandhaṃ dāpayet । devaṃ ratnasihmāsane tadvāme (l.5) gaurīṃ savyena de.......na sahāropya । vāmato'grato vā nṛpaḥ । nṛttakanṛttakī mardakā ca upayoḥ pārśvayoḥ geyanṛttanaṃ ca kṛtvā । vādyaiḥ (l.6) saha saptagrahaparyantaṃ naṭitvālayaṃ praviśyā(śya) nīrāñjanādi pūrvavat kuryāt । paścādāsthānamaṇṭape saṃsthāpya । sāṣṭāṅgamarghyaṃ datvā phalaiḥ saha (p.47b, f.44a, tiruvanandalavivāham, l.1) nivedya । nīrāñjanādyaiḥ pūjayet । paścāt gavyasnapanādinā saṃsnāpya । śuddhakarpūraṃ pūrvavat samskṛtya । phālā sarvāṅgaṃ dhṛtvā । suśvenai jātipuṣpai(l.2)....vastreścālaṅkṛtvā । pāyasaṃ nivedya । dhūpadīpādi datvā । yānakāle paṅktiyānaṃ śanaiḥ śanaiḥ kāryam । tatra vedanṛttagītādibhiḥ pūrvavat paṅkti(l.3)…..naṃ kṛtvā । nīrāñjanādisarvaṃ pūrvavat kṛtvālayaṃ praviśya । sarvopacāraiḥ saṃpūjya । rātriśeṣaṃ vinodadarśanena vyapohayet । (l.4) aruṇodaye nṛtteśaṃ saṃpūjyālaṅkṛtya । nadyādau pūrvavattīrthaṃ kṛtvā । kautukaṃ visṛjya । purādikaṃ pradakṣiṇīkṛtya । śuddhadeśe diṣṭanigrahaśiṣṭaparipālanaṃ (l.5) sarvavyavahāravācakaṃ ca śrāvayitvā । devyāḥ praṇaye kalahaṃ vyapohya । pariveṣaṃ vinālayaṃ praveśayediti svasthāne saṃsthāpya (l.6) snapanādyairabhiṣicya śuddhanṛttaṃ kārayediti kṛṣṇagandhavidhiḥ samāptaḥ ॥

	anantaraṃ daśame saptame vāhni vivāhaṃ vidhivadaupacārikeṇa (p.48a, devyutsavam, bhaktotsavam, l.1) kalyāṇavratotsavaṃ kārayet ।

atha devyutsavavidhiḥ –
navāhasaptāhatriyāhamekāhaṃ vā utsavaṃ kārayet । ekādaśadivasarātrāvindravimānādisarvamalaṅkṛtya (l.2) saṃprokṣya snapanapūrvaṃ devamabhiṣicyālaṅkṛtya devān sarvān tattadvimānamadhiropya paraṃ pradakṣiṇakāle aiśānyā(yāṃ) diśi prapāṃ puṣpādidhvajāntaira....mantrya (l.3) tanmadhye vimānasthaṃ śivamānīya taddevi pūrvavat caṇḍeśāntaṃ bhūpayajamānavādyaiḥ saha pradakṣiṇatri(tra)yaṃ kārayitvā-layapraveśakāle (l.4) gopurāgre harmyāgre vā praṇayakalahaḥ vyapohatam । vinodadarśanaṃ ca kārayitvā । yathāvidhi praveśayediti devyutsavavidhiḥ samāptaḥ ॥ (l.5)

atha bhaktotsavavidhiḥ – (p.48a, l.5)
	adha(tha) tattadberādīn śivāgre maṇḍape ca saṃsthāpya । snapanapūrvacalācalaberādīnabhiṣicya cūrṇotsavaṃ (l.6) pūrvavat kṛtvā । bhaktaberāṇāṃ dhṛtvācāryaḥ sādhakādīnāṃ dīkṣitānāṃ bhaktānāṃ cūrṇaṃ dāpayitvā kuṅkumoṣṇīṣapavitramālādi datvā । (p.48b, f.45a, l.1) bhaktastutibhi(bhiḥ) stutvā । śivabhaktānāṃ tāmbūlādi datvā । puraṃ pradakṣiṇīkṛtyālayaṃ praviśyā(śya) । tattatsthāne sarvān saṃsthāpya । nivedya । dhūpadīpau datvā । śivagānavidāṃ vastrādi (l.2) dāpayediti bhaktotsavavidhiḥ ॥

(pañcakṛtyakam -)
aparedyuḥ mahāsnapanapūrvaṃ śāntihoma-mūrtihoma-diśāhomādikaṃ krameṇa kṛtvābhiṣicya । śramaharaṇaṃ kārayet ।

utsavaṃ pañcadhā *proktaṃ sthityutpattilayātmakam ।* (l.3)
tirobhāvānugrahaṃ ca sṛṣṭyādyādyaṃ pañcakṛtyakam ॥
aṅkurārpaṇakalyāṇaṃ dhvajārohaṇameva ca ।
rakṣābandhanamityuktaṃ *sṛṣṭikarmamudāhṛtam ॥* (l.4)
vāhanotsavasaṃyuktaṃ homakarmabalibhramam ।
sthitikarmamiti proktaṃ saṃhāra(raṃ) kathyate'dhunā ॥
rātricūrṇasamāyuktaṃ *rathārohaṇameva ca ।* (l.5)
kālāgarutsavaṃ caivamāgameṣu prasiddhimat ॥
maunotsavaṃ tirobhāvaṃ śaktiyātrā tvanugraham ।
tīrthasya divasā*dyante praṇayaḥ kalahocyate ॥* (l.6)
bījā kṣetrasamāyuktaṃ sṛṣṭyādyaṃ pañcakṛtyakam ।
॥ iti parameśvaraparanāmadheyaśrīmadaghoraśivācārya(l.7)viracitāyāṃ kriyākramadyotikāyāṃ vināyakotsavādibhaktotsavāntavidhiḥ samāptaḥ ॥ śrī tripurasundarisahāyam ॥ (p.49a, māsotsavam, l.1)

atha māsotsavavidhiḥ –
saṅkrāntisamayaḥ sūkṣmo dujñe(rjñe?)yaḥ viśitekṣaṇaḥ ।
tadyogāccāpyathaścordhvaṃ triṃśaṃ nāḍyaḥ pavitratāḥ ॥
bhaviṣyatyayane puṇyastriṃśadevaṃ *tu dakṣiṇe ।* (l.2)
atītauttare nāḍya iti proktā harāḍhajāḥ ॥
saṃkrāntiviṣuvaścaiva viśeṣeṇāyanadvayam ।
vṛṣavṛścikakumbheṣu siṃhe caiva yadā raviḥ ॥ (l.3)
etat viṣṇupadaṃ nāma viṣuvādithikaṃ phalam ।
kanyāyāṃ mithune mīne dhanuṣyapi ravergatiḥ ॥
ṣaḍaśītimukhā proktā puṇyādhikatamā dvijāḥ ।
nāḍyaḥ *ṣoḍaśapūrveṇa saṃkrānterapareṇa ca ॥* (l.4)
vartamānatulāmeṣanāḍyastūbhayato daśa ।
bhaviṣyatyayane viṣṇau vartamāne tathā viṣau ॥
ṣaḍaśītimukhe caiva *hyatīte cottarāyaṇe ।* (l.5)
rāhudarśanasaṅkrāntivivāhātyayavṛddhiṣu ॥
snānadānādikaṃ kāryaṃ nidhikāmyavrateṣu ca ।
devotsavaṃ ca tatraiva pūjāṃ *kuryācchivasya ca ॥* (l.6)
yāryāḥ sahitā nāḍyastāstā puṇyatamā smṛtāḥ ।

	atha saṅkrāntisamaye'pi vūrvavat snapanādipurassaraṃ devamabhiṣicya mūlaliṅgādīn sarvā(l.7)laṅkārairalaṅkṛtya । sarvavādyato samanvitaṃ vināyakasomāskandādibhaktaberasahitaṃ grāmaṃ vā puramālayaṃ vā pradakṣiṇaṃ nītvā । brahmasthāne (p.49b, f.46a, pakṣotsavavidhiḥ, l.1) vṛṣāgre vā pūrvava....rātmanādi kṛtvā svasthāne niveśayediti ।

evaṃ yatkriyate rāṣṭre māsadoṣā na vidyate ।
tasmāt sarvaprayatnena utsavaṃ samyagācaret ॥ iti (l.2) māsotsavavidhiḥ ॥

atha pakṣotsavavidhiḥ –
aṣṭame'śeṃ caturdaśyāṃ kṣīṇo bhavati canadramāḥ ।
amāvāsyāṣṭame'mśe vā punaḥ kila bhaviveduḥ ॥
dṛṣṭacandrāsinivāli naṣṭacandrārka *hu? matā ।* (l.3)
madhyāhnādyā tvamāvāsyā parastāt saṃpravartate ॥
bhūtasiddhā tu sāndreyā nāsā pañcadaśi bhavet ।
ghāṭikaikāpyamāvāsya pratipat *sunavet tadā ॥* (l.4)
bhūtavisiddhā vikartavyā daivapitrye'pi karmaṇī ।
pratipatsvapyamāvāsyā pūrvāhṇavyāpini padi ॥
bhūtaviddheva sākāryā daive *karmaṇi sarvadā ।* (l.5)
tulāmakaramīneṣu kanyāyāṃ mithune tathā ॥
bhūtavirdhāpyamāvāsyā pūjyā bhavatu yatnataḥ ।
caturdaśyātra saṃpūrṇaṃ nandādyākṣayagāmini ॥ (l.6)
devotsavaṃ tu bhūtāyāṃ māyāṃ tīrthamācaret ।

amā vai somavāreṇa saptamī caturthī bhaumavāreṇa viṣuvat sadṛśaṃ phalam । amā vai soma(p.50a, l.1)vāre cecchivarātrisamā bhavet । rautrau jāgaraṇaṃ kuryāddevotsavamathācaret ।

amāvāsyaṃ yādārdrāyāṃ yuktā cet somavāsare ।
koṭiyajñatapo *dānaphalaṃ prāpnoti ta?vratāt ॥* (l.2)

athāmāvāsyāyāṃ mūlaliṅgādyā(dyāḥ) sthāpano sā(sthā)panaberāṇāṃ pañcagavyapurassaraṃ mahābhiṣekaṃ kārayitvā । sarvā(l.3)laṅkārairalaṅkṛtya । mahāhaviṃ nivedya । grāmakheṭādike balibhramaṇaṃ vinā chatracāmarādisaṃyuktamanekavicitradīpikāsahitaṃ nānāvādyasahitaṃ devaṃ (l.4) pradakṣiṇaṃ nītvā । brahmasthāne samānīya ।

pūrvavannīrājanādi kṛtvā svasthāne niveśayet ।
evaṃ yaḥ kārayenmartyaḥ sapuṇyāṃ gatimāpnuyāt ॥ (l.5)
putrārthi labhate putrān kanyārthī kannikāṃ śubhām ।
dhanārthī vipulānarthān mokṣārthī mokṣamāpnuyāt ॥
na kālavarṣiparjanyo nārogā nirupadravaḥ ।
amāvāsyotsavenaiva sarvānkāmānavāpnuyāt ॥ ityamāvāsyotsavavidhiḥ ॥

atha paurṇamāsavidhiḥ – (p.50b, f.47a, l.1)
…(rā)jā cānumatī caiva paurṇamāsī dvidhā matā ।
yā pūrvā paurṇamāsī ca sānumatiryotyuttarā saha ॥
pakṣāntā pañcadaśyākhyā paurṇamā*sīti sā smṛtā ।* (l.2)
ṣoḍaśe'hanyabhīṣṭeti madhyā pañcadaśe'hani ॥
caturdaśe jaghanyeti pāpā saptadaśe'hani ।
pratipat bhratavaḥ sarvā iti yādodayadraveḥ ॥ (l.3)
saṃpūrṇā iti vikhyātā devakarmaṇi pāvani ।
yugmāni yamabhūtānāṃ ṣaṇmunyorvasurandhrayoḥ ॥
rudreṇa dvādaśī yuktā caturdaśyā ca *pārṇamā ।* (l.4)
bhūtavidhāpyamāvāsyā tithyoryugmaṃ mahatphalam ॥

	tatrāpi pūrvavadvighneśapūśādipurassaraṃ naivedyāntaṃ karmaṃ(rma) kṛtvā (l.5) vighneśādidevān sarvālaṅkārasaṃyuktān purādipradakṣiṇaṃ nītvā । pūrvavat svasthāne samāropayediti samastavidhivicchidradoṣo (l.6) naśyecchivājñayā ॥ iti paurṇamāsotsavavidhiḥ ॥

atha ṛkṣotsavavidhiḥ –
atha sarveṣu ṛkṣeṣu pūjanaṃ syācchivapriyam ।
tatphalaṃ *tu samāsena pravakṣyāmi haradvijāḥ ॥* (p.51a, l.1)
vāramekaguṇaṃ proktaṃ tithirdaśaguṇaṃ bhavet ।
tasmāddaśaguṇaṃ yogaṃ tasmātkaraṇamucyate ॥

tasmāddaśaguṇaṃ (l.2) lagnaṃ tasmādrakṣarma(llakṣaṃ ma)hat dhamuk bhavādviti nāḍikā । tatraivopavasedrakṣaṃ snānaṃ prajā ca kṛtakṛt । yadvārdhayāmādarpanakṣatraṃ prāpya । na kṣamate dine । (l.3) upaṃ ca pūjā ca snānaṃ dānaṃ ca tatra tu ।

ekamāse dvirikṣe cet pūrvaṃ gṛhyaṃ nacottare ।
uttaraṃ yogayuktaṃ cedviṣuvaṃ vāyanaṃ tathā ।
saṃkrāntigrahaṇaṃ *vāpi haripādayutaṃ bhavet ।* (l.4)
paraṃ grāhyaṃ muniśreṣṭhā devakarmaṇi sotsave ॥
rukṣapradhānasāyāhne vyāpike tīrthamācaret । (l.5)
kṛttikāsu śivaṃ pūjya dhanadhānyādikairyutam ॥
iha saukhyaṃ paraṃ hutvā ante sāyujyameva ca ।
apamṛtyuvināśārthaṃ rohiṇyāṃ *śaṅkaraṃ yajet ॥* (l.6)
………mye ceśvaramabhyarcyai mahābalaparākramaiḥ ।
ojasvi brahmavarcasvi sa bhavet sādhakottamāḥ ॥
ārdrāyāṃ sarva*sampattiṃ labhate harapūjakāḥ ।* (l.7)
punarvasubhavaṃ pūjyāḥ śatrūnhanti sapattakaiḥ ॥
dehapuṣṭikaraṃ puṣye śleṣāyāṃ ca varān sutān । (p.51b, f.48a, l.1)
labhate tatra deveśa pūjakastu bahusutān ।
mayā suśive miṣṭvā tu svajanaśreṣṭhavān bhavet ॥
pañcādipañcakaiḥ yuktasaubhāgyaṃ pūrvaphalgune ।
dhanadhānyasuratnādibahudāna*pradottare ॥* (l.2)
haste tu hara......lnadanaśreṣṭhatā vrajet ।
rūpaguṇāśvāryayutāṃ paduniṃ harārcakaḥ ॥
cittāyāṃ labhate sadya svātirikṣatu pūjakaḥ ।
bahu lābhena *saṃyuktaḥ vāṇijya nama hayanam ॥* (l.3)
labdhā tu suciraṃ kālaṃ sukhena saha vartate ।
viśākhe śivamiṣṭvā tu bahūn kāmānavāpyate ॥
kurvatāṃ cānurādhāyāṃ dadyudvakramauttaram ।
jyeṣṭhāyāṃ bhavamiṣṭaṃ tu dhaniṣkārdhaṃ labherdhuvam ।
mūlakṣe śivamabhyarcya dehārogyaṃ bhavetsudhīḥ ॥
pūrvāṣāḍhe mahā*devapūjayā prāpyate yaśaḥ ।* (l.5)
uttarāṣāḍa(ḍha)ke śambhuṃ pūjakarmaḥ sakṛdbhavet ॥
śravaṇe harapūjā cet śubhāṃ kanyāṃ labhettarām ।
dhaniṣṭhāsu śivārcyaṃ te labhe *puṣkalaṃ mahat ॥* (l.6)

labdhā sukharāṃ ca hutvā dānaśīlo bhavet abhijidrakṣake devaṃ prakosakalārthavit ।

ṣaḍaṅgena samāyuktaṃ vedaṃ ca labhate svaram ।
vāruṇe *śivapūjāyāṃ bhiṣaksiddhiṃ ca bheṣajam ॥* (l.7)
proṣṭhapadyā ṛkṣe tu śivaṃ saṃpūjayedyadi ।
ajo gāvaśca bahavo labhate nātra saṃśayaḥ ॥
uttare *proṣṭhapadye cā śivamārādhayat sudhi ।* (p.52a, l.1)
sarvarogavinirmukti(ktiḥ) bhavedīśājñayā dvijāḥ ॥
puruṣāṇāṃ ca sarveṣāṃ strīṇāṃ rājñamāhatau ।
sārvakālaṃ mahatkopaṃ *vinaśyecchaṅkarārcanāt ।* (l.2)
aśvinyāṃ pūjako yastu gajānaśvānlabhennaraḥ ॥
bharaṇyāṃ śivamārādhye dīrghamāyuśca vindati ।
tasmāt sarvaprayatnena ṛkṣeṣveteṣu pūjaja(na)m । (l.3)
svayaṃ kartumaśaktaścet anyainyaiḥ śaṅkarapūjanam ॥
kārayitvā tu matitān tatphalaṃ labhate t(?) phalam ।
tattadṛkṣe sthire śambhupūjanaṃ cotsavaṃ nayeta ॥ (l.4)
tat koṭikoṭiguṇitamārdrāyāṃ sopavāsakaḥ ।
pūjākāraitā vāpi putrān pautrān labhate vratāt ॥
samayiputrake vāpi sādhako vāpyadīkṣitāḥ । (l.5)
nācaredyastu śarvakṣaṃ sa vāpi narakaṃ vrajet ॥
brāhmaṇā kṣatriyā baiśyā śūdrāśca dvijasattamaḥ ।
bandhukāḥ kanyā vā vidhavā sumatā vā yativratiḥ ॥ (l.6)
sarvamāseṣu sarvakṣaṃ pratirikṣaṃ samācaret ।
snānaṃ sāyāhnakāle tu kṛtvā sandhyāmupāsya ca ॥
śivapūjāṃ tataḥ kṛtvā tataḥ svāyambhuvādike । (p.52b, f.46a, l.1)
pūjāṃ cotsavaparyantaṃ snānayitvā yathāvidhi ॥

devaṃ pradakṣiṇīkṛtya stutvā stotraparaiḥ śivaṃ tu praṇamya bahudhā bhaktyā svagṛhaṃ prāpya bhojayet ।

mārgaśīrṣake *māse tu rudrākṣe śivasannidhau ।* (l.2)
saṅkalpaṃ tu tataḥ kṛtvā varṣamekaṃ caret gri(gṛ)hi ।
yaṃ kāmayate kāmaṃ tattadāpnoti sādhakaḥ ॥
vatsarānte tu mahimā*nudyāpanamathācaret ।* (l.3)
varitvā brāhmaṇebhyaścā(śca) śaktito dakṣiṇāṃ dadet ॥
hayamedhasahasahyā yajñakoṭiśatasya ca ।
koṭikanyāpradānasyā(sya) *phalaṃ prāpnoti ta pradhāt? ।* (l.4)
utpātā tasya naśyantiśatrupīḍāśca dāruṇāḥ ॥
sarvavyādhivinirmuktaḥ putrapautradhanairyutaḥ ।
iha hutvā mahā*bhogā cirāyuḥ sukhamodhate ॥* (l.5)
idaṃ nasyaṃ paramaṃ vratānāmuttamottamam ।
aṣṭamyāṃ caturdaśyāṃ pradoṣe somavāsare ॥
vyatīpāte harārcāyāṃ *yatphalaṃ labhate naraḥ ।* (l.6)
tatphalaṃ kena labhate teṣāṃ phalamanuttamam ॥
brahmaghno vā suro vāpi goghnaśca gurutalpakaḥ ।
mahāpāpasamūhebhyo *mucyate tadvratottamān ॥* (l.7)
śivabhaktāya śāntā yā śrotriyā yā kuṭumbine ।
daridrāya mahāvyādhiyutāya pramarvratam ॥
iti parameśvarapara(p.53a, prāyaścittavidhi, l.1)nāmadheyaśrīmadaghoraśivācāryaviracitāyāṃ kra(kri)yākramadyotikāyāṃ bṛhattālādiṛkṣotsavāntavidhiḥ samāptaḥ ॥ śrī gurubhyo namaḥ ॥

atha prāyaścittavidhiḥ – (l.2)
prāyopaveśanātyuktaccittaṃ sandhānamucyate ।
punarṣṭa?sya sandhānaṃ prāyaścittamiti smṛtam ॥
svasyācārasya lopena mānuṣo narakaṃ vrajet ।
