luptācārasya śuddhyarthaṃ *prāyaścittaṃ videdguruḥ ॥* (l.3)
atyantarogayukte'ṅge nṛpataskarabhītiṣu ।
gu(ku)rvanti devakṛtyeṣu nityahānau na pāpabhāk ॥
brahmacārigṛhasthaśca prāyaścittadvayo*datim ।* (l.4)
sakāmāntastau ca dvividhau parikīrtitau ॥
akāmāttu sakāmasya niṣkṛti dviguṇoditā ।
bhautikā dviguṇā sa syānneṣṭike brahmacāriṇi ॥
triguṇāpi vanasthasya *yatīnāṃ tu caturguṇā ।* (l.5)
aśītivatsarādūrdhvaṃ prāyai ṣoḍaśavatsarāt ॥
tasya vṛddhasya bālasya prāyaścittārthamādiśet ।
strīṇāṃ ca varmāthitānāṃ* ca prāyaścittārthamīritam ॥* (l.6)
svayaṃ kartumaśaktasya dviguṇāṃ cānyatastu yat ।
aśaktasya sutabhrātā patnyādyaiḥ pitṛbāndhavaiḥ ॥
vibhajyoktaṃ prakartavyaṃ *prāyaścittaṃ tu santadā? ।* (l.7)
snapanasya vidhiṃ vakṣye śivasiddhāntacoditam ॥
trividhaṃ tatsamuddiṣṭaṃ nityanaimityakāmyakam ।
ekaikaṃ trividhaṃ paroktaṃ *kanyasaṃ madhyamottamam ॥* (l.8)
pañcabhirnavahīnaṃ pañcaviṃśatikumbhakaiḥ ।
madhyamekonapañcāśadekāśītighaṭaṃ tathā ॥
aṣṭottaraśataṃ śreṣṭhaṃ dviśataṃ *ṣoḍaśādhikam ।* (p.53b, f.50a, l.1)
aṣṭottarasahasrārdhaṃ sahasraṃ navadhocyate ॥
nitye naimittike kāmye kramāntatritayaṃ matam ।
tapaḥ kṛcchrādayaḥ proktokṛcchrānyastaptasaṃjñakaḥ ॥
atikṛcchraḥ *parākau dvau cāndrāyaṇavidhistataḥ ।* (l.2)
trirātramekabhuktisyāttridinaṃ laṅghanaṃ bhavet ॥
evaṃ kṛcchraḥ samākhyāta prājāpatya iti smṛtaḥ ।
pibet soṣṇaṃ triyahaṃ vāri *tridinaṃ kṣīrabhojanam ॥* (l.3)
soṣṇamājyaṃ tryahaṃ cādyatridinaṃ kevalo yadā ।
taptakṛcchramiti khyātaṃ tviti kṛcchraśca kathyate ॥
ekaviṃśatidinaṃ tatra triphalakṣīra*bhojanam ।* (l.4)
atikṛcchrasamākhyātaḥ parāka iha kathyate ॥
dvādaśāhaṃ nirāhārāḥ parāka iti saṃsmṛtaḥ ।
māsārdha...sa vṛddhyācchukle kṛṣṇe ca *hrāsayet ॥* (l.5)
cāndrāyaṇavidhiprokto jalenaiva jalātmikā ।

	pūrvavanmaṇṭape śubhāśayāṃ vā tanmadhye hāstikaṃ vṛttakuṇḍamāpādya śāntyāṃ paścime vā (l.6) ghaṭacatuṣṭayā vardhanīcatuṣṭayaṃ vā dhānyapuñje saṃsthāpya vighneśapūjāpurassaraṃ puṇyāhaṃ vācayitvā । ekasmin pūrvakumbhe śivāstraṃ (l.7) vakṣyamāṇadhyānapurassaraṃ saṃpūjya । kasmin dakṣiṇe kumbhe pūrvoktavadaghoradhyānapurassaraṃ saṃpūjya । yadvā – aghorāstraṃ vakṣyamāṇavat (p.54a, l.1) saṃpūjyai(jya) paścime ghaṭe pāśupadāstram । vakṣyamāṇavat saṃpūjyottare ghaṭe pratyaṅgirāmevaṃ saṃpūjayet । kāmikādiśivajñānaṃ tattaddiṅmukha(l.2)jātaṃ pañcadikṣu dīkṣitā asaṅkhyaṃ paṭheyuḥ । prācyādidikṣu tatpuruṣādimantrā na? saṅkhyaṃ japeyuḥ । ṛgādyadhyayanaṃ śāntipaṃ taṃ ca pratiṣṭhāmaṇṭapo(l.3)ktapadasaṅkhyaṃ japeyuḥ । etān krameṇa japāntaṃ saṃpūjya । tadanantaraṃ madhyakuṇḍe kuṇḍasamskārādi-caruhavanaprāyaścittāntaṃ karma pūrvavat kṛtvā । (l.4) kramādekaikāṃ mūrtiṃ saṃpūjya juhuyāt । yadvā – kuṇḍacatuṣṭaye mūrtipaiḥ saha tattaddevā(vānā)vāhya juhuyuḥ । mulu(mūla?)trayamadhuratrayatiladūrvā(l.5)kṣatādipāyasaṃ kṛsaraṃ vā kuśatrayaphalatrayasarṣapaṃ śataṃ sahasramardhaṃ vā pratyahaṃ sāyaṃ prātaḥ pūrṇāsahitaṃ juhuyuḥ । svāhānte bhagavan (l.6) sarvakṣobhataṃ ...padaṃ punaḥ kurakurviti saṃyuktaṃ pratimantradaśāntaramiti bahudivasapakṣe śivaṃ tatraiva nirudhya । devasamīpaṃ gatvā (l.7) somakarma nivedya । rakṣāṃ kṛtvā । duritakṣayanāśanaṃ prārthayet । ekatripañcasaptāhanarāhāśādvisaptabhaiḥ । pakṣati-rviśvanakṣatrai(p.54b, f.51a, śāntihomam, l.1)ravicchinnāyatastata iti śānti ॥

atha śāntihomaḥ –
	pūrvavanmaṇṭape sthaṇḍile sarvadhānyopari sarvālaṅkārasaṃyuktamekaṃ kumbhaṃ niveśya । parito'ṣṭaghaṭān (l.2) saṃsthāpya । madhyakumbhe – sthirāsanaṃ saṃpūjya । pāśupatāstraṃ dhyāyet ।

praṇavāsanamārūḍaṃ(ḍhaṃ) durādharṣaṃ mahālayam ।
pañcāsyaṃ daśakarṇaṃ ca prativaktraṃ trilocanam ॥ (l.3)
damṣṭrākarālamugraṃ muttanādaṃ sudurjayam ।
kapālamālābharaṇaṃ candrārdhakṛtaśekharam ॥
kekarākṣaṃ mahānāgābharaṇaṃ lelihāhasam । (l.4)
sūryakoṭyayutābhaṃ ca vighnasaṃghārimardanam ॥
bhujairdaśabhiratyugrairbhairavāstraiḥ samanvitam ।
khaḍgacāpamahāśūlābhayapāśāṃśca *dakṣiṇe ॥* (l.5)
kheṭabāṇakapālāṃśca varaprāsau ca vāmake ।
pihābha?śmaśrūbhrūyuktaṃ tathāvidhaśiroruham ॥
mahāpāśupataṃ devaṃ dhyāyet sarvārthasiddhitam । (l.6)

	iti dhyātvā । oṃ ślīṃkārasyāyuktaṃ paṃ śuṃ huṃ phaṇṇama(maḥ) svāhetyāvāhanādibhogāṅgapūjāntaṃ nirvṛtya । pūrvādikalaśeṣu vajrādīn (l.7) pūrvavat saṃpūjya । vividhanaivedyādikaṃ datvā । japaṃ samarpya । kuṇḍāntikaṃ gatvopaviśya । kuṇḍasamskārādicaruhavanaprāyaścittāntaṃ nivartyā(rtya) । tanmadhye – (p.55a, diśāhomam, l.1) astrarājaṃ pūrvavat saṃpūjya । samidājyatilasattupūrvamadhutraya-madhuratrayaphalatrayacarubhiḥ śataṃ sahasramardhaṃ vā śatamaṣṭādhikaṃ vā tattaddoṣāpanuttaye tanmantreṇa pūrṇāntaṃ hutvā । (l.2) prāyaścittāntaṃ pūrṇāvasānāṃ hutvā । dhūpadīpau datvā । kumbhe samāropyāgniṃ visṛjya । tat kumbhasthāpanāni yai(yaiḥ) jelamabhiṣiñcet । bahudivasapakṣe śāntyuktadina(l.3)saṅkhyayā kāmikādipaṭhanaṃ śāntipaṭhanādikaṃ ca pūrvavat kārayitvā । brāhmaṇān bhojayet । iti śāntihomavidhiḥ ॥

diśāhomādvi(mavi)dhiḥ -
	prāyaścittāṅgamanyat snapanāṅgama(mi)ti (l.4) pūrvottaramaṇṭapamadhye vedikāyāṃ liṅgaberaghaṭādikaṃ saṃsthāpya । tadagre śivāstramekasminkumbhe parito'ṣṭaghaṭān saṃsthāpya । sarvaśobhānvitaṃ madhya(l.5)kumbhe śivāstramabhyarcayet । pūrvavadāsanaṃ kalpayitvā ।

caturvakraṃ(ktraṃ) jvalatkeśaṃ vidyutpuñjasamaprabham ।
candramaṇḍitamūrdhānaṃ taccatuṣkarapallavam ॥ (l.6)
ardha....hnisaṃyuktama..........bhīmavikramam ।
dvādaśākṣiyutaṃ śaktiśūlābhayavarānvitam ॥
bhūtapretapiśācāni duṣṭarākṣasamardakam ।
raktanetraṃ mahāraudragnaumi *divyaṃ śivāstrakam ॥* (l.7)

	iti dhyātvā । sarvopacāraiḥ saṃpūjya । kuṇḍe sthaṇḍile vā saṃpādya । tasmin caruhavanaprāyaścittāntaṃ santarpyābhilāṣitaṃ prārthanā...pa(marpa)yet । (p.55b, f.52a, l.1) yadvā – madhye śivakumbhavardhanīṃ prāgdiśi vāstram । paścime pāśupatam । dakṣiṇe aghorāstram । saumye pratyaṅgirāmityāhuḥ । oṃ hrīṃkāraṃ tataḥ kṛṣṇa(l.2)vāsase ceti ya(e)kapadam ।

tataśca sihmavadane mahāvadana ityapi ।
mahābhairavivarṇāśca sarvaśabdaṃ tadanyataḥ ॥
karmavidhvaṃsanītyevaṃ paramantrapadaṃ tataḥ ।
ce(che)dinītyuddharet paścāt *sarvabhūtadamanyapi ॥* (l.3)
sarvabhūtāṃ tato bandhabandheti padamuddharet ।
sarvavighnetipadaṃ cindhicindhīti yatpadam ॥
sarvavyādhinikṛnteti nikṛntetipadaṃ ca yat ।
sarvaṣṭāntata?to *bhakṣabhakṣeti padamuttare ॥* (l.4)
jvālājihve karāletivattadaṃṣṭre padaṃ punaḥ ।
pratyaṅgire padaṃ paścādonnamo'stviti yatpadam ॥
svāhetyuddhare *doṣamantraḥ pratyaṅgirātmakaḥ ।* (l.5)
sarvaśobhānvitavardhanīmadhye parameśvarīṃ pratyaṅgirāṃ sthirāsane saṃpūjya ।
dhyāyet –
	sihmavaktrogradaṃṣṭāgni sa prabhordhva*śikhānvitām ।* (l.6)
	kṛṣṇāñjananibhā vṛttaraktanetratrayānvitām ॥
	vahatīṃ savyahastābhyāṃ śūlaṃ ḍamarukaṃ param ।
	vahatīṃ savyahastābhyāṃ muṇḍaśīrṣo'tha pallavam ॥ (l.7)
	kṛṣṇāmbaradharāṃ sāsramāsā syānnaktaneratām ।
	mauktikābharaṇe yogyai sarvāvayavabhūṣaṇaḥ ॥
	aṭṭahāsa ratopetāṃ kṛṣṇājinasamanvitām । (p.56a, l.1)
	rogavighnārisaṃhartri dhyāyet pratyaṅgirāmaham ॥

	iti dhyātvā । tanmūlenāvāhanādisarvopacārairvajrādyāvaraṇakalaśapūjāntaiḥ saṃpūjya । padmakuṇḍamadhye yonikuṇḍe (l.2) vā pratyaṅgirāṃ saṃpūjya । pakṣādisamidgavyatiladūrvākṣīracarumadhutraya-mūlu(la)trayakāśatrayagaurasarṣapairlavaṅgajātipatraśrīgandhakuṅkumagorocanā(l.3)dyaiḥ śataṃ śataṃ pañcāśadaṣṭāviṃśatirvā pratyekametairjuhuyāt । prāyaścittapūrṇāsahitaṃ datvā । devīṃ tadduritopaśāntiṃ prārthayitvā । deveṣu yajamāne (l.4) vā tatkumbhasthapānīyaiḥ saṃsnāpya । bhasmarakṣāṃ kārayediti । yadvā – pūrvavadastracatuṣṭayaṃ madhye pratyaṅgirāṃ ca yajerityāhuriti diśāhomaḥ ॥

saṃhitāhomam – (l.5)
	nityāgnau viśeṣāgnau vā tanmadhye sabrahmāṅgaṃ śivaṃ sāsanaṃ saṃpūjya । taddoṣāpanuttaye samidādidravyaiḥ sabrahmāṅgamantraiḥ śataṃ sahasra(l.6)mardhaṃ vā hutvā । prāyaścittādikaṃ kuryāt । ghaṭavarjitamiti saṃhitāhomaḥ ।

atha mūrtihomavidhiḥ –
	harmyāgre anyatra vā sthaṇḍilādikṛte maṇṭapamadhye śiva(p.56b, f.53a, l.1)kumbhaṃ vardhanīmaṣṭadikṣu vajrādisaṃjītānaṣṭakumbhān sasūtrān sāvi(pi)dhānān vastrahiraṇyaratnauṣadhigandho-dakenāpūritān saṃsthāpya । pūrvavanmaṇṭapaśuddhyādi kārayitvā । (l.2) madhyame śivamiṣṭvā । vardhanyāmaghorāstraṃ yajet । athavā madhye śivakumbhavardhanīṃ saṃsthāpya । tasyāmaghorāstraṃ yajet । tatparito vajrādīn (l.3) yajet । astrāsanāya huṃ phaṇṇamaḥ । astramūrtaye huṃ phaṇṇamaḥ । ityabhyarcya ।
dhyāyet –
sūryasya sahasrābhaṃ pralayāmbudanisvanam ।
pradīptadaśanabhrānta*prakāśamukhamaṇḍaram ॥* (l.4)
tryakṣaṃ lalanā jihvā dīptabhrūśmaśrumūrdhvajam ।
sarpopavītaṃ śūlāsiśaktimudgaradhāriṇam ॥
caturbhujaṃ caturvaktraṃ *śaraccandrārdhaśekharam ।* (l.5)
nṛttamānaṃ mahākāyaṃ nāgābharaṇabhūṣitam ॥
devadānavadaityānāṃ darpitānāṃ vimardanam ।

	iti dhyātvā । oṃ purasphura iti padaṃ (l.6) prasphura dvitayena ca । tanurūpa iti padaṃ caṭa caṭeti padottaram । praca dvitayaṃ paścāt kahakahetipadottaram । vama vameti ca padaṃ bandheti dvitīyam । (p.57a, l.1) paraṃ ghātaya dvitīyaṃ paścādaghorāstra itīritam । huṃphaḍacvaṃ svāhāntaṃ vātha mantraṃ japtvā samarcayedityāvāhanādijapāntaṃ saṃpūjya । kuṇḍe sthaṇḍile vāstrarāja(l.2)māvāhya samidājyatilakṣīradūrvāmadhuratrayahaviṣyānnaiḥ śataṃ sahasramardhaṃ vā śatamaṣṭottaraṃ vā pratyekamaghorāstreṇa taddoṣanivṛttyarthaṃ pūrṇāsahitaṃ juhuyāt । (l.3) athavā madhye śivakumbhaṃ vardhanīṃ cāṣṭadikṣu śarvādīnaṣṭapakṣe । pakṣaṃ ca pakṣe tu । pṛthivyādimūrtīn saṃsthāpya । pūjayet । tataḥ prāyaścittāntaṃ hutvā । (l.4) pālāśodumbarāśvatthavaṭaśamikhādiramāyūraplakṣādyairaṣṭottaraśataṃ pañcāśatsaṅkhyākaṃ vā pṛthak prayuktamantreṇa hutvā । pūrṇāṃ datvā । liṅgaṃ (l.5) vā yajamānaṃ vā tattoyairabhiṣicya । tadbhasmadhārayitvā śāntyuktadinasaṅkhyayā sarvaṃ samācaret । atrāpyadhyayanaśāntipaṭhitān (l.6) kāmikādi-ta...pāṭhakastotrāśca yajamānaḥ ācāryapurassaraṃ gandhapuṣpavastrādyairalaṅkṛtya yathoktadakṣiṇāṃ datvā । namaskṛtya । duritopaśāntiṃ prārthayet । (l.7) mātāpitaiḥ sārdhamācāryo āślakṣatāṃ dadyāt । dīnāndhakṛpaṇānāthān bhojanācchādanaiḥ santoṣayediti mūrtihomaḥ ॥

sparśam -
svajātyādīkṣitaṃ (p.57b, f.54a, sparśam, l.1) spṛṣṭvā sacelasnānamācaret । śūdrasya sparśane sadyaḥ sacelasnānamācaret । sarveṣāmantarālānāṃ sparśane snānakadvayam । caṇḍāladarśane kāle sadāśivamanu(l.2)smaret । tasya pā...... tu saṃśra(śru)tvā mūlamantraṃ śataṃ japet । daṇḍamātrasamīpe cet kartavyaṃ snānakadvayam । pramādā(dāt) sparśane vidyājjalasnānaṃ tu pūrvataḥ । bhasmasnānaṃ tataḥ (l.3) kṛtvā śivamantraṃ śataṃ japet । buddhyā saṃsparśane cettu dviguṇaṃ pūrvamācaret । taṃ dṛṣṭvā bhojane kāle taccheṣānnaṃ tyajedbudhaḥ । snānadvayaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā । (l.4) japtvā mūlaśatāṣṭakam । durganadhaṃ vāyunā gacchet nāsāgre yadi vartate । gomayaṃ tu samāghrāya । sadyaḥ pañcākṣaraṃ japet । svajātyadīkṣitānnaṃ ca bhuktvā grāme (l.5) nave gṛhe । vyomavyāpiṃ japedekaṃ yadi kāmaḥ śataṃ japet । nṛpānnabhojane vipro ghoramantraśataṃ japet । bhuktvā vaiśyānna kasyāpi sahasrāntaṃ japet (l.6) kramāt । bhuktvā ........ kāmāttu śūdrānnaṃ kra(kṣi)praṃ cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret । cāndrāyaṇaṃ ca kāmādvai kṛpra caivātikṛcchrakam । bhuktvā vai kāhamūrdhvaṃ tu kṛcchraṃ cāndrāyaṇaṃ (l.7) caret । māsaṃ nirantaraṃ bhuktvā śūdratvamupayujyate । śūdreṇaiva tu saṃpṛkto nityaṃ śūdravadācaret । śūdrastrīsaṅgamaṃ kṛtvā (p.58a, l.1) taptakṛcchraṃ tataścaret । taptakṛcchrādikṛcchraṃ ca māsaṃ māsordhvagāminaḥ । ṣāṇmāsaṃ vatsaraṃ cāpi kṛcchraṃ cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret । abdādupari saṃyoge prāyaścittaṃ na vidyate । (l.2) tasyācārasya tadra......maraṇāntaṃ viśeṣataḥ । eraṇḍārkavaṭāśvatthapatreṣu yadabhojayet । kāmādvā kāmato vāpi śivamantraṃ śataṃ japet । ucchiṣṭe ghṛtasaṃyukte (l.3) kṣīre lavaṇavarjite । japtvā ca hṛdayaṃ tasya sarvadoṣaniśrantanam । keśāsthidarśane'nne(hne) cennavakīṭādidarśane । alpamannaṃ parityajya hastaṃ prakṣālya bhasmanā । spṛṣṭvā (l.4) smṛtvā tu .....veśajalaṃ prāśya viṣuddhyati । laśunaṃ matsyamāṃsaṃ ca nirmālyaṃ ca visarjayet । pramādāt bhakṣayitvā tu śivamantraṃ japecchucau । kāmena yadi (l.5) bhuñjīta śivamantraṃ śataṃ japet । brahmahatyā surāpānaṃ suvarṇasteyameva ca । bhuvraṃganāgamaścaiva tadyogāt pañcamo bhavet । gurutalpaṃ (l.6) surāpāne brahmahatyā ca te trayaḥ । maraṇānte ime proktāsteṣāṃ tu maraṇaṃ śṛṇu ॥ rūpyaṃ harati yo lobhādaindavaṃ vratamācaret ॥ (p.58b, f.55a, svarṇā....raṇam, l.1) taptakṛchraṃ tataścaret ।

taptakṛcchrātikṛcchraṃ ca māsaṃ māsordhvagāminaḥ ।
ṣāṇmāsaṃ vatsaraṃ cāpi kṛcchraṃ cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret ॥
abdādupari saṃyoge prāyaścittaṃ na vidyate । (l.2)
tasyācārasya ta.....maraṇāntaṃ viśeṣataḥ ॥
eraṇḍārkavaṭāśvatthapatreṣu yadanto jayet ।
kāmādvā kāmato vāpi śivamantraṃ śataṃ japet ॥
ucchiṣṭe ghṛtasaṃyukte *kṣīre lavaṇavarjite ।* (l.3)
japtvā ca hṛdayaṃ tasya sarvadoṣanikṛntanam ॥
keśāsthidarśane nne? cennakhakīṭādidarśane ।
alpamannaṃ parityajya hastaṃ prakṣālya bhasmanā ॥
spṛṣṭvā smṛtvā *tu deveśa jalaṃ prāśya viśuddhyatī ।* (l.4)
laśunaṃ matsyamāṃsaṃ ca nirmālyaṃ ca visarjayet ॥
pramādāt bhakṣayitvā tu śivamantraṃ japecchucau ।
kāmena yadi *bhuñjīta śivamantraṃ śataṃ japet ॥* (l.5)
brahmahatyā surāpānaṃ suvarṇasteyameva ca ।
...vṛṅganāgamaścaiva tadyogāt pañcamo bhavet ॥ (l.6)
gurutalpaṃ surāpānaṃ brahmahatyā ca te trayaḥ ।
maraṇānte ime proktāsteṣāṃ tu maraṇaṃ śṛṇu ॥
rūpyaṃ harati yo lobhāddaivaṃ vratamācaret । (p.58b, f.55a, svarṇādaharaṇam?, l.1)
gadyāṇadaśakādyadhvamārjatā(tāt) dviguṇaṃ caret ।
āsahasrāttu dviguṇamūrdhvahemavidhi smṛtam ॥
śreṣṭharatnāpa(bha)raṇe svarṇasteyavidhiṃ carettu ।
sarveṣāṃ dhātulohānāṃ *parākaṃ tu samācaret ॥* (l.2)
dhanyānāṃ haraṇe kṛcchriṃ tilānāṃ mandapaṃ smṛtam ।
nīcaratnāpaharaṇe kṛcchriṃ ghoroyutadvayam ॥
vastraśayyāsanādīnāṃ steye *gāyatriyā yutam ।* (l.3)
gurutalpasamatvevaṃ lakṣārdhaṃ caindavaṃ caret ॥
govadho vrātyatāstainyastrīṇānāṃ cayanakra(kri)yā ।
atāhitāgnitāpasya *vikrayaṃ parivindanam ॥* (l.4)
bhutakāryāpakādānaṃ bhūtakāryāpakaṃ tathā ।
paradyāryaparāvittā vāddhyuṣya lavaṇakriyāḥ ॥
strīmṛgakṣatriyavadho *ninditā copajīvinam ।* (l.5)
nāstikyaṃ vratalopaśca svasutānāṃ ca vikrayaḥ ॥
dhānyakrayaṃ paśusteyamayā(thā)jyānāṃ ca yājanam ।
pitṛmātṛsuhṛtyāgastaṭākārāma*vikrayaḥ ॥* (l.6)
kannikādūṣaṇaṃ caiva parivindakayājanam ।
kanyāpradānaṃ tasyaiva kauṭilyaṃ vratalopanam ॥
ādhanavikriyārambho madyapa*strīniṣevaṇam ।* (p.59a, l.1)
svādhyāyāgnisutatyāgo bāndhavatyāga eva ca ॥
indhanārthaṃ himacchedastrīhiṃsauṣadhajīvanam ।
hiṃsā mantravidhānaṃ ca vyasanānātmavikrayaḥ ॥
asacchāstrābhi*gamanamākareṣvabhikāritā ।* (l.2)
bhāryāvikrayaṇaṃ caivamekaikamupapātakam ॥
upapātakaśuddhi(ddhiḥ) syādaindave vipratena ca ।
payasā vāpi māsena varākeṇāpi (l.3) vā bhavet ॥
aghoralakṣato vāpi viśeṣastvatha kīrtyate ।
brahmahatyā caturthāṃśaṃ dvijakṣatravadhe caret ॥
caredvaigya?vadhe'ṣṭāṃśaṃ ṣoḍaśāṃśaṃ ādrahā । (l.4)
...nimittaṃ tathā vā(ghā)te prāṇināṃ tu śataṃ japet ॥
prāṇihiṃsātmakāt pāpācchatāghorajapācchuciḥ ।
anasthiprāṇasaṃghāte svedaje vā *.....satim ॥* (l.5)
ajātasya śataṃ japyamakāmāṃ tu tadardhataḥ ।
vanyapuṣpādijānpūtvā na sthini vahanyapi ॥
dṛṣṭāni sūkṣmāṇi śivasya smaraṇācchuciḥ । (l.6)
ākhumājā(rjā)rakavadhe śaśa tenaiva śucirbhavet ॥
śvaśṛgālavadhe caiva dviguṇaṃ kapi kukkuṭe ।
sarvānyaṇḍajakhāte tu hamsādisakunīṣvapi ॥
siṃhādimṛga*ghāte tu ajādipaśughātane ।* (l.7)
śataṃ ca śatāntaṃ tu bahurūpajapācchuciḥ ॥
dhenuṃ pramādato hatvā ghoralakṣajapācchuciḥ ।
yoṣit bālavadhaṃ *kṛtvā ghoralakṣau śuddhyati ॥* (p.59b, f.56a, mṛgādiba(va)dhai, l.1)
nāvādikasya chitvā tu kāmāllaṃ japedbudhaḥ ।
kāmato garbhapātacche(ścet) tri(tṛ)tīyasyāyutaṃ japet ॥
śunā daṣṭo'pi śuddhyeta gāyatryāṣṭa*śatena ca ।* (l.2)
mārjāramūṣikairdaṣṭaḥ śataṃ japtena śuddhyati ॥
sṛgālavṛṣadaṣṭo vā śuddhatyaṣṭaśatena tu ।
kumbhirajakarairdaṣṭaḥ sakṛdeva viśuddhyati ॥
pramādāt *sarpadaṣṭaścejjapet ghorasahasrakam ।* (l.3)
kāmāt paścādibhirdaṣṭo japet ghorāyutaṃ jale ॥
viṣavidyāṃ ca kuryuścedindrajālaṃ calācalam । (l.4)
bhūtagrahapiśācādivicchede tu śatadvayam ॥
na māraṇaṃ tu manasā manunā vāpi kārayet ।
uccāṭanādikaṃ kṛtvā bahurūpāyutācchuciḥ ॥ (l.5)
parebhyo du(duḥ)khamutpādya pāraṅmanaḥ kāyakarmabhiḥ ।
pramādataḥ parijñāya śivamantraṃ śataṃ japet ॥
karmaṇā manasā vācāpyakṛtaṃ *yo'bhibhāṣite ।* (l.6)
vāmadevaśataṃ japtvā tasmānmuñceta kilbiṣāt ॥
śatamuktaṃ parā niṣṭā(ṣṭhā)dvāgdoṣā dviguṇaṃ japet ।
parastrīdravyasaṅkalpaścetā *sāniṣṭacintanam ॥* (l.7)
akāryābhiniveśaṃ ca caturthārkamānasam ।
aharniśaṃ pralāpitvamasatyaṃ cāpriyaṃ ca yat ॥
parāpavādaśūnyaṃ ca caturdhā *karmavācikam ।* (p.60a, l.1)
abhakṣyabhakṣaṇaṃ hiṃsāmīdhyākāmasya sevanam ॥
paraspānāmṛdānaṃ caturdhā karmakādhikam ।
vācike syātryahaṃ maunamagnikāryācanaṃ japam ॥ (l.2)
samāpte gurudevāya(yā)dīnāṃ vācaṃ nivedayet ।
sa mukto vācikairbandhaiḥ karmabandhaiśca kathyate ॥
nirāhāraṃ trirātraṃ ca trisandhyaṃ jalagāhanam । (l.3)
brahmajāpi ca maunī ca karmabandhairnamucyate ॥
mānasasya tu pāpasya prāṇāyāmaśate kṛte ।
śivagāyatrimantreṇa śatānmutacenmatamānasā ॥ (l.4)
ajaptvā śivāyutamantrī sarvadoṣāpanuttaye ।
pañcagavyaṃ sakṛtpītvā sarvapāpai(paiḥ) pramucyate ॥
mātrā hi pitṛsamukabhūtaṃ sahajā catukaṃ ca yat । (l.5)
asthimajjāgataṃ pāpaṃ pañcagavyo dahet kṣaṇāt ॥
akāmataprasuptasya śuklādesyadane sati ।
snātvā punaḥ sacelastu astramantraṃ *śataṃ japet ॥* (l.6)
divāpi syandane svapte jātena dviguṇaṃ caret ।
saṅgamya yoṣitaṃ kāmāt praṇavasya mahātmanaḥ ॥
prāṇavṛttinirodhena *lakṣamāvartayedvratiḥ ।* (p.60b, f.57a, l.1)
tapasvī viṣayāsatto vratoddhārapurassaram ॥
strīparigrahaṇaṃ kuryādanyathā patanaṃ dhṛ(dhru)vam ।
jaṭākaceṣu yā saṅkhyā dehe *vā bhasmareṇavaḥ ॥* (l.2)
tāvatkālaṃ mahāghore raurave nivase kri(kṛ)tiḥ ।
yat sakṛdyoṣitaṃ gacchet kāmāndha punarūhayet ॥
vāmadevasahasreṇa *śuddhatyeva na saṃśayaḥ ।* (l.3)
balādutsṛjya śuklaṃ tu divā vāmacatuśśatāt ॥
rātrau cedviśataṃ jāpyaṃ divā suptaḥ śataṃ japet ।
paśukrīḍāṃ *yadā paśyedakāmāt sa śataṃ japet ॥* (l.4)
ṛtusnātā yadā patni tadā pañcadināvadhi ।
sevyāpatnyā ca putrānadhamanyathā bhrūmahā bhavet ॥ (l.5)
na tastavyahva niṣkrānto japahomādikaṃ caret ।
satataṃ yaścaredvipro maithunaṃ kāmamohitaḥ ॥
sa vipraḥ kharavat jñeyastasya *mantrānmukhāḥ ।* (l.6)
kṣatryādau na ca doṣo'sti divā parvaṇi doṣabhāk ॥
karmaṇā manasā vācā na kāryo pāradārikim ।
....ddhirlakṣārdhapādena *yadi mohāt samācaret ॥* (p.61a, l.1)
kanyāyāṃ dviguṇaṃ kāryaṃ tathā varṇāntareṣu ca ।
parvasandhiṣu vāṣṭamyāṃ kadācinmaithunaṃ dvī ॥
kṛtvāyutaṃ japetprājñuḥ praṇava..ya vrate *sthitaḥ ।* (l.2)
rātrau kṛtvāyutārdhaṃ tu sandhyāyāmayutaṃ japet ॥
kathā maithunajāṃ(jñāṃ) śrutvā hṛdbījasya śataṃ japet ।
brāhmaṇimitarāṃ vāpi yo'bhigaccheddīkṣitaḥ ॥
vipraḥ *sāṣṭaśataṃ japtvā tasmāt pāpāt pramucyate ।* (l.3)
kṣatriyo dviśatenaiva vaiśyastu triśatena ca ॥
śūdraścatuḥ śataṃ japtvā vāmadevaṃ śivāgrajaḥ ।
ekarātroṣito *bhūtvā tasmāt pāpāt pramucyate ॥* (l.4)
bhuktvā yadi ciraṃ kṛcchraṃ kuryādvāmaśatena te ।
mohādgatvā vivarṇāṃ vā tadbhavāṃ kāmacāriṇīm ॥
dināni *trīṇyanudvigno nābhidaghnodake sthitaḥ ।* (l.5)
prāṇāyāmena japtavyo guhyapāpādvimuddhyati ॥
udakyāṃ vā sakṛdgatvā upavāsamakaṃ mataḥ ।
guhyapāpe *na kartavyaṃ kāmādvitryahameva tu ॥* (l.6)
caṇḍālastrī yadā jñānājandire sā vyavasthitā ।
mṛṇmayāni tyajet sadya(dyaḥ) snātvā saṃśodhya tadgṛham ॥
naraṃ pañcasahasrāṇi *japtvā kṛcchraṃ samācaret ।* (l.7)
bhuktvā sāyādyakāmena taptakṛcchrapurassaram ॥
vāmadevāyutaṃ japtvā pañcagavyena śuddhyati ।
kāmataḥ sā caru *bhuktā cet kṛtvā kṛcchrātikṛcchrake ॥* (p.61b, f.58a, l.1)
vāmadevaṃ japettadvat parākānte vrataṃ matam ।
jñātvā bhuktvā ciraṃ kālaṃ tadāsau tatsamo bhavet ॥
rajjaṃ karmakāriṃ *vā spṛṣṭvā vā mandire sthitām ।* (l.2)
bhāṇḍatyāgādajācchuddhistryahaṃ pañcasahasrakam ॥
akāmato yadā spṛṣṭvā bhuktvāghoraṃ japettryaham ।
kāmāccedviguṇaṃ hyetat *sakṛdyogāt samācaret ॥* (l.3)
akāmāt bahusaṃyogāḥ sañjātastadvayorapi ।
tataścāndrāyaṇaṃ kāryamanyathā vai(nai)va śuddhyati ॥
bahudaiva*siko yogaḥ sañjātaḥ kāmato yadi ।* (l.4)
tadā cāndrāyaṇaṃ kāryaṃ vāriṇā kevalena tu ॥
satpratigrahaṃ gṛhyaḥ(hya) sadyo dvādaśabhiḥ punaḥ ।
mucyate *......kṣipraṃ dīkṣitaścāpyakutsitaḥ ॥* (l.5)
sarpistailaṃ śarkarāṃ ca gṛhītvā vāmamahyaset ।
lavaṇaṃ guḍatitikṣṇāti gandhāni vividhāni ca ॥
kṣīraṃ dadhi ghṛtaṃ tailaṃ *madhu māṃsāni dhānyakam ।* (l.6)
paṭṭavastraṃ ca kārpāsaṃ maunaṃ kṣaumaṃ ca mauñjikam ॥
carma pratigṛhaṃ gatvā bhavedghoraśatatrayam ।
hemādilohasaṃghātaṃ *vajrādyaṃ .....sañcayam ॥* (l.7)
haritālādidhātṛni parākadanyasañcayam ।
vṛṣabhaṃ hastinaṃ yānaṃ kṣetraṃ madhyantaraṃ tathā ॥
ālayādiṣu yuktena bahu*rūpāyutācchuciḥ ।* (p.62a, l.1)
kāṣṭho(ṣṭhe) phalatrarādīnāṃ haraṇācchataśaḥ śuciḥ ॥
anaḍvāhaṃ hayaṃ strīṃ ca sahasruvā śataṃ naraḥ ।
bahusasyayutāṃ bhūmīṃ gṛhye śasya sahasrakam ॥ (l.2)
gṛhītvā vastu yatkiñcit bahurūpaṃ japedbudhaḥ ।
vastraśayyāsanādīnāṃ steye gāyatryā śatam ॥
vidyāpaharaṇe vakraṃ sahasraṃ prajapedbudhaḥ ।
svavṛttiṃ yaḥ *parityajya anyathā yo'nuvartate ॥* (l.3)
dīkṣitaḥ karmaṇa(ṇaḥ) svasmāt patitaḥ punarāśataḥ ।
ghoraṃ satataṃ japtvā māsamekaṃ śivāgrataḥ ॥
hutvāyutaṃ śivāgnau tu tasmāt *pāpāt prakasyate ।* (l.4)
daśadhā vartayet ghoraṃ tricatuṣvatha lagne(laṅgane) ॥
pāpānāmapyanekeṣāṃ saṅkare samupasthite ।
oghoramantraṃ japtavyo *yāvaccetaḥ prasīdati ॥* (l.5)
ahanyahani yo'dhīte vidhisthaśivasaṃhitām ।
sahasti sarvapāpāni tāmisramiva bhāskaraḥ ॥
pūrvāparāhṇasandhyāyāṃ *sadyojātaśataṃ japet ।* (6)
ahorātrakṛtaṃ pāpaṃ ta(tat)kṣaṇānte vinaśyati ॥
yadā puṣpavatī nārī na saṃśuddhā yajecchivam ।
prathame'hani caṇḍālī dvitīye *brahmaghātakī ॥* (p.62b, f.59a, l.1)
tṛtīye rajakī proktā caturthe snānataśśuciḥ ।
snānaṃ mṛttikayā kṛtvā gavyaṃ prāśya yajecchivam ॥
alābhe ghoramantrya(ntra)śataṃ japtvā yajecchivam । (l.2)
antaryāgo'thavā dhyānaṃ kāryaṃ pūrvadinatraye ॥
rajodivā cedādyāhaḥ tṛtīyāṅgyaṃ vinā niśi ।
tṛtīyāṃśe yadi prātarādyāhariha iṣyate ॥ (l.3)
avijñāte rajasrāve vamavattat striyā gṛhe ।
spṛṣṭaṃ yadvastu tadadṛṣṭaṃ na dṛṣṭaṃ sannikarṣataḥ ॥
caṇḍālasūtikodakyā śavataddāhasūtakaṃ ca । (l.4)
spṛṣṭabandhumṛtiṃ śṛtvā nadyādā snānakālataḥ ॥
abhiṣicyāthavā bhuktvā prājāpatyaṃ samācaret ।
brāhmaṇī śūdraviṭkṣatryastrī rundatyā spṛśedyadi ॥ (l.5)
kṛcchraṃ pādonamardhaṃ vā kramāt kṛtvā viśuddhyati ।
asnātvā yadi bhuñjīta vrataṃ cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret ॥
śūdrādyā kramaśo dānaṃ pādakṛcchropavāsakaiḥ । (l.6)
viṭśūdre kṣatryā spṛṣṭvā śuddhye tatpadārthakṛcchrataḥ ॥
vaiśyākṣatriyaśūdrastrīsparśane pādakṛcchrataḥ ।
śūdrāpurvaṇa saṃsṛśyā *dānenaiva viśuddhyati ॥* (p.63a, l.1)
rajasvalā ......nā mohāt spṛṣṭvā śavādibhiḥ ।
tryahe paryavete tu kṛcchraṃ cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret ॥
rajasvalābhi(bhiḥ) bhuñjānā divā kīrtiṃ yadaspṛśet । (l.2)
upavāsatrayaṃ kuryāt pañcagavyena śuddhyati ।
sārta vā yadi caṇḍālamucciṣṭā sā praviśyati ॥
āsnānādupavāsa(saḥ) syāt bhuktau cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret ।
sagotre rajasvale spṛṣṭe *tadha(ta)ścopavase(set) tryaham ॥* (l.3)
udkyodakyayā spṛṣṭvā snātvopoṣya viśuddhyati ।
saṃbhāṣaṇe tathā śuddhe tu bhuktvā dṛṣṭvā rajasvalām ॥
āsnānakālāt nānīyāt pañcagavyaṃ *pibettataḥ ।* (l.4)
śave vā sūtake vāpi vartamāne rajo yadi ॥
sā snātvā bhojanaṃ kuryāt ...tvā copavasedahaḥ ।
śavāśauce udakyā ca sūtakaṃ vā *bhavedyadi ॥* (l.5)
madhye'bhiṣicya bhuñjīta spṛṣṭvocchiṣṭena kṛcchratam ।
rajasvalā mṛte sūte bhuktvā pītvā tathātrataḥ ॥
aprāyujya samurmapanne malavadvisasa *striyaḥ ।* (l.6)
abhiṣekeṇa śuddhi syādanyathā nāśanaṃ tryaham ॥
snānairmṛttike toyai(yaiḥ) snāyāt samalamuddhṛte ।
snāyāt prātaścaturthehni ṣaṇṇāḍi*vigame varam ॥* (l.7)
ūṣotsvedanakhaṇḍādyairvāsaḥ śuddhiralepitā ।
triḥ sapta kṛtvā bhasmādbhibhuktau kāṃsyenāśuciḥ ॥
śayanaṃ kṣālayedbhistat *kṣaṇādyairalepanam ।* (p.63b, sparśam, l.1)
yaddeśe svāpabhuktatadgovirśakrāntyā viśuddhyati ॥
ṛtusnātā balānmohān nānyān patye patiṃ vinā ।
yatra bhūmau tṛṇaṃ nāsti jalaṃ codyatyayā saha ॥ (l.2)
govā(pā)lavyajanādarvāk snānaṃ tatra vidhīyate ।
gopālavyajanaṃ daṇḍo yadvā puruṣamānataḥ ॥
triṇapārṇavighāteṣu dravyenāntaritena vā ।
gopālaṃ vyajane vāpi *snānaṃ tatra na vidyate ॥* (l.3)
karmamadhye tu tāṃ dṛṣṭvā gṛhasthapraṇavaṃ japet ।
ādityadarśanaṃ paścāt prāṇāyāme kṛte pratiḥ ॥
anye'pi darśanaṃ kṛtvā mūlamantraṃ śataṃ japet । (l.4)
ekavṛkṣasamārūḍo caṇḍālo dharajasvalā ॥
ekarātroṣitā bhūtvā pañcagavyena śudhyati ।
ādvādaśāhānnārīṇā mūtravacchā ca mīpyate? ॥ (l.5)
aṣṭādaśāho va snānaṃ syāttrirātraṃ parato śuciḥ ।
rajasvalā tu yā nārī punareva jaṣvasvalā ॥
sā viṃśatidinādūrdhvaṃ trirātramaśucirbhavet ।
snāyāt saṃghuṣya dantādimalaṃ mṛttikayā *muhuḥ ॥* (l.7)
pañcagavyaṃ ghṛtaṃ vāpi prāśya pūjayecchivam ।
antaryāgaḥ prakartavyaṃ svayaṃ pūrvadinatraye ॥
yadvānte ghoramantrasya sahasraṃ parivartayet ।
svajātyudatyā *saṃspṛṣṭā kāmādyādiriva yajet ॥* (l.7)
svajātiśvaramantrasya sahasraṃ pūjayettadā ।
ajānato ca netasyāstaṅgājjāyate śuciḥ ॥
japeda*ṣṭottaraśataṃ sandehe samupasthite ।* (p.64a, l.1)
hīnajātīyasaṃsparśe kramādvitricaturguṇam ॥
utkṛṣṭajātisaṃsparśe hīnastasyārdhamācaret ।
sūtakābhyantare śaktyakartavyadhyānamaiśvaram ॥
tadantai(nte) *pañcagavyāni yajecchivamatandritaḥ ।* (l.2)
nāraṃ spṛṣṭvāśthisaṃsnehaṃ snātvā ghoraśataṃ japet ॥
asnehasparśane snāyādakaṃ vācamya darśayet ।

darka vācamya darśayetu(ttu) śavaspṛśyamṛta(l.3)nīhāni śvamānapretasūtakaiḥ ।

vāhakairdāhakai(kaiḥ) sparśe snāyāchaivānyaliṅgibhiḥ ।
pāñcarātrakālarātrai(traiḥ) sparśe kāpālikaulikaiḥ ॥
bakāśe(śaiḥ) *somabhṛkabhiśchabauddhanagnāsitāṃ paraiḥ ।* (l.4)
kṛtvā snānaṃ sacelastu tri(tṛ)tīyasya śatācchuciḥ ॥
caturthasparśane vipraḥ sacelasnānamācaret । (l.5)
antarālāni sarvāṇi spṛṣṭvā snānadvayaṃ kuru ॥
kāmārapañcaṣaṭkasāmīpye snānaṃ kuryādyugādayaḥ ।
cā(vā)cālabhāṇarajakasāmīpye vā dvidaṇḍataḥ ॥ (l.6)
sārdhadaṇḍādaparaṃ snāyāt sāmīpye sūtakāstriyaḥ ।
kaṅkagadhṛvaraścāntyavarṇasaṃsparśane tathā ॥
tadaiva kṛtaśmaśānāṅgagrāmasṛkarakukkuṭaiḥ । (p.64b, l.1)
nimālyagadbhiḥ patitai(taiḥ) sparśane snānamācaret ॥
gomāyukṛkalāsaiśca divābhītakuraṇḍakaiḥ ।
śvapākena ca saṃsparśe vāruṇaṃ snāna*mācaret ॥* (l.2)
divākīrtiṃ citiṃ yūpaṃ niṣādaṃ sūtikāṃ tathā ।
vikarmasthaṃ dvijaṃ spṛṣṭvā lokāyatikanāstikān ॥
caityapra(vṛ)kṣaṃ tathā spṛṣṭvā sacelo *jalamāviśet ।* (l.3)
ekavakṣastathārūḍhaścaṇḍālādiryadā bhavet ॥
brāhmaṇastatra nivasat snānena śucitāmiyāt ।
śuklaviṇmūtrasaṃsparśe nābherūrdhvaṃ *karaucinā? ॥* (l.4)
vapane vamane spṛśyā sparśane snānamācaret ।
ūrdhvaṃ nābheryāni kāni tāni medhyāni sarvataḥ ॥
dhānyadhastamyamedhānidehāccaiva *malādinā ।* (l.5)
amedhyopagate dehe mṛdbhiḥ śuddhyati saptabhiḥ ॥
uttamāṅgopaghāte ca vāriṇā snānamācaret ।
śvāprabhṛtiṃ ca viṭślepo*tkākādīnāṃ ca pakṣiṇām ॥* (l.6)
mṛdā kṛtvā kṛtaśaucamadhamāṅge viśuddhyati ।
uttamāṅge tu sarvatra snānameva tu vāribhiḥ ॥
prāyaścittaṃ japaṃ kuryāt *gāyatryāṣṭaśataṃ budhaḥ ।* (p.65a, l.1)
ucchiṣṭasya ca ...llepāt kṛtaḥ śauce yathāvidhi ॥
snātvā tu śuddhimāyāti kra(kri)yā daśaguṇācchatāt ।
anṛte mūtrakaraṇe sacelasnānamācaret ॥ (l.2)
śuklaviṇmūtra....praṭbhiḥ saṃspṛśyaṃ mukhamaṇḍalam ।
mṛttikā gomayairlepāt pañcagavyena śudhyati ॥
mānuṣāsthivasāviṣṭhāmārtā vā mūtraretasi ।
majjānaṃ śoṇitaṃ spṛṣṭvā *parya.....snānamācaret ॥* (l.3)
udakyā hyudakaṃ spṛṣṭvā snānaṃ kṛtvā viśuddhyati ।
ucchiṣṭastena saṃspṛṣṭa(ṣṭaṃ) snānenaiva viśuddhyati ॥
divākarakarairyuktaṃ divāsnānaṃ praśasyate । (l.4)
na praśastaṃ tu tadrātrau rāhoranyatra darśanāt ॥
yadvā gurvādivākyena rātrau ca snānamācaret ।
bhasmasnānaṃ pra(vra)tiḥ kāryaṃ mantrasnānaṃ sadā kṛtī ॥ (l.5)
aśuci spṛśedanye ekavarṇe sa dūṣyati ।
tatspṛṣṭo'nyo na dūṣye tu sarvocchiṣṭeṣvayaṃ vidhiḥ ॥
devayātrā vivāheṣu yajñeṣu *prakṛteṣu ca ।* (l.6)
utsaveṣu ca sarveṣu spṛṣṭvā spṛṣṭaṃ na dūṣyati ॥
tīrthe vivāhe yātrāyāṃ saṃgrāme deśavibhrame ।
nagaragrāmadāhe ca spṛṣṭvā spṛṣṭaṃ na dūṣyati ॥ (p.65b, l.1)
nocchiṣṭasya bhaveddoṣastvoṣṭhayośca vilomake ।
tāmbūle ca ...le caiva bhuktasyāpi viśiṣṭake ॥
dantalagnasya saṃspṛśe nocchiṣṭasya bha.......raḥ । (l.3)
adbhiḥ patrai(traiḥ) mūlapuṣpaistri(stṛ)ṇakāṣṭhamadhaistathā ॥
sugandhibhistathādravyairnocchiṣṭo bhavati dvijāḥ ।
snātvā pītvā kṣute (l.4) .......pte bhuktau rathyopasarpaṇe ॥
ācāntapunarācāme dvisopi paridhāya ca ।
kṛtvā mūtraṃ purīṣaṃ ca hyanācāntaṃ paya(yaḥ) spṛśet ॥
amedha....ni *skandhayānī saṃspṛśyācamya śuddhyati ।* (l.5)
kṣute niṣīvine cottā vakra tasya vacaśrutaḥ ॥
niṣīvane tathābhyaṅge bhuktvāpi paridhāya ca ।

ucchiṣṭānāṃ tu (l.6) saṃsparśet sparśe ceṣṭasya liṅgāde darśane cāntyavāsinām ॥

jihvāyā caiva saṃspṛśya dantasaktamupaspṛśet ।
uttīrayodakamācāme *devaniryattane caiva ॥* (p.66a, l.1)
strīśūdro'mbaṣṭhasaṃbhāve purīṣotsargadarśane ।
devatārādhane .....ścedācamyottaramācamet ॥
dantavaddantasarteṣu yacchāpyantargataṃ *mukhe ।* (l.2)
khe...vāntasyāvaśiṣṭaṃ ...bhirgaraṃ nācamettadā ॥
sandhyārdhe bhājanārdhe vā pitṛrthe ca tathaiva ca ।
na(nā)cāmecchūdrahastena japahomārcaneṣu ca ॥
svajātya(l.3)...kṣitaṃ spṛṣṭvā āgneyaṃ snānamācaret ।
agnisnehaganthalepo(pā)rthaṃ gomayenoṣṇavāriṇā ॥
hemaraupye ca śuddhi(ddhiḥ) syāllohe ghaṣa(rṣa)ṇatāpanaiḥ । (l.4)
uddhya...mena tāmrorthaṃ bhasmanā kāmsyabhājanam ॥
āmlena bhasmanā vākśuddhaṃ syān sarvadhātujāḥ ।
veṇukumbā(mbhā)dipātraṃ ca ........*vālyai sogaṣaṇaiḥ ॥* (l.5)
vāriṇā śuddhyamādhāti bhikṣāpātraṃ tu pārthivam ।
viśuddhyatyayasā spṛṣṭadāravā vāriśodhanāt ॥ (l.6)
śaṅkha....mauktikasya kṣālanānmathitena tu ।
godhūmasya cuṣairvihistuṣairvā ghaṣayettataḥ ॥
śuddhi kṣālanakartavyaṃ janabhāṇḍanā nityam । (l.7)
salilagharṣaṇāt taśaivodakabhāṇḍānāṃ paryagnikaraṇādapi ।
vāruṇātvamajātinā mathitenāyasena tu ॥
jalajā.....nna sarveṣā *vaiṇavānāṃ tavāmbhasā ।* (p.66b, f.63a, l.1)
śarāvapatrapuṭakaprabhṛtīnāṃ ca bhojane ॥
śuddhirātropayuktānāṃ parityāgo'bhidhīyate ।
viṇmūtra..........tyājyāste *veṇusambhavāḥ ॥* (l.2)
sarvadhā tu samutpañcavahnau spṛṣṭvā viśuddhyati ।
pātraṃ maṭākhumājārairāliḍaṃ tu viśuddhyati ॥
saptāstrakṣau nādadbhiḥ paryagni*karaṇāpi ।* (l.3)
kharoṣṭraśvādināghātamavaliḍantu santyajet ॥
gorūpā vrātapātraṃ ca saṃparityājyameva hi ।
nakalairavalīḍantu yatpātraṃ *ghṛtameva vā ॥* (l.4)
saptaśaścāpanirmuktamagnitā śuddhimeva hi ।
vāriṇādarpi pūrvāṇi śuddhimāyāti bhāṇḍavat ॥
prabhūtamagnaṃ kākādyai(dyaiḥ) juṣṭa*mātravarjitam ।* (l.5)
ucchiṣṭasya ca saṃsparśādantadonnaṃ nidhāya cā(ca) ॥
ācamyānnādikaṃ prokṣya sparśedastro'rkena tu ।
śniślānāṃ rūkṣaṇācchuddhistha*lepānāmalepanam ॥* (l.6)
gandhāpanayanācchuddhi durgandhānāmupā hṛdā ।
āsanaṃ śayanaṃ yānaśuddhimeti mṛdāmbhasā ॥
bhṛtyahastaṃ kāruhastaṃ *pa...pi trayamādarāt ।* (p.67a l.1)
aduṣṭaṇḍau tamadbhiśca śakuniśatāditaṃ phalam ॥
kṣmāgotrāntyā ca khananādagdhaṃ śuddhā ca lepitā ।
rathamāmārgataṃ śuddhaṃ pānacandrārkaraśmibhiḥ ॥ (l.2)
makṣikā kaiśasammiśraṃ pādāntopahataṃ ya yat ।
rajaḥ pipīlikāyuktaṃ bhuñjānontamavekṣitam ॥
tāvanmātraṃ samuddhṛtya prokṣayedambhasā tapaḥ ।
ājyā*bhighāraṇācchuddhirbhasmarkṣeṇaivā(va) bhavet ॥* (l.3)
pakvadroṇādhikaṃ jannaṃ kākaśvādyupaghātitam ।
grāsamuddhṛtya tanmātraṃ na tulālāghṛtaṃ? bhavet ॥ (l.4)
hemodakena ...rabhyu...ja tenāmbunāthavā ।
kākaśvāvalīḍhaṃ tu droṇānnyūnaṃ tu varjayet ॥
viprāṇāṃ brahmaghoṣeṇa pūtaṃ *bhojyaṃ ca tadbhavet ।* (l.5)
bahvante ca vivāheṣu yajñeṣu prakṛteṣu ca ॥
pratiṣṭhādiṣu kāleṣu dīkṣotsavadineṣvapi । (l.6)
kākaiśvabhiścāsaṃspṛṣṭamannaṃ tacchucitāmiyāt ॥
khananātpūraṇāddāhādapi gharṣaṇalepanāt ।
gobhirākramaṇāt kālāt *bhūmi śuddhyati saptabhiḥ ॥* (p.67b f.64a, l.1)
caṇḍālaśvapavaṃ caiva śivadūṣaka eva ca ।
annagrahopayuktaścettat prāk sṛjya mṛdambhasā ॥
mṛdbhāṇḍāni parityajya *gomayenopalepayet ।* (l.2)
brāhmaṇairdīkṣitaiścaiva hiraṇyakuśavāribhiḥ ॥
sarvamabhyukṣayedveśma tataḥ śuddhyeta saṃśayaḥ ।
saṃvāso yadi *................nnatāpau vinirdiśet ॥* (l.3)
ekarātroṣitā āpastyājyā medhyā apīsvadhīm ।
vāpīkūpataṭākeṣu dūṣiteṣvapi śodhanam ॥ (l.4)
asthicarmuśca khārādi....te vāyyuṣite'thavā ।
ghaṭānāṃ śatamuddhṛtya pañcagavyaṃ kṣipettataḥ ॥
jalāśayeṣvadhāśleṣu sthāpaye…ṣu *mahītale ।* (l.5)
caṇḍālādyaśucisparśe tīrthaṃ tatparivarjayet ॥
bālairanuparikrāntaṃ strībhirācaritaṃ ca yat ।
maśakairmakṣikābhiśca *saṃspṛṣṭaṃ nopahanyate ॥* (l.6)
arciṃ nāgraṃ ca yadvastrimahātalā tu ratnajam ।
brāhmaṇā vāsadho bhūmidevāgāraṃ tathaiva ca ॥
medhyaścaiva *sadā medhya gavāṃ goṣṭhaṃ ca yā bhavet ।* (p.68a, l.1)
sarvatra vasudhā medhyā yatra lopo na vidyate ॥
amedhyabhūtale vṛkṣapatrapuṣpaphalātmakaḥ ।
teṣāṃ naiva *pradūṣyanti patrapuṣpaphalāni ca ॥* (l.2)
naṭanṛttakatakṣāṇāṃ ca makāraḥ suvarṇakṛt ।
sthāṇukā ṣaṇḍagaṇikā abhogyā tāḥ prakīrtitāḥ ॥
bhāryālobhakā......*sūcikāstantuvāpikā ।* (l.3)
cakropajīvi rajakaḥ kitavastaskarastathā ॥
dhvaji manopajīvī ca śūdropādhyāyayācakau ।
kulālaścitrakamāci *vārdhūṣiścamuvikradhi ॥* (l.4)
parigrahe garbha(rbhi)ṇi yā jñātvācāret pratigṛham ।
vṛthā śramī vrathā dātā āśramāṇiṃ ca bhedikaḥ ॥
sagotre *pariṇeyaśca puṇyavikrayiśca yaḥ ।* (l.5)
yonisāṅkarikaścaiva śivadīkṣāṃ vihīnakaḥ ॥
raṅgopaji vikrūddhāśi virahā *guptakaś yaḥ ।* (l.6)
bhiṣak ca garadaścaiva rūpajīvi ca yācakaḥ ॥
śaunakaḥ pauṇikaścaiva dīkṣādau gratakaśca yaḥ ।
karmaśveteṣu yo mohāka *brāhmaṇo yaśca vartate ॥* (p.68b, f.65a, l.1)
prāyaścitte vicarite parihāryo bhavet sa hi ।
ete caiva hi caṇḍālāḥ sarve brāhmaṇaḥ kilā(la) ॥
tasmāt deve ca ...la..... *ca varjitāstattvadarśibhiḥ ।* (l.2)
pariprān bhūpathanakatapodhanagatāsukān ॥
jaṭī muṇḍī liṅgadharā gatāsūn śivadīkṣitān ।
etāṃśca kha(va)tanesthāmata liṅgaṃ vā *pratimādikam ॥* (l.3)
…vāsaṃ pūjayedyastu tena svāyambhuvādikam ।
spṛṣṭe ghorasahasreṇa vāyutaṃ pūjite sakṛt ॥
japtvā śāntiṃ tadā kṛtvā *hyātmano hitamiccatām ।* (l.4)
vīrabhadraṃ ca śāstāraṃ durgāṃ kāliṃ ca reṇukām ॥
sthāne śaṅkhaṃ caiva bhūteśaṃ gaṇānvīrāṃśca bhaktakān । (l.5)
svapradhānāni mānyo vai pūjayet sa tu ninditaḥ ॥
śūdreṇārādhitaṃ liṅgaṃ beraṃ vā bhitticatrakam ।
darśanaṃ vandanaṃ tatra manasāpi na kārayet ॥
śūdreṇa *pūjitaṃ ....mohādajñānato vāpi vā ।* (l.6)
śūdreṇaiva samaḥ so'pi jīvito nindito bhavet ।
anyaiḥ kāpālikādyairvā pūjitaṃ sthānamaiśvaram ॥ (l.7)
darśane vandane tacca mahādoṣaḥ prajāyate ।
kāṇaḥ paṅktvarbhakaścaiva badhiro pathidyagāyakau ॥
ātmārthapūjāhīnaścātha *śūdrastrisaṅgamastathā ।* (p.69a, l.1)
adīkṣitaścaturvedi na spṛśennāpi cāca(rca)yet ॥
na śaivadīkṣito vipro na vaikhānasasūtrakam ।
na pāñcarātrasambandhi parārthavittasāṃ ca yā ॥ (l.2)
śivakeśavayoḥ pājūṃ vatsaratrayamācaret ।
sa vai devalako nāma sarvakarmasu garhitaḥ ॥
śaṅkhaṃ cakraṃ ca liṅgaṃ ca yo mūḍho dhārayettanau ।
pāṣaṇḍa(ḍaḥ) sa *hi vijñeyaḥ sarvakarma bahiṣkṛtaḥ ॥* (l.3)
spṛśet ghorasahastre(sre)ṇa pūjite prokṣaṇaṃ nayet ।
kūnakhi śyāpadantaśca mahārogayuto'pi vā ॥
brahmacāri vipatniko madyamāṃsa*niṣevakaḥ ।* (l.4)
hīnādhikāṅgayukto vā kuṣṭhiśvitryarbhukastathā ॥
mahāpātakayukto vā vidhuro vā yatistathā ।
parārthaṃ pūjayetsadya(dyaḥ) rājarāṣṭraṃ *vinaśyati ॥* (l.5)
taddoṣaparihārārthaṃ śāntiyuk prokṣaṇaṃ caret ।
kṛṣṇāṣṭaṃyāṃ caturdaśyāmārdrāyāṃ māsi ca ॥ ?
upavāsavihīno yaḥ sa tu vai *nirjito bhavet ।* (l.6)
tastamudrādiyuktastu śvitrikuṣṭhita...vaca ॥
harārādhanakāle ca nagnādipratilomakaḥ ।
darśanaḥ bhāṣaṇaṃ nasya manasāpi na kārayet ॥ (l.7)

	raudrāgamapravṛttena pūjiṃ cet paramārthakasiddhāntajñāna(naṃ) kartavyam । kartavyaṃ cet sthirādikam । mṛdbhāṇḍāni parityajya । jalasaṃprokṣaṇaṃ nayet । (p.69b, f.66a, nityavidhihīnam, nityapūjā-hīnam, l.1)

āsane śayane snāne śivapūjādikarṇi ।
saha vā saṅkaroktestu prāyaścittirbhaveddhṛ(ddharu)vam ।
śivārcāṃ kārayedrātrau ekakālārcite yadi ॥
iṣṭaliṅgā*rcanaṃ homaṃ dānaṃ sandhyāṃ caret sadā ।* (l.2)
vinā somoparāgaṃ ca vinā siddhyādikāmyakam ॥
śaucamācamanaṃ snānaṃ bhasmarudrākṣadhāraṇam । (l.3)
prokṣaṇaṃ tvāghamāṣaṃ(marṣaṇaṃ) ca sandhyādhyānaṃ ca tarpaṇam ॥
karāṅganyāsakaṃ caiva mantrajāpyaṃ pradakṣiṇam ।
digdāvatānamaskāraṃ nityakarmamudāhṛtam ॥ (l.4)
jñānato'jñānato vāpi ekaikasya vilopane ।
ekavāraṃ japedghoraṃ tattatpāpaviśuddhaye ॥
rajanyāṃ śivamabhyarcya *bhogārthī cet pramādataḥ ।* (l.5)
rājacorabhayairbhuktastajjīrṇe snānataḥ śuciḥ ॥
ajīrṇe vamanaṃ kṛtvā snātvā ghorasahasrakam ।
japtvā snātvā ca *vidhivatpūjayediṣṭaliṅgakam ॥* (l.6)
sa japecchivamantraṃ ca śatamaṣṭādhikaṃ pratiḥ ।
mumukṣuḥ sarvadhā(dā) śambhoḥ pūjāṃ dhyānaṃ samācaret ॥ (p.70a, liṅgārcanam, l.1)
ekakālārcatā hīne japedghorasahasrakam ।
akāmataḥ sakāmataḥ dviguṇaṃ pūrvamīritam ॥
ekakālārcanaṃ ya sa .......trikālakam ।
tṛtīyaṃ vihitaṃ tasya prāyaścittaṃ *samaṃ tayoḥ ॥* (l.2)
dinadvitīye(ya)mārabhya aghoreṇa sahasrakam ।
triśatottarāvṛddhyā divasaṃ prativartayet ॥
pakṣaṃ pūjātra lope japet ghorayutatadvayam । (l.3)
vilope māsapūjāyāstryayutaṃ bahurūpiṇā ॥
tryayutottarayā vṛddhyā māsaṃ saṃvatsarāvadhi ।
tadūrdhvaṃ dīkṣayā śuddhiḥ śivapūjā vilopataḥ ॥
sveṣṭaliṅga*hṛditacoraimūṣikairvā narādibhiḥ ।* (l.4)
dagdhe bhinne'thavā chinne naṣṭe vā srotasā hṛte ॥
lakṣaṃ japedaghorasya sthāpayedanyaliṅgakam । (l.5)
tasya śuddhiriyaṃ proktā piṇḍikāyāstathā bhavet ॥
calācalāntāṃ berāṇāṃ tatpīṭhānāṃ tadeva hi ।
gaḍḍukādā.........*tācca svalpādayutaṃ jāpataḥ ॥* (l.6)
mahā...ghātā taddivaguṇamaghorasya viśuddhyati ।
liṅge tu lugite sveṣṭe japedghorasahasrakam ॥ (l.7)
dviguṇaṃ dviguṇe tasmin tri caturluṭhite yutam ।
lakṣaṃ śvabhrācchyute liṅge japecca bahurūpiṇā ॥
ādhāre patite liṅge tāle *tāle'yutaṃ japet ।* (p.70b, f.67a, pādādisparśanam, l.1)
dviguṇaṃ tu nirādhāre kāmākāmāttu pūrvavat ॥
nṛmānāt patitaṃ liṅgaṃ kāmāccet sarpa(rva)bhāvataḥ ।
bhavedyadāpi śīrṇaṃ tat prāyaścittaṃ *na vidyate ॥* (l.2)
bhede liṅgaṃ śalākāṃ vā pāpaṃ tasya mahadbhavet ।
lakṣatrayamaghorasya japaṃ kuryādviśuddhaye ॥
kāmācchīrṇaṃ yadā liṅgaṃ prāyaścittaṃ *tadā na hi ।* (l.3)
spṛṣṭe pādena liṅge cellaṅghite'pyayutadvayam ॥
pādasatnajalaṃ spṛṣṭe liṅge ghorasya cāyutam ।
liṅgasyā..... kuryāt *sarvatra snapanaṃ budhāḥ ॥* (l.4)
liṅge ca śleṣmaṇā spṛṣṭe śleyuktodakena ca ।
tryayutaṃ dvyayutaṃ tasya bahurūpeṇa kīrtitam ॥
liṅge *mūtrāmbhasā sikte sārdhaṃ tasyāyutadvayam ।* (l.5)
raktaṃ reto rajo mūtraidūṣite'yutapañcakam ॥
tatsaṃyuktāmbhasā spṛṣṭe liṅge tatpādahīnakam । (l.6)
liṅgaṃ tu viṣṭhayā spṛṣṭhe japellakṣamaghorataḥ ॥
pādahīnaṃ tadamvo? histadvannirmālyaga..... ।
.....ṣmāgarte nipatite liṅge lakṣaṃ ...hārdhakam ॥ (l.7)
adīkṣitairjanai(naiḥ) spṛṣṭhe liṅge ghoraśambaram ।
ekadvitriśataṃ tatra prāyaścittamudāhṛtam ॥
liṅge śūdreṇa saṃspṛṣṭe'ghoraṃ *pañcaśataṃ japet ।* (p.71a, ratnādisparśam, aṣṭabandhanahīnam, l.1)
kaivartakanaṭādyaiśca spṛṣṭe daśasahasrakam ॥
liṅge kākādibhisspṛṣṭe tvayutadvitayaṃ japet ।
vikṣiteśvapacairliṅge japedayutapañcakam ॥ (l.2)
taispṛṣṭe dviguṇaṃ liṅge prajapedghoraśambaram ।
vāmadakṣiṇamārgasthai(sthaiḥ) liṅge spṛṣṭe'yutaṃ japet ॥
pīṭhācca cālite liṅge nirdoṣaṃ tu *bhavedyati ।* (l.3)
tadeva sthāpayettatra japellakṣamaghorataḥ ॥
tyajet sa doṣaṃ talliṅgaṃ ca pacādyaiśca sevitam ।
liṅgāntaraṃ pratiṣṭhāpya viśuddhimadhi*gacchati ॥* (l.4)
nirvāṇe seśvare cāgnau trirātrmupavāsayuk ।
kriyā yāni yamasyāpi pradāne ca vilopane ॥
japedaṣṭaśataṃ teṣu śaivaṃ *gāyatriyā śatam ।* (l.5)
aṣṭabandhanavicchede liṅge pūjā bhavedyadi ॥
bindumātraṃ harerbhāge brahmaṇo bhāgake'pi vā ।
rudrabhāgodakaṃ prāpte mahādoṣaḥ prajāyate ॥
tatkāle caikakālaṃ vā dvikālaṃ vā trikālakam ।
pūjite niṣphalaṃ proktaṃ rājño rāṣṭrasya *dussthitiḥ ॥* (p.71b, f.68a, arpādipatanam, l.1)
kartuḥ kārayitā vāpi mahādoṣakaraṃ bhavet ।
tatkālake vā kartavyaṃ māsapakṣadinaṃ vinā ॥
jalasaṃprokṣaṇaṃ kṛtvā śāntihomamataḥparam । (l.2)
lakṣādhikamaghorasya japtvā bandhanamācaret ॥
saṃkrāntau ca caturdaśyāmaṣṭamyāṃ parvaṇorapi ।
harakṣai vilupte connityalokapā*ccaturguṇam ॥* (l.3)
āṣāḍā(ḍhā)diṣu māseṣu pavitrāropaṇe kṛte ।
japellakṣadvayāmaghoraṃ kuryāccāndrāyaṇaṃ tataḥ ॥
kṛtvā pavitrakaṃ karma taduktā*cāravarjite ।* (l.4)
cuturmāsyāntarāle tu grāmāntaragato'pi vā ॥
pavitrahīnaṃ tataḥ śuddhirvataṃ cāndrāyaṇaṃ vinā ।
damanāropaṇādye *cāakṛte tvayutaṃ japet ॥* (l.5)
arghyapātrādike pātre tvavisarjitaśambare ।
vivartite ca vigne(ghne) ca japedghoraśatadvayam ॥
āmrācchatāni pañcāpi sahasraṃ *tatra nirvate ।* (l.6)
śuddhi syādakṣamālāyā liṅgasyottardhapādataḥ ॥
chinne sūtre nave sūte(tre) japādghorasahasrakam ।
jīrṇasūtramavicchinne *ghoramantraśataṃ japet ॥* (p.72a, japamālāpatanam, upakaraṇapatanam, l.1)

	hastānnipatitāṃ bhūmau akṣamālāṃ sugrandhitāṃ prakṣālya । vāriṇābhyarcya । gāyatryāṣṭaśataṃ japet । aparodhe'tha ghaṇṭāyāliṅgoktāṣṭama(l.2)bhāgataḥ prāyaścittaṃ prakurvīta prajojakaraṇādike? ।

spṛṣṭo pādena cāghoraṃ japenmantraśatadvayam ।
sakāmādayutaṃ tatra bālādīnāṃ tu pādataḥ ॥
naivedye *keṭakīṭāstimāṃsamatyā(dyā)didūṣite ।* (l.3)
japecchatamaghorasya samakāmādayutaṃ tataḥ ॥
hīne naivedyamāne'tha nitye ghoraśatadvayam ।
da...prāṇyaṅgadhūpe *syāt sadyojātaṃ śataṃ matam ॥* (l.4)
pādena kramato liṅgaṃ guruṃ spṛṣṭvā śivāgnikam ।
aghora(raṃ) ca(cā)yutācchuddhiḥ kāmataḥ ṣaḍguṇaṃ tataḥ ॥ (l.5)
ācāryaśivacaṇḍānāmājñābhaṅge tu lakṣakam ।
hīnasya lakṣaṇe teṣāṃ pādonaṃ lakṣamīritam ॥
nirmālyabhakṣaṇe lakṣa(kṣaṃ) pādataḥ śu.....rīritā । (l.6)
dānaṃ ca bhakṣaṇasamaṃ tadardhaṃ na ....pekṣyaṇe ॥
akāmāt bhakṣaṇe yadvā nirmālyasya japecchuciḥ ।
brahmapañcakasāhasramardhena sahitaṃ tataḥ ॥ (l.7)
kāmato bhakṣaṇe dīkṣā prāyaścittaṃ na cānyataḥ ।
nirmālyalaṅghane ghoraṃ prajapedayutaṃ tataḥ ॥
sparśanaṃ laṅghanasammo vikrayo bhakṣaṇena ca । (p.72b, f.69a, naivedyaṣaḍvidhanirmālyam, l.1)

(naivedyaṣaḍvidhanirmālyam -)
nirmāyaṃ ṣaḍvidhaṃ tasyāddeva svarṇamayaṃ matam ।
devadravyaṃ ca naivedyaṃ niveditamataḥparam ॥
caṇḍadravyaṃ ca nirmālyaṃ teṣāṃ lakṣaṇamucyate ।
pura*kṣetrādidevasvaṃ taddravyaṃ hemavastrakam ॥* (l.2)
pakvaṃ gandhādinaivedyaṃ śivocchiṣṭaṃ niveditam ।
caṇḍasvaṃ caṇḍadattaṃ syānnirmālyaṃ mā(ā)rjitaṃ bahiḥ ॥
visa(ṣa)juṣṭe'pi *gaurīśe nirmālyaṃ na hi pīṭhakam ।* (l.3)
nirmālyasya pradvāna syāt kravyādo mānavādhamaḥ ॥
tamātaṅgo bhakṣaṇārtasya siddhibhraṃśaśca ......... ।
dānādvakastasya *caṇḍālo dāhato bhavet ॥* (l.4)
…baro vikraye tasya strītvaśca sparśatā bhavet ।
yat saṃspṛṣṭaṃ tu nirmālyairdravyaṃ yattadbhujāvapi ॥ (l.5)
pūrvoktaṃ tattripādonaṃ prāyaścittaṃ prakalpayet ।
garbhagehapraviṣṭaṃ yatpuṣpādyamanuṣaṅgataḥ ॥
caṇḍā(ḍāṃ)śaṃ sa bhaktasya nirmālyaṃ nātra saṃśayaḥ । (l.6)
pūjākāle ca dhūpānāṃ gandhapuṣpādikasya ca ॥
dīpaprabhādīnāṃ ca pravāho doṣakṛttarhi ।
pratiṣṭhāyāṃ nirmālyaṃ caturthīkarmaṇāvadhi ॥
annādya*mauṣadhaṃ puṣpaṃ toyaṃ patraphalādikaiḥ ।* (l.7)
śivāya datvā taccheṣaṃ bhyo(bho)jyaṃ bhuñjīta buddhimān ॥
adattaṃ ye'ṣṭamaṃ nādyaṃ yontuṃ ca kte śiva*dīkṣitaḥ ।* (p.73a, varjyāntam ucchiṣṭam, l.1)
mūlenāṣṭaśataṃ japtvā saṃpūjyā(ya) ca viśuddhyati ।
sveṣṭaliṅgasya yaddattaṃ caruvattaṃ na saṃśayaḥ ॥
tadbhuktvā śivapūjāyāḥ phalaṃ saṅgamavāpsyati । (l.2)
pratiṣṭhāsu ca devānāṃ taṭākārāmayostathā ॥
japetpañcaśatānyatra bhuktvānnaṃ pañcamena ca ।
svāyambhudaivike bāṇena nirmālyaṃ niveditam ॥ (l.3)
sakalānāṃ na nirmālyaṃ pīṭhānāṃ ca tathaiva ca ।
bhuktvā puṃsavane cānnaṃ vāmadevaśataṃ japet ॥
ekādaśe'hni yo bhuṅkte dvādaśādike'pi vā । (l.4)
lakṣalakṣatri(tra)yaṃ vāpi pūjayedghoramantrataḥ ॥
anumāsiśivaśrārdhe ghorasya triśataṃ japet ।
gaṇānnaṃ gaṇikānnaṃ ca annaṃ *sanyāsināṃ tathā ॥* (l.5)
bhuktvā ca yāpa(ca?)kasyānnaṃ careccāndrāyaṇaṃ vratam ।
pākādhvare vivāhe ca godāne mauñjibandhane ॥ (l.6)
caule tvācāraviprāṇāṃ bhuñjāno'nnaṃ na pāpabhāk ।
yasya varṇasya yo bhuṅkte hyannamāmantraṇaṃ vinā ॥
tajjāti(tī)śasvajātīśāvekīkṛtya śataṃ japet । (l.7)
ekaṃ dvi tri dināni syurupavāsaṃ caret kramāt ॥
puruṣāghoravāmājā viprādīnā*mathivarāḥ ।* (p.73b, f.70a, l.1)
īśānastvanyajātīnāmevaṃ proktaṃ śivadvijaḥ ॥
ajīrṇe tu japaṃ snānaṃ pūjāhomaṃ tyajedbudhaḥ ।
jīrṇojānne tataḥ snātvā gāyatryāṣṭa*śataṃ japet ॥* (l.2)
dīkṣitadīkṛtaṃ spṛṣṭvā samucciṣṭaṃ savarṇakam ।
svajātīśaśataṃ cāṣṭamāvartya pūjayedbudhaḥ ॥
tadevādīkṣita(taṃ) spṛṣṭvā japet snātvā śatadvayam । (l.3)
vipro vaiśyaṃ ca śūdraṃ ca samucciṣṭaṃ ca dīkṣitaḥ ॥
dvirātraṃ ca trirātraṃ ca dīkṣitaḥ samupoṣitaḥ ।
tajjātīśāvekīkṛtya japet .......kramāt ॥ (l.4)
napṛḥ śūdraṃ samucciṣṭaṃ spṛṣṭvā ca dārupoṣitaḥ ।
tajjātīśasvajātīśāvekīkṛtya japet kramāt ॥
adīkṣitāṃśca te spṛṣṭvā pūrvokta*dviguṇaṃ caret ।* (l.5)
nṛpastasmādahīnāṃśca vaiśyastvardhena śuddhyati ॥
caturthāṃśena śūdrastu śuddhimeti na saṃśayaḥ ।
prāśite cottamocchiṣṭe *svajātīśāyutaṃ japet ॥* (l.6)
hīnocchiṣṭāśane śreṣṭhastadeva dviguṇaṃ caret ।
sopavāsaḥ śucisnātaḥ pañcagavyāśane rataḥ ॥ (l.7)
brahmacāryastvadhaśśayyā sarvatraivaṃ vrataṃ smṛtam ।
sveṣṭaliṅgaṃ paribhṛṣṭaṃ mahāhṛdi sarāṃsi ca ॥
dabhyubhyo vānarādibhyo *gṛhītaṃ labhyate punaḥ ।* (p.74a, naṣṭaliṅgaṃ punaḥ svastham, l.1)
kālāntareṇa saṃprāpte tatkathaṃ pūjayedvijaḥ ॥
jalasaṃprokṣaṇaṃ kṛtvā śāntikarma samācaret ।
japtvā lakṣamaghoraṃ *ca mūlamantraṃ ca tatsamam ॥* (l.2)
liṅgadvaye'pi kurvīta pūjā nityamatandritaḥ ।
pūrvadattaṃ purābhyarcya paścāddattamataḥparam ॥
prāyaścittamitaṃ(daṃ) proktaṃ gṛhasthānāṃ *yathākramam ।* (l.3)
dviguṇaṃ brahmacārī ca vanasthastriguṇaṃ caret ॥
caturguṇaṃ ca sansāsī prāyaścittaṃ samācaret ।
dvārapālārcanā hīne vighna*bādhā mahadbhavet ॥* (l.4)
tadarthaṃ saṃhitāhomaṃ samāpya dvarapā nyaset ।
pañcaśuddhivihīnā yā pūjā sā tu kṛtā bhavet ॥ (l.5)
āsanakramahīne tu dusthititvamabhijāyate ।
daśasaṃskārahīne tu narāduccāriṇo bhavet ॥
athopacāravaikalye *kṣutpipāsādibhirnaraḥ ।* (l.6)
pīḍyante kramabhaṅge tu sāṅkaryaṃ bhuvi jāyate ॥
vidyādyā pratibhaṅge tu preṣyāṇāṃ dusthitirguroḥ । (l.7)
śāntihomaṃ tadā kṛtvā yathāvat sarvamācaret ॥
nitye naimittike kāmye pādyādidravyahīnake ।
tattalliṅge *bhavelloke tattaddravyavināśanīm ॥* (p.74b, f.71a, l.1)
pādadravye tadīye vā vihīne pādarogatāḥ ।
tadvadācamane ge ca mukharogo bhaviṣyati ॥
śirasya rghe tadaṅge *vā hīne sya(syā)t ghoṇarorutaḥ ।* (l.2)
ghaṇṭāyāstāḍanā hīne piśācā rākṣasādayaḥ ॥
pīḍyante copasargāśca jñāyante tannivāsinām ।
dīpapradāna*hīne yaṃ galope vā dṛṣṭi śogataḥ ।* (l.3)
omantriṇahavirlope tadaṅgānāmathāpi vā ॥
naivedyasya tadaṅgasyā(sya) pradāne sasya*nāśanam ।* (l.4)
tadaṅgadravyanāśa(śaḥ) syāt kṣupipāsā mahadbhavet ॥
tāmbūladānābhāve'ṅgahīne vā yatra rogatā ।
alābhe caṇḍapaṭe *hīne devānāṃ sannidhikṣayaḥ ॥* (l.5)
devaṃ saṃpūjya tadravyaṃ dviguṇaṃ dāpayecchive ।
śāntihomaṃ tataḥ kuryāt snapanaṃ vā guruṃ nayet ॥
tadadrihyāṅga*vihīne ca dviguṇaṃ dāpayecchive ।* (l.6)

	kartavyaṃ saṃhitāhomaḥ śatāhomaṃ kṛtvā tāghorajya pu po'thavā dravyāṇāmevameteṣā(l.7)mardhe pādatadardhake ।

pradhāne ghoramantreṇa dvicatuṣṣaṭśatakramāt ।
japtvā devāya tadd dviguṇa dāpayettataḥ ॥
dravyāṇye*tānyamantreṇa dattā yadiva śaṅkare ।* (p.75a, l.1)
adattadevataddravyamandevebhyo'bhyarcya deśikaḥ ॥
dadyādante svamantreṇa dravyaṃ vā dviguṇaṃ samam ।
snapanaṃ kārayediṣṭaṃ śata*ghorarūpaṃ ca yat ॥* (l.2)
nitye naimittike kāmye pādyāditrayalopane ।
sadyojātena neśānān śatadhāvartya sādhakaḥ ॥
pādyādyadviguṇaṃ dadyāt tadrūpyaṃ *kevalaṃ tu vā ।* (l.3)
dūrvapuṣpākṣate hīne śatajāpo hṛdā mataḥ ॥
pravaktamāne'bhyaṅge vodvartimajjanādike ।
hīne hṛdā śataṃ japtvā dviguṇaṃ dāpayet punaḥ ॥ (l.4)
asamskṛte'ṅgatailādye pūjāyā saṃhitāhutiḥ ।
nityanaimityakāmyeṣu pūrvakupte śarāhṛte ॥
vilupte saṃhitāhome *...dakṛtvā dviguṇamācaret ।* (l.5)
anena vidhinā kuryāt pañcagavyena niṣkṛtim ॥
pūrvaṃ saṃkalpite gandhe lupte sadya(dyaḥ) śataṃ japet । (l.6)
juhuyādvātha taddravyaṃ dviguṇaṃ samameva vā ॥
apuṣkalavihīne vā puṣpaṃ datte viparyayāt ।
śatavāraṃ japedīśaṃ taddadyādadviguṇaṃ tataḥ ॥ (p.75b, f.72a, agnibādhai, l.1)
dhūpaścārātrikaṃ ghoraṃ japtvānte dviguṇaṃ tataḥ ।
dhūpārātrikayo(yoḥ) dānaṃ kāle nīrāñjanasya ca ॥
vicchinne śatadhāvartya punaḥ saṃsmṛtya kārayet ।
dīpamālā*sthitenātha mūṣakādyānanena vā ॥* (l.2)
vahninā yadi saṃspṛṣṭo liṅgaberāṅgamādikam ।
amūvāgato vāpi rājño rogo bhaviṣyati ॥
saṃśodhya vahninā *dṛṣṭaṃ snāpayet parameśvaram ।* (l.3)
pañcāmṛtasamāyuktanālikerodakānvitam ॥
saṃpūjya devadeveśa mūrtihomaṃ samācaret । (l.4)
aṇumātro bhavedvahniryadi śāntiṃ samācaret ॥
liṅgaberādisaṃsthāni vāsaṃsi yadi vahninā ।
dagdhāni yadā kuryāt karmasaṃprokṣaṇātmakam ॥ (l.5)
dagdhāni cet pradeśena tadāśātoyasena tu ।
jalajānāṃ ca sarveṣāṃ vaiṣṇavānāṃ tathāmbhasā ॥
śarāvapatrapuṭakaprabhṛtīnāṃ ca bhojane ।
śuddhi(ddhiḥ) *rātropayuktānāṃ parityāgo vidhīyate ॥* (l.6)
viṇmūtraśleṣmasaṃśliṣṭā tyājyāste veṇusambhavāḥ ।
sarvadhātusamutpannaṃ vahnau spṛṣṭvā *viśuddhyati ॥* (l.7)
pātraṃ kiṭākhumājārairālīḍhaṃ tu viśuddhyati ।
saptāstrakṣaṇanādadbhiḥ paryagnikiraṇādapi ॥
kharoṣṭhaśvādināmāghrātamavalī *santu santyajet ।* (p.76a, l.1)
gorūpā prā(vrā)tapātraṃ ca sampatyojyameva hi ॥
na kulairavalīsantu yatpātraṃ ghṛtameva vā ।
saptaśaścāpanīmuktamagninā śuddhimeva hi ॥
vāriṇā *darvipūrvāṇi śuddhimāyāti bhāṇḍavat ।* (l.2)
prabhūtamagnaṃ kākādyai(dyaiḥ) juṣṭaṃ tanmātravarjitam ॥
ucchiṣṭasya ca saṃsparśādannado'nnaṃ nidhāya ca ।
ācamyānantādikaṃ prokṣya spṛśe*dastoka(da)kena tu ॥* (l.3)
snigdhānāṃ rūkṣaṇācchuddhiḥ salepānāmalepanam ।
gaḍhā ca nayanācchuddhi durgandhānāmupā hṛdā ॥
āsanaṃ śayanaṃ yānaśuddhimeti mṛdāmbhasā । (l.4)
bhṛtyahastaṃ kāruhastaṃ puṇyavikrayamādarāt ॥
aduṣṭāṇḍau tamadbhiśca śakuniśāditaṃ phalam ।
kṣmāgotrāntyā ca khananāddagdhaṃ śuddhā ca lepitā ॥ (l.5)
rathyā mārgagataṃ śuddhaṃ vātacandrārkaraśmibhiḥ ।
makṣikākeśasammiśraṃ pādāntopahataṃ ca yat ॥
rajaḥ pipīlikāyuktaṃ bhuñjāno'nnamavekṣitam । (l.6)
tāvanmātraṃ samuddhṛtya prokṣayedambhasā tapaḥ ॥
ājyābhighāraṇācchuddhirbhasmakṣapeṇa vā bhavet ।
pakvaṃ droṇādhikachannaṃ kākaśvā*dyupaghātitam ॥* (l.7)
gra?muddhṛtya tanmātraṃ tattu lālāghṛtaṃ bhavet ।
hemodakena cārabhyukṣyedrājatenāmbunāthavā ॥
kākaśvādyavalīḍaṃ(ḍhaṃ) tu droṇānnyūnaṃ *tu varjayet ।* (p.76b, f.73a, utsavaprāyaścittam, l.1)
viprāṇāṃ brahmaghoṣeṇā(ṇa) pūtaṃ bhojyaṃ ca tadbhavet ।
bahvanne ca vivāheṣu yajñeṣu prakṛteṣu cā(ca) ॥
pratiṣṭhādiṣu kāleṣu dīkṣotsava*dineṣvapi ।* (l.2)
kākaiśvabhiścantiṃ samācaret ?
dahane satma(dma)naḥ kuryāt satma(dma)saṃprokṣaṇaṃ nayet ।
devāya snapanaṃ kuryāddhāmachodādisambhavet ॥
taduktāṃ niṣkṛtiṃ *kuryāt sandhānādisamanvitam ॥* (l.3)
vahninā liṅgaberādyamanyavarṇamupāgate ।

	mānuṣaṃ cet parityājyaṃ gṛhyaṃ svastikaṃ pratikrayākarmoktyā kuryāt saṃ*prokṣaṇānvitam ॥* (l.4)

prāṇyaṅgadhṛpadānena pañcamasya śataṃ japet ।
dīpānāmatha vaikalye japedghoraśatatrayam ॥
dīpāni dviguṇaṃ paścā(ścāt) *devadeva pradāpayet ।* (l.5)
rekṣā(khā)di(dhi)pasya vicchede vyeva? dviguṇajāpayuk ॥
haviṣyadatte deva .... snapanaṃ tu vidhāya ca ।
dviguṇaṃ dāpayetpaścā*dāmantraṇa(ṇaṃ) haviṣyati ॥* (l.6)
atha kuṇḍapaṭe hīne kavacaṃ dviśataṃ japet ।
pratisaṃvatsaraṃ māsaṃ tithiyukta.....rthakam ॥
niścitya vartate pūrvamukasavo yaidi(rdi)naiḥ purā । (l.7)
navāhādyairyuto bherītāḍanādipurassaram ॥
taddinatriguṇe vahni taddinadviguṇe'pi vā ।
tadanyathākṛto rāṣṭrarājño doṣāvaho *bhavet ॥* (p.77a, l.1)
tatra śāntiṃ vidhāyādau prāgvadutsavamācaret ।
kintu māsāntare sa syāttasminmāse'thavā bhavet ॥
dviguṇe taddine vātha mūrtihomādiśāsatiḥ ।
kramāddvidhāya *kartavyamutsavastadvidhānataḥ ॥* (l.2)
dhvajārohaṇapūrve tu triguṇāhaṃ prakalpite ।
vidhānamevamākhyātaṃ dviguṇepyevamūhyatām ॥
liṅgaṃ svāyambhuvaṃ cettu vidhāna*midamīritam ।* (l.3)
mānuṣe bāṇaliṅgādau daivike vārṣake'pi vā ॥
māsabhaṃ? tīrthanakṣatramanyathā vā nayeddvijāḥ ।
utsavo yai?dinai(naiḥ) pūrvaiḥ tasya vṛddhilapradā ॥ (l.4)
rāṣṭrakṣobhe ca durbhikṣe rājacorabhayādiṣu ।
vittābhāve'nyabhītibhiḥ pra(prā)gvat kartumaśakyatā ॥
yadi jāyeta tatrāpi śāntipūrvavidhāya ca । (l.5)
dinahīnaṃ tu vā kuryāt utsavaṃ pūrvamārgataḥ ॥
sarvalopāt praśastasyādalpāhe naṣṭatotsavaḥ ।
sarvalope kṛte tasya *mahādoṣaḥ prajāyate ॥* (l.6)
śāntiṃ ca śāntihomaṃ ca mūrtihomaṃ tadā nayet ।
anena vidhinā yena sarvaṃ naimittikādikaḥ ॥
vighnādyairutsave *hīnaḥ prārabdho rāṣṭranāśanam ।* (p.77b, f.74a, l.1)
śāntihomaṃ tadā kuryāt punarutsavamācaret ॥
vṛkṣādhi(di)vāsanāpete dhvaje vā ropite sati ।
gāvo naśyettataḥ *kuryācchāntihomaṃ taduktavat ॥* (l.2)
vṛṣayāgaṃ jayettatra dhvajadaṇḍasya mūlataḥ ।
kiṃ tu dhvaste dhvaje mantranyāsādyaṃ manasā caret ॥
dhvajārohaṇahīne tu nṛpate*rbhayamādiśet ।* (l.3)
śāntiṃ kṛtvā tadā mantrī punarāropaye(yet) dhvajam ॥
dhvajasya bhramaṇe hīne grāmādāvaśubhāvaham ।
śāntihomaṃ tadā kuryāt sarvadoṣa*nivṛttaye ॥* (l.4)
vedihīnahīnā vā lakṣaṇā vā śubhapradā ।
śāntihomaṃ tadā kṛtvā vedīṃ kuryāt salakṣaṇām ॥
daṇḍo lakṣaṇahīnaśca pradhānasyāśubhāvaham ।
mūrtihomaṃ *tadā kuryāt śāntihomamathāpi vā ॥* (l.5)
svalakṣaṇavihīnaśchedupadaṇḍo bhayapradā ।
skandamānasya vaināhau? śāntihomaṃ tadā nayet ॥
daṇḍopadaṇḍa*skandāderbhodabhaṅgādisaṃbhave ।* (l.6)
śāntihomaṃ tadā kuryāt bhagne śāntyantaraṃ nayet ॥
dhvajo lakṣaṇahīnaścedrāṣṭrasyāsya bhayaṅkaraḥ । (p.78a, l.1)
salakṣaṇadhvajaṃ cānyaṃ rohaṇārthaṃ samārabhet ॥
śāntikarma nayeddhīnalakṣaṇadhvajaropaṇe ।
evameva vṛṣe kuryānniṣkṛtido hānayet(ye) ॥
ūrdhvacitranṛ(nu) nṛttaśce(ścet) mantramaiśaṃ śataṃ japet । (l.2)
adho vetravihīnaścennaiva doṣāya kalpite ॥
rajjucchede ri?bhibādhā śāntihomaṃ samācaret ।
ākhvādidaṣṭe vastrādyai(dyaiḥ) śāntihomaṃ tadā caret ॥
patākā patite bhūmau *yadi rāṣṭrasya duḥsthitiḥ ।* (l.3)
śāntihomaṃ tadā kṛtvā vidhinā ropayeddhvajam ॥
dhvajabhedādisaṃyukte śāntikarma tadācaret ।
patākāropayayedanyāṃ saptāhābhyantare dvijāḥ ॥ (l.4)
pūrvaṃ pūrvaṃ praśastaṃ syādalpahāni yadi dvijāḥ ।
mūrtihomaṃ tadā kuryācchāntihomamathāpi vā ।
ketāvaruṇyamā(ṇe)ne'sminvātādyabhibhavena vā ॥
liṅgite doṣadaḥ *śāktyai śāntihomaṃ samācaret ।* (l.5)
darbhamālāvihīnā cecchāntihomaṃ tadācaret ॥
daṇḍaṃ salakṣaṇadarbhamālāyā veṣṭayettataḥ ।
rajjurvā darbhamālāṃ *vā pradakṣiṇavidhānataḥ ॥* (l.6)
daṇḍe saṃyojitā sā cet japtvā ghoraṃ sahasraśaḥ ।
pradakṣiṇakramādagrādagramālādikaṃ nayet ॥
upadaṇḍo'pi kūrco yajjapet *ghoraṃ śatatrayam ।* (l.7)
dhvaje ghaṇṭāvihīne cenna syādvṛṣabhasannidhiḥ ॥
āvartayedaghorāṇāṃ triśataṃ deśikastathā ।
ghaṇṭāyā patane bhūmau dakṣiṇaṃ triśataṃ *japet ॥* (l.8)
āruhya bandhanaṃ neṣṭaṃ daṇḍanāthaḥ śivo yataḥ ।
dhvajārohaṇakāle tu kumbhanyāso na cet kṛtaḥ ॥
vṛṣakumbhavibhedāni kāraṇāt keturohaṇam । (p.78b, f.75a, bheryādipatanam, l.1)
kṛtvānte śāntihomaṃ ca kumbhāt saṃsādya pūrvavat ॥
mantranyāsaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā manasokṣaṃ hṛdambuje ।
tadāvaraṇakumbhāmbhobhiṣeko na kṛte yadi ॥
ekaikasya svamantraistu japaṃ *kuryācchataṃ śatam ।* (l.2)
āropite dhvaje tasminvā layādavalambite ॥
ekadvitricaturmātrairnirdoṣāya prakalpitaḥ ।
adho'dho ravimātrāntaṃ ghoratriśataṃ japet ॥
tadadholambite *ketau śāntihomaṃ vidhāya ca ।* (l.3)
āropayeddhvajaṃ paścādvṛṣabhā(bhāḥ) ca na saṃyutam ॥
bharīsantāḍane hīne koke moha(haṃ) prajāyate ।
śāntihomaṃ tadā kṛtvā *bherīṃ santāḍayettataḥ ॥* (l.4)
bṛhattālaśya paṭhanaṃ na kṛte śāntihomā caret ।
utsavārambhataḥ pūrve devatāvāhane sati ॥
devatāvāhane hīne devādīn *na ca sannidhiḥ ।* (l.5)
śāntihomaṃ nayetprāgvaddevatāhvānamācaret ॥
keśābāskandavighneśabhānucaṇḍeśvaro yadā ।
anāhūtā tadā doṣo nānyā *nāhutīrāpade ॥* (l.6)
patākāropaṇāpetadevānāvāhayennava ।
bherītāḍanakāryaṃ tu tadvadeva samācaret ॥
bherī camādibhedādau ghoṇacedādi*sambhave ।* (l.7)
aghorāstraśataṃ japtvā bherīmanyāmathācaret ॥
tathaiva ghoṇamanyaṃ ca gri(gṛ)hītvā prāgvadācaret ।
ghoṇe vā patite bhūmau *aghoratriśataṃ japet ॥* (l.8)
tatkāle vā ................ vādake patite bhuvi ।
śāntihomaṃ tadā kuryādrāṣṭraṃ sthitibhānaye ॥
bherikā patite bhūmau bhavedrājño ....yaṃ *mahat ।* (p.79a, kalaśādipatanam, l.1)
śāntihomaṃ tadā kṛtvā bherīṃ saṃpūjayeddhṛdā ॥
yāgaśālāgnidagdhā vā vihīnā lakṣaṇaistu vā ।
vimānaṃ kuṇḍavedī vā rāṣṭraṃ dussthiti vā bhavet ॥ (l.2)
yathāvat kārayet pūrvamusavenutsave yadi ।
pratyahaṃ śāntihomaṃ syādyāvattīrthadinaṃ bhavet ॥
jñātvā tadutsavaṃ caiva yāgaśālaṃ ca lakṣaṇā । (l.3)
śāntiṃ kuryāttadaikāhaṃ taddoṣavinivṛttaye ॥
vidhānametat sāmānyaṃ pratiṣṭhāyāṃ ca niṣkṛtau ।
snapane prokṣaṇe māsapūjādau sūtralopaṇe ॥ (l.4)
utsavāṅge ca vānyasmin jīrṇoddhāre'ṣṭakāvidhau ।
nitye naimittike kāmye karmaṇyanyakrayo japet ॥
yāgopakaraṇe*'pyevaṃ kiṃ tu yānāvasānake ।* (l.5)
śāntihomamaghorāṇvo dviśataṃ triśataṃ japet ॥
vihīne sthaṇḍilāṅge vā śāntihomaṃ tadācaret । (l.6)
pūrvavat sthaṇḍilaṃ kuryāt tasya doṣanivṛttaye ॥
śivakumbhe ca vardhanyāmāvṛteṣu ghaṭeṣu ca ।
utsavāṅgeṣu cānyeṣu *kalaśeṣu yathā bhavet ॥* (p.79b, f.76a, kaṅkaṇaṃ pādukādi, l.1)
bhedo vā suṣirachechedo vai lakṣaṇyakrameṇa tu ।
śāntihomamaghorāṇāṃ japaṃ pañcacatuśśatam ॥
śata...śatamityeva *kuryādrogāpanuttaye ।* (l.2)
kumbhādyaṃ navamānīya prāgvat saṃsthāpanaṃ nayet ॥
vardhanī vātha kumbho vā ghaṭavidyeśvarādhipāḥ ।
svarṇāmbujavihīnā *vā svalpasvarṇasamanvitāḥ ॥* (l.3)
kuryuḥ kartuśca dāridraṃ japtvāśorasahasrakam ।
śāstrasiddhaṃ punardadyāttaddoṣaṃ ca nivṛttaye ॥
kumbhādyā *vastrahīnaścedvātarogo bhaviṣyati ।* (l.4)
japtvāghorasahasraṃ tu dadyādvastraṃ punarguruḥ ॥
pañcaratnādivaikalye'pyevameva samācaret । (l.5)
sūtrakūrcavidhānādirabhāve'pyevamācaret ॥
hīne kautukabandhe tu tadaṅge vā purudite ।
sa yāgo niṣphalaproktastasmāt *taddoṣaśāntaye ॥* (l.6)
śāntihomaṃ tadā kuryāt kautukaṃ bandhayettataḥ ।
japedaghoraṃ triśataṃ tadaṅgānāṃ vilopane ॥
tadagnikṛte tasmin *kṛtvā sadyo'dhipālanam ।* (p.80a, bali, l.1)
pūrvasathānasthite deve proktaṃ kautukabandhanam ॥
ānīya maṇṭapādau vā japtvāghoraṃ śatārdhakam ।
balitālāditālānāmabhāve *dakṣiṇaṃ japet ॥* (l.2)
pādukā vāhakā vāpi puṣpāñjalikṛtā navim ।
patane nāṭyahīne vā pādukāpatane tathā ॥
triśataṃ ghoramantrasya pādukāyā sahasrakam । (l.3)
...panaṃ kārayitvā tu pādukābhramaṇaṃ nayet ॥
triśataṃ dīpahānau ca dviguṇaṃ dīpamācaret ।
utsave yānahīnaṃ cet sarvadoṣāvaho nṛṇām ॥
snapanaṃ *śāntihomaṃ ca pratiyānaṃ samācaret ।* (l.4)
vṛṣṭikāle mahāvāte rāṣṭrakṣobhe mahābhaye ॥
grāmapradakṣiṇaṃ neṣṭaṃ dhāmapradakṣiṇam ।
snapanaṃ śāntihomaṃ *ca tadā kāryo dvijottamāḥ ॥* (l.5)
ekavāstuni viprendrā vartamāne śivotsave ।
anyadevotsavastatra kṛtaṃ cet grā........... ॥
taddoṣa*vinivṛtyarthaṃ śāntihoma samācaret ।* (l.6)
tathaiva mātṛśāstārau prāyaścittaṃ samācaret ॥
aṅgāṅgajavalī caiva yā......*tau nadauṣabhāḥ ।* (l.7)
vāmabhairavasiddhaścet bali grāmakṣayo bhavet ॥
balipramādāddattaścecchāntihomaṃ samācaret ।
śūnyagrāmaṃ samā*śritya baliyāne prakalpite ॥* (p.80b, f.77a, l.1)
tadā rāṣṭrakṣayaṃ vidyāt kṛtaṃ cocchāntimācaret ।
ālaye baliyāne vā tasya devālayāvṛtau ॥
devāṅgavāsta....stasya *paravāste viśeṣataḥ ।* (l.2)
tathaivāsannavastuśca durvāsto dvijottamaḥ ॥
nānāvṛkṣasamāyukto naikavṛkṣasamanvitaḥ ।
tasmātpraśastasyādviparīte vipatkaram ॥ (l.3)
brahmakṣatraviśāṃ vāstu śreṣṭha ityabhidhīyate ।
antarvāstorviśiṣṭassyāt bahirvāstu balibhrame ॥
ekatra ba(tva)yāne vā śreṣṭhamityabhidhīyate ।
anukalpohya *śaktānāmanyatraivaṃ samācaret ॥* (l.4)
anukalpapravṛttānāṃ śāntihomaṃ samācaret ।
hīne vitāne ghorāṇuśatasaṅkhyājapa....yet ॥ (l.5)
dhvaje vihīne'pyevaṃ syājjapāt ghāraśatāṇunā ।
dhvaje vihīne rātrau tu doṣo nāsti dvijottamāḥ ॥
balidravye vihīne tu kṣudbādhā *jāyate nṛṇām ।* (l.6)
mūrtihomaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā yānānte vā baliṃ nayet ॥
devālaye balihīne yadā ghoraśataṃ japet ।
yasyāṃ diśi balihīnagrāmādau *saṃhitāhutim ॥* (l.7)
tattaddiśāpate mantraṃ śatajāpasamanvitam ।
sahāyayānakāle vā tadante vā baliṃ nayet ॥
annaliṅge vihīne tu durbhikṣaṃ *jāyate bhuvi ।* (p.81a, śūlādipatanam, l.1)
śāntihomaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā sānnaliṅgaṃ baliṃ nayet ॥
tacchedabhedapātādau tadheva balimādiśet ।
talliṅgaṃ sudṛḍhaṃ kṛtvā yajedastraṃ tu tadgatam ॥ (l.2)
vidhāya saṃhitāhomaṃ tasmin sthāne tadantaram ।
nayedārabhya talliṅgapādasthānāt pradakṣiṇaḥ ॥
patitaṃ cet yajedannaṃ sampādyānnaṃ samarcayet ।
samāpya pūrvavadyānaṃ *saṃhitāhomamācaret ॥* (l.3)
balipātre tu patite tvaghorāṇaṃ śataṃ japet ।
bhedādāvanyadādāya balidravyaṃ tu nikṣipet ॥
daśāyudhāni vā bhūmau saṃ*bhūtāni patanti cet ।* (l.4)
nirmāṇāni tadā kuryācchāntihomamajvarāvahaḥ ॥
chedabhedādiyuktāni yadi dārumayāni vā ।
tathānyāni samāpādya saṃ*bhūtāni narāṇi ca ॥* (l.5)
samskṛtya sva sva mantraistu sā viśeṣaṃ samācaret ।
lohajātyanukarmoktyā nītvānte sthāpanaṃ nayet ॥
śūlāstre'rpati *te rājño bhayaṃ bhavati tatra ca ।* (l.6)
dhāmādādi diśāhomaṃ nayedastrārcanānvitam ॥
chedabhedāke jāte tvāpādyo vānukarmavat । (p.81b, f.78a, l.1)
utsavaṃ pūrvavat kāryā da?nyenāstreṇa vā dvijāḥ ॥
samāpyeran sva kāryaṃ tu tat sāyānukarmavat ।
saṃprokṣaṇaṃ tadā kuryādastrarājapratiṣṭayā ॥ (l.2)
yānakāle'nyakāle vā berādau patite sati ।
rājā maraṇamāpnoti sadyaḥ kuryāt pratikra(kri)yā ॥
apyaṅge patite bere .......... *pākarāṃ dadhaḥ ।* (l.3)
dvikaratrikarā vāpi tadūrdhvaṃ ca kramānnayet ॥
saṃhatā śāntihomau ca śāntiṃ muktiṃ tadāhutīm ।
snapanaṃ ca sahasrā.... *tāle tāle yutaṃ kramāt ॥* (l.4)
snapanaṃ vā japo ghorāṇunā daśasahasrakam ।
naivedyamapi sāmānyamutsavaṃ śeṣamācaret ॥ (l.5)
aṅgahīne tu karmoktyā sandhānādyaṃ ca niṣkṛtim ।
adhivāsādikaṃ karma sadya eva samācaret ॥
māsarkṣatithivārādiprekṣāṃ hi kvaiva deśikaḥ । (l.6)
punaśca snapanaṃ kṛtvā sahasrārdhaṃ śataṃ tu vā ॥
punaśca kautukaṃ badhvā utsavādyaṃ prakalpayet ।
uttamāṅgādihīne *cānukarmoktyā samācaret ॥* (p.82a, l.1)
tatrāpyanyena bereṇa tatsandhānaṃ hi cotsavam ।
kārayeddeśiko dhīmān pratyahaṃ śāntiho........ ॥ (l.2)
dhāmāntaragatenātha kartavyo vā dvijottamāḥ ।
garbhadvārādimāne'ṣṭamānayuktena nānyathā ॥
tato bera(raṃ) samāpādyānukarkoktyā tadeva hi ।
pratiṣṭhāṃ ca *vidhāyānte aṅkurārpaṇapūrvakam ॥* (l.3)
utsavaṃ kārayeddevaṃ navāhyādyutsaveṣu ca ।
liṅge'pyevaṃ samānaṃ syāt *maheśīva dīkṣitāḥ ॥* (l.4)
utsavagrāmabāhye tu baddhapratisaro dvijāḥ ।
bere nīte tadā doṣā rājño bhavati śāntaye ॥
śānti*homaṃ tadā kuryāt saṃhitāhomameva vā ।* (l.5)
kurvāṇaṃ cet kriyāmbhau patito deśikastathā ॥
grāmā....va sthijñe *tadarthaṃ bahurūpiṇā ।* (l.6)
japet pañcaśataṃ mantriḥ patanepyevameva hi ॥
ārabdhe devakāryeṣu bahusaṅkhyāni teṣu ca । (p.82b, f.79a, ācāryā matādi, l.1)

(ācāryamatādi -)
ācāryasya pramādena vyādhibhirvātha mṛti bhavet ।
tatpitrorvā mṛtiṃ prāpte kathaṃ kurvīta tatra hi ॥
bhrāntibhirvātha putreṇa kāraye*dvidhipūrvakam ।* (l.2)
bhrātṛhīnā guruścātra śiṣye badhvā sarasvakam ॥
snātvā sacelastat kṛtyaṃ yathākarma tathā caret ।
anyena cet kṛtaṃ *karmā tathā pitro(troḥ) gurūttamaḥ ।* (l.3)
snātvā tatraiva śālāyāṃ sthāpa bhukti tadantike ॥
yāgāntaṃ vidhivat kṛtvā utsave'nu*tsavepi caḥ(ca) ।* (l.4)
baddhapratisaro śiṣya snātvā saṅkalpapūrvakam ॥
vṛṣayāgaṃ samārabhya daṇḍadakṣiṇapārśvake ।
uttarā *hi mukho bhūtvā manasokṣaṃ samarcayan ॥* (l.5)
bṛhattālasya paṭhanaṃ devān daṇḍe samarcayet ।
devayānaṃ baliṃ tatra narka*kālikadināntakam ॥* (l.6)
dvivārasaṃkhyayā kṛtvā grāme devālaye'vā ।
daṇḍe pradakṣiṇaṃ vātha dvivārakramato nayet ॥ (p.83a, l.1)
tatkālikaṃ yathāyogyaṃ tīrthāntaṃ sarvamācaret ।
snapanaṃ śāntikaṃ karma taddoṣavinivṛttaye ॥
devasya kārayitvā tu karma tatra nayedvidhi । (l.2)
prasūtau ca mṛtāvevaṃ baddhapratisarena tat ॥
hutaśeṣaṃ sa bhuktvā tu tatraiva nivasedguruḥ ।
rājñapradhānamahiṣi tatpitā bhrātṛputrakau ॥ (l.3)
patnitaḥ pradhānaśca mṛtaścennṛvite bhayam ।
balidātari tatkāle patiteśaṃ śataṃ japet ॥
āyudhā astrabimbeśavāhake patite *guruḥ ।* (l.4)
śataṃ japet ghoradviguṇaṃ śaṅkarāstrayoḥ ॥
agnirpāṇapradipasya naivedyādervilopane ।
prāguktavidhinā kuryāt *yacchittaṃ gurūttamaḥ ॥* (l.5)
pūjāyāścaikasandhyādivihīnāyā vilopane ।

pādyādīnāṃ ca gavyasya puṣpāṇāṃ dhūpadīpavilupte vahnikāye ca homadravyavilopane ।

nirvāṇāgnau śivopete viśive vātha pūrvavat । (p.83b, f.80a, rathahanam, l.1)

(rathahanam –)
rathārohaṇahīnaścennicāsyumanujā bhuvi ।
śāntihomaṃ tadā kṛtvā rathārohaṇamācaret ॥
rathārohaṇakāle vā avarohaṇa....*pi vā । (l.2)
rathānayanakāle vā gobrāhmaṇamṛtau yadi ॥
diśāhomaṃ tu tatraivā(va) mahāsnapanameva ca ।
rājño mṛti vināśārthaṃ kāraye....*dvijasattamaḥ ॥* (l.3)
nāgādikṣatriyamṛtau viṭchūdrastrījanādike ।
aṅgacchedādike vāpi rājño doṣāvaho bhavet ॥
diśā....*hmaṇa mūrtihomaṃ śāntihomaṃ ca śāntikam ।* (l.4)
tattaddoṣavināśāya kārayet sādhakottamaḥ ॥
chatre tu patite bhūmau che....*vāyvādinā yadi ।* (l.5)
rājño nāśakaraṃ caiva prajānāṃ nāśahetukam ॥
diśāhomaṃ tadā kri(kṛ)tvā snapanaṃ parameṣṭhinaḥ ।
chatramanyaṃ tu saṃ....dya *puṇyāhaṃ vācayet guruḥ ॥* (l.6)
samskṛtya vidhivacchatraṃ yathāpūrvaṃ nayedguruḥ ।
dhvaje tu patite bhūmau chedde(dda)gdhe ....*topari ॥* (p.84a, l.1)
ārūḍhe sati gṛdhrādyai graṃhite śatrubhirnaraiḥ ।
anyebhyo bhūpatibhyaśca bhayaṃ rājño bhaviṣyati ॥
taddoṣaparihārārthaṃ snapanaṃ kārayecchive । (l.2)
rarāṅgāni ca sarvāṇi patite snapanaṃ nayet ॥
bhūtādhāre'pi vicchinne rathāṅge chedapātane ।
punaranyat samāyojya pañcagavyakuśodakaiḥ ॥
yānakāle *rathādīnāṃ bhaṅgolavitavodbhavet ।* (l.3)
taddeśe dhāmni vā kuryādyānānte doṣaśānti(nta)yet(ye) ॥
sandhānakarmayogyaṃ ca rathāntarapathānnaye... । (l.4)
ānīya śibikādyaṃ vā tatrāropya maheśvaram ॥
pūrvavaddevadeveśaṃ svālayaṃ viniveśayet ।
rathāni te tu caṇḍālai...*....dyaistathaiva ca ॥* (l.5)
snapanaṃ kārayedante śāntikarma samācaret ।
vinodadarśane kāle jātaścet kala.....rān ॥ (l.6)
śāntihomaṃ tadā kuryādaghoraṃ vā japedadhā(tha) ।
vinodadarśane hīne saṃhitāhomamācaret ॥
nīrājanavidhau *ne prāyaścittaṃ tu pūrvavat ।* (p.84b, f.81a, nri(nṛ)ttamūrtihīnam, l.1)

(nṛttamūrtihīnam -)
.........ne prāyaścittaṃ tu pūrvavat ।
yānānte snapane hīne śāntihomamavilopite ॥
devasannidhivaikalye mamū(mū)rtihomaṃ pratikra(kri)yā ।
snapanaṃ śāntihomaṃ *ca nayet santathi(ti)siddhaye ॥* (l.2)
hīnādhiṣṭhitadeśe vā devayāne pravartite ।
pracurasnapanaṃ kuryāt śāntihome mahanta(tta)ram ॥
tailābhyaṅge vihīne *tu śiro rogo nṛṇāmihā(ha) ।* (l.3)
śāntihomaṃ tadā kṛtvā tailābhyaṅgaṃ samācaret ॥
nṛttamūrtyutsave hīne pūrva*kalpite dine ।* (l.4)
bhavanti vāpinaḥ sarve tadardhaṃ śāntimācaret ॥
naṭeśabhramaṇaṃ paśco(ścā)diṣṭakāle samācaret ।
yātrādānasya *vicchede sa yātrā niṣpa(ṣpha)lā bhavet ॥* (l.5)
śāntihomaṃ tadā kṛtvā pūrvavaddānamācaret ।
mṛgayātrā tu vaikalye nṛpasya tu ca *jayo bhavet ॥* (l.6)
śāntihomaṃ tadā kṛtvā mṛgayāne'ṣṭahe nayet ।
hīne kautukatīrthe syānnaiva gambhādi? sannidhiḥ ॥
tīrthe *sūryodayāt pūrvatīrthako(kā)rye kṛte sati ।* (p.85a, l.1)
niṣkṛti syāt kramācchāntihomaṃ vai saṃhitāhutiḥ ॥
etat kautukatīrthasyāddhvajārohe'tra te sati ।
anyatra kautukaṃ *snānaṃ kalpayedvarjayettu vā ॥* (l.2)
cūrṇotsave tadaṅge vā hīne syādrogapīḍanam ।
śāntihomaṃ bhavedaṅginyaṅge syāt saṃhitāhutuḥ ॥
vilupte dhamni vā *kuryāttīrthasnāne'thavāmataḥ ।* (l.3)
cūrṇotsavakrame hīne saṃhitāhomamācaret ॥
mahātīrthe vilupte syāt sa yāgo niṣphalo dvijāḥ ।
śāntiṃ *kṛtvā tadarthaṃ tu tīrthe kāryaṃ tato nayet ॥* (l.4)
pūrvaṃ nadyādike tasminvartamāne tadanyathā ।
kṛte taddoṣaśāntyarthaṃ śāntihomaṃ tadācaret ॥ (l.5)
tīrthaṃ galope kartavyaḥ saṃhitāhoma iṣṭadaḥ ।
prāsāde manuje jāte rājño bhayaṃ mahadbhavet ॥
mudādi tatpradeśāttu *visṛjya ca bahistataḥ ।* (l.6)
prokṣayedgomayāmbhobhiryena deśena te gataḥ ॥
taddeśaṃ tu kuryāt puṇyāhaprokṣaṇaṃ ca vai । (p.85b, f.82a, l.1)
mṛte prasūtadeśe tu khānayo'bhyaṅkhanettadā ॥
parimṛjya mṛdaṃ śuddhamṛdbhiśca paripūrayet ।
prakṣālyāsanaṃ na kuryāda(dda)rvī gomayā*lepanaṃ sthale ॥* (l.2)
puṇyāhaṃ tantra(tra)gavyena prokṣaṇaṃ ca kramānnayet ।
śaivasaṃbhojanāṃ cānte jalaṃ saṃprokṣaṇaṃ nayet ॥
snapanaṃ śānti*homaṃ ca yadi garbhagṛhe bhavet ।* (l.3)
śāntihomocitaṃ kuryādardhamaṇṭapake sati ॥
pratimāmaṇṭape śāntihomaṃ ca snapanaṃ vinā ।
anta*ssālāntare ceddhi jalasaṃprokṣaṇaṃ nayet ॥* (l.4)
antassālasthadevānāṃ snapanaṃ śāntihomayuk ।
gavyena prokṣayettasthapīṭhādyāpi viśuddhaye ॥ (l.5)
tyajenmadāni bhiṇḍāni kṣālayeddārujaṃ jalaiḥ ।
antassālā ca bāhye ca snapanena diśāhutiḥ ।
yāgasthānādideśeṣu *maraṇādau ca saṃbhave ॥* (p.86a, ratnapatanam, l.1)
pūrvavacchuddhimāpādya paryagnikaraṇaṃ nayet ।
diśāhomaṃ tadā kuryā(ryāt) mūrtihomaṃ tathāpi vā ॥
prākāramayabāhye tu śāntikarma *samācaret ।* (l.2)
dhāmno dvādaśadaṇḍāttarvāstāmartyā mṛtā yadi ॥
parityajyaivaṃ tataḥ kuryānnityanaimityakāmyakam ।
kṛtaṃ cet grāmanāśassyāt tadardhaṃ *snapanaṃ nayet ॥* (l.3)
sṛtāvapi mṛte dhāmni paśavo pakṣiṇo mṛgāḥ ।
sarpo vā jāyate rāṣṭre narāṇāmiha dusthitiḥ ॥
gomayā*lepanaṃ tatra prokṣayet pañcagavyataḥ ।* (l.4)
snapanaṃ garbhagehe cet śāntihomaṃ na kārayet ॥
kevalaṃ snapanaṃ cārdhamaṇṭape *parikalpayet ।* (l.5)
devatāmaṇṭape tasthadevān saṃprokṣya gavyataḥ ॥
saṃpūjya gandhapuṣpādyaiḥ śāntihomaṃ samācaret । (p.86b, f.83a, l.1)
etatsarvaṃ tu sāmānyamutsave'nutsaveṣu ca ॥
devālaye sabhāsthāne gobrāhmaṇaśarīrataḥ ।
bindumātramasṛkpāte tu virājyo mṛtirbhavet ॥ (l.2)
śāntihomamasaṅkhyātabrāhmaṇān bhojayettadā ।
uṣṇīṣamuttarīyaṃ vā vasanaṃ yajñasūtrakam ॥
śiro vā vahnidagdhe syādācāryadambhdha(dagdha)tiḥ । (l.3)
jvarabādhā bhavedgrāme taddoṣaśamanāya ca ॥
gurusnā(ssnā)tvā śucirbhūtvā śāntihomaṃ samācaret ।
evameva bhavet *karturhotṛṇāmamuni sattamāḥ ॥* (l.4)
vastraśayyāsanādīnāṃ yadā chedādisambhave ।
daṣṭo vā mūṣikādyaistu kramāt phalamihocyate ॥ (l.5)
koṇe vā bhogavṛddhī syāt prānte'niṣṭaṃ prakīrtitam ।
putro patniryaśaścaiva daśā pāśāntamadhyame ॥
prānte'niṣṭaṃ samākhyātaṃ *rogo vā mṛtyuranyataḥ ।* (p.87a, l.1)
vastraṃ vā kṛ(tri)guṇaṃ kṛtvā brahmendrayamavahniṣu ॥
mahādoṣassmākhyāto doṣo nānyatra kathyate ।
evameva narāṇāṃ ca *tadā tatsnāpitāya ca ॥* (l.2)
jatvā(ptvā)ghorasahasraṃ tu duṣṭavastrāṇi dāpayet ।
narāṇāṃ śivabhaktānāṃ dīkṣitānāṃ śivāya ca ॥
vastrādyairdaivikaṃ ceddhi *prokṣya gavyena śuddhaye ।* (l.3)
kāraye(yet) saṃhitāhomam aghoraṃ vā japedatha ॥
adīkṣitaiśca śūdrādyai(dyaiḥ) praviṣṭe yāgamaṇṭape ।
saṃprokṣyāstrāṇunā *ghoraṃ daśavāraṃ japenmanum ॥* (l.4)
tatsaṃspṛṣṭe tu kumbhādyetadanyaṃ viniveśayet ।
saṃhitāhomapūrvaṃ tu śvādīnāṃ ca praveśane ॥ (l.5)
gomayālepanaṃ caiva puṇyāhaprokṣaṇādikam ।

samidājyena caruṇā tilenāpi samanvitam ॥
śatamaṣṭottaraṃ hutvā pūrṇāmante samācaret ।
tasmādbhasmaṃ samādāyā(ya) yajamānāya dāpayet ॥
saṃprārthya vāñchitaṃ karma *praṇamecca punaḥ punaḥ ।* (l.3)
dinamekaṃ samārabhya saptāhāntaṃ samācaret ॥
dviguṇaṃ triguṇaṃ vāpi catuṣpañcaguṇaṃ tu vā ।
ṣaṭsaptaguṇasaṅkhyātaṃ karmasidhyanta*meva vā ॥* (l.4)
pūjā vai śeṣikākhyātā pūrvoktaphaladāyani ।
naimittikaṃ ca nityānte kartavyamavirodhataḥ ॥
nityapravartamāne tu yadā naimittikaṃ bhavet । (l.5)
ru(sa)māpyālpena kālena nityanaimittikaṃ caret ।
sandhyāparārdhakālā cet pūrvārdhe vidyate yadi ॥
naimittikamibhe? ceddhi samārabhya svakālake । (l.6)
ekādyāvaraṇaṃ caiva sandhyāśeṣaṃ samāpyakam ॥
ca balyante sarvakalaśai(śaiḥ) snāpayeduktavartmanā ।
naimittikadvayaprāpte ....*nṛpasya janmarkṣe ॥* (p.101b, f.101a, snapanavidhi siddhāntaśekhare, l.1)
yuddhodyogo jaye sati dīkṣāyāmabhiṣecane ।
anāvṛṣṭau jvare caiva dṛṣṭā bhūtanivīḍane ॥
prāya...(ści)tte *tathotpāte ghṛtakambalakādiṣu ।* (l.2)
śāntikarmaṇi sarvatra māṅgalye mantrasādhane ॥
evamādiṣu kāleṣu vidadhyāt snapanaṃ budhaḥ । (l.3)
prāsādasyāgrataḥ kuryānmaṇṭapaṃ navadhā tathā ॥
pañcaṣaṭsaptahastaistat kaniṣṭhatritaye matam ।
madhyamatritaye cāṣṭaṃ navapadiśarnaghasta?vistṛtam ॥
uttama*tritaye cārkadinaviṃtihastakam ।* (l.4)
stambhāṣṣoḍaśaṣaṭtriṃśaccatuṣṣaṣṭi yathākramam ॥
tālaissamuditā kāryā daśaikādaśabhānugaḥ ।
vidhimadhyagata*stambhā madhyastambhavivarjitāḥ ॥* (l.5)
ityuktamaṇṭapaṃ kakuryādvā(ddvā)ratoraṇabhūṣitām ।
aṣṭamaṅgalasaṃyuktaṃ maṅgalāṅkurabhūṣitam ॥
vitānadhvaja*saṃyuktaṃ darbhamālāpariṣkṛtam ।* (l.6)
tālamātraṃ talotsedhaṃ phalapallavabhūṣitam ॥
gomayālepite tasmin puṇyāhaṃ vācayedguruḥ । (p.102a, l.1)
svarṇatāmrādyaiḥ nirmitā kalaśāśśubhāḥ ॥ ?
kāraṇe kathitā grāhyā kalaśā(śāḥ) lakṣaṇānvitāḥ ।
kālaṃ bhinnaṃ ca purāṇādyā varjanīyāḥ prayatnataḥ ॥
vedāgniprana(ṇa)yana*prasthaiḥ sa pūrṇāścottamādikāḥ ।* (l.2)
dviguṇairvardhanī pūrṇā śivakumbhaścaturguṇaiḥ ॥
kalaśān veṣṭayet sarvān sūtreṇaikena buddhimān ।
vardhanīṃ sūtrayu....na *śivakumbhaṃ trisūtrataḥ ॥* (l.3)
pañcaṣaṭsaptahastoccaṃ pañcabhāgaikavistṛtam ।
āhitaṃ nūtanaṃ śvetaṃ kumbhaveṣṭanavastrakam ॥
sthitiṃ ca munibhi*rdarbhaiḥ kuryaṃ syāduttamādikam ।* (l.4)
uttamaṃ śivakumbha(mbhaṃ) syānmadhyamaṃ vardhanīgatam ॥
adhamaṃ kalaśe kūrcaṃ śivākhye pañcaratnakam ।

dhānyārdhaṃ taṇḍula? *madhyamam? ॥* (p.102b, f.103a, one folio missing, navakalaśasnapanam, l.1)
pūrvavannavakoṣṭhāni kṛtvā kumbhāśca vinyaset ।
śivaṃ vidyeśvarānaṣṭau pūjayet snāpayecca taiḥ ॥
kaniṣṭhamadhyamaṃ proktaṃ vakṣye *hīnottamaṃ tataḥ ।* (l.2)
prāgagrāṇyudagagrāṇi sūtrānyaṣṭau ca vinyaset ॥
madhye navapade kumbhaṃ śivākhyāṃ vinyasedbudhaḥ ।
tadbāhye paritaḥ kuryāt *vidhimekapadāvṛtām ॥* (l.3)
aṣṭau dvārāṇi kurvīta vidhyāntaṃ vardhanīṃ nyaset ।
prathamāvaraṇe cāṣṭau kalaśānvinyaset kramāt ॥
pādyamācamanaṃ *cārghyaṃ pañcagavyaṃ kuśodakam ।* (l.4)
pūrvādyuttaraparyantaṃ gandhāmbhaḥ koṇakoṣṭhake ॥
dvitīyāvaraṇe kṣaudraṃ koṣṭhapañcaiva guli *payaḥ ।* (l.5)
sarṣapaṃ ca lavaṅkaṃ ca takkolaṃ dadhimeghakam ॥
tilānuśīramājyaṃ ca śakracūrṇaṃ? ca saktukam ।
nālikeraṃ ca hāridraṃ nyaset pūrvāditaḥ kramāt ॥ (l.6)
yajet sadāśivaṃ madhye vardhanyāṃ tu nonmanīm ।
vāmādyā(dyāḥ) śaktayaḥ cāṣṭau prathamāvaraṇe mataḥ ॥
dikṣu śakrādayaścāṣṭau vidyeśāstu *vidiksthitāḥ ।* (p.103a, pañcaviṃśatikalaśasnapanam, l.1)
evaṃ saṃpūjya kalaśān tato homaṃ samācaret ॥
garbhagehaṃ tato gatvā liṅgaśuddhiṃ vidhāya ca ।
liṅge devaṃ samāvāhya samskuryā*ddaśadhā budhaḥ ॥* (l.2)
devadājyāṃ samādāya tata(taḥ) snapanamācaret ।
śivakumbha(mbhaṃ) samuddhṛtya garbhagehaṃ praviśya ca ॥
sthaṇḍilopari tau kumbhau sthāpayeddeva*sannidhau ।* (l.3)
maṅgaladhvanibhirliṅgaṃ mūlena snāpayedguruḥ ॥
gośṛṅgāgrapramāṇena śivakumbhāmbhasā śanaiḥ ।
piṇḍikāśaktimantreṇa *saṃsiñce(ñcet) vardhanīṃ jalaiḥ ॥* (l.4)
ādyāvaraṇakumbhāt liṅgamindrāditaḥ kramāt ।
dvitīyāvaraṇe tadvat brahmāṅgairabhiṣecayet ॥
sudhādhāraṇayā *liṅgaṃ saṃsiñcet gandhavāriṇā ।* (l.5)
nityoktavadyajelliṅgaṃ devaṃ caṇḍāvasānakam ॥
guruṃ saṃpūjayet kartā pradadyānmaṇṭapādikam । (l.6)
pañcaviṃśatikaṃ bhāvyaṃ snapanaṃ kathi(thitaṃ) tat mi(ma)yā ॥
kṛtāgniṣṭhomena yajñena viśvanāthena dhīmatā ।
vakṣye'thaikonapañcāśat kalaśasnapanam – (p.103b, l.1)
kalaśānvardhanīmadhikapathi prāgādinavake kumbhān gandhatoyena pūritān ।
madhyame navake dikṣu pādyamācamanaṃ nyaset ।
arghyaṃ ca pañcaga....*ca kṣīraṃ dadhi ghṛtaṃ madhu ॥* (l.2)
koṇeṣu vinyasettatra dvitīyāvaraṇe yathā ।
kuśodakaṃ ca lājāśca cakrarmacūrṇaṃ sasaktukam ॥
hāridrāṃ bhasmacekṣtvaṃ bu....*kadalīphalakattilāḥ ।* (l.3)

ici ṣoḍaśa vinyasyettritīya*dravyamucyate ॥* (l.4)
candanaṃ caiva lohaṃ ca carmadoṣābhidhānakam ।

dāntamauja *muraṃ kṛṣṇaṃ campakaṃ saha devikā ।* (l.5)

yavaśca śvetatūrvāśca takkolaṃ śaḍgasaṃyutam ।
tṛṇalā*lavaṅgavallī ca caturviṃtisaṅkhyayāṃ(yā) ॥* (l.6)

dvātriṃśadduvyasaṅkhyākaṃ caturthāvaraṇe nyaset ॥

bāhyaṣoḍaśakumbheṣu vyañjanāvaraṇe'rcayet ॥
