pañcaviṃśatikoṣṭhādhya(dya)madhyavyūhe navāṃśake ॥
śivakumbhaṃ nyasenmadhye vardhanīṃ tasya saumyake । (l.5)
prathamāvaraṇe tatra kumbhān ṣoḍaśa vinyaset ॥
pañcaviṃśatisaṅkhyātāt prativyūhaṃ nyasedghaṭān ।
evaṃ kumbhadravyaḥ samuddiṣṭāḥ śata*yugamaṃ ca ṣoḍaśaḥ ॥* (l.6)
vardhanī śivakumbhaśca dvāmādyāvadhikau matau ।
kumbhasthāpanamityuktaṃ kumbhadravyamathocyate ॥ (p.108a, l.1)
pādyaṃ madhu kuśāmbhaśca tailamācamanaṃ tataḥ ।
ikṣukṣīraṃ ca khaṇḍākhyamarghyaṃ ca panasaṃ dadhi ॥
kadalī pañcagavyaṃ ca cūtāśve mātulaṅgake ।
dravyāni(ṇi) śoḍa....*tāni vinyasetprathamāvṛtau ॥* (l.2)
yavasarṣapalājāśca talāścātha haridrakāḥ ।
gośakṛccūrṇaṃ bhasma candanaṃ laghukoṣṭhakam ॥
tuṭikarpūrakaṃ cātha phalānāṃ tritayaṃ tatha(thā) । (l.3)
dāṣakarvā tathā drākṣā nāgakesarasaṃjñakam ॥
babaraṃ gulguluṃ caivānnajuṣṭhaṃ lodhramañjanam ।
gorocanā turuṣkaṃ ca kuṅkumaṃ ca visaga(rga)kam ॥ (l.4)
gairikaṃ caiva rāsnā ca takkolaṃ ca manaśśilā ।
viṣṇukrāntā sahā(ha) lakṣmī dvātriṃśaddravyasañcayam ॥
dvitīyāvaraṇe nyasya *tri(tṛ)tīye dravyamucyate ।* (l.5)
balvadarbhā śamīpatramapāmārgadvayaṃ tathā ॥
śvetapadmaṃ ca kumudaṃ dhūrdhūraṃ vellikā tathā ।
karavīraṃ ca punnāgaṃ *pa...................... ॥* (p.108b, folio broken in the beginning, l.1)

	paṅkajaṃ paṅkajaத்க்கு கீழேயிருக்கிர உபதலமாகிய பாஹ்யதலம் வித்யோர்த்வஸ்த-ஶிவாந்தம் (l.2) ......vicitaṃ vidyā śuddhavidyātattva மூதர்கோண்டு ūrdhvaṃ jele sthāyirāத்திரிக்குர ஶிவாந்தம் ஶிவதத்வபர்யந்தம் பத்ம.... tadupari.......(l.3)பண்ணப்படி ரூப்பதாயிமக்குர-பீடம் brahmaviṣṇurudrapīṭhaமாக śivasya ஶிவனுக்கு āsanaṃ இருப்பிடம் sarvādhvādhikabhāgasaṃyutaśivajñā.......... (l.4) .......liṅgātmakaṃ sarvādhvā ṣaḍadhvākaகளுக்கும் அதிகமிஞ்சினதாயிருக்கு bhāgā brahmaviṣṇurudrabhāgaங்க-ளோடே saṃyuta துடிக்க (l.5) ……. யிருக்குர śivajñānākhyā paraśivayicchājñānasvarūpaரென்பைரா-யிருக்குர liṅgātmakaṃ liṅgarūpaத்தை அடஞ்சி ரூபத்தா(l.6)…..rdhādhikabhāganālasahitaṃ sarpā(rvā) ṣaḍadhvāக்களுக்கு அதிகமாயிருக்கிரபாக த்ரிபாகங்-களினாலே நாளஸஹிதம் gomukha(l.7)....... ஸஹிதமாயிருப்பதாயிருக்குர பீடம் ஸமோ na tu kriyāśaktyātmikaṃ kriyāśaktisvarūpaத்தை அடஞ்சி-ருக்கிரதாக ஸ்யாத். (p.109a, l.1) ………..விடும்.16. svādhāre gatamanasaḥ śivamanośaktyātmako dānato mātrā dīptakaleśvarakramagate mantraṃ samuccārayan nītvā kāntaka(l.2)…….paraśivaṃ prāpyaikabhāvaṃ tato ghaṇṭānādalalāṭagaṃ kusumagaṃ lakṣye mahonta nyaset । .... svādhāre gatamānasaḥ । (l.3) .......nāḥ । śaktyātmakaḥ । mātrā dīptikaleśvarakramagaterdānataḥ । mantraṃ samuccārayan । guruḥ añjaliṃ kāntakaṃ nītvā । paraśivaṃ (l.4) ...........vaṃ prāpya । sañcintyā(ntya) । tataḥ puṣpāñjaliṃ adho gamayan san । ghaṇṭānādalalāṭāntam । tataḥ kusumagaṃ mahālakṣye antaḥ (l.5) .......... என்று svādhāre தன்னுடைய மூலா-தாரத்திலே gatamānasaḥ gata அடஞ்சிருக்குரமாஸம். மனஸ்ஸை உடைத்தாயிருப்பர். (l.6) ……… vamaheḥ śivamantraத்துநுடைய ஶக்த்யாத்மக: jñānasāmarthyaśaktisvarūpaநாயிருப்பானாயி மாத்ரா தீப்தகலேஶ்வரக்ரமகதே: । (l.7) ………..காராதிமாத்ரைகள்நாலே dīptaprakāśamānamāவிருக்குர கலேபத்து. 19-24. 4. 10. யந்த கலஸகளினால அது வ்ரதமா (p.109b, suprabhede śaivotpatti caryāpādam, l.1)

martyānāṃ malanāśāyā(ya) doṣā(ṣa)muktyarthamuktavaḥ ।
parārthapūjanā .... ye parārthabodhanāya ca ॥
sthāpanaprokṣaṇā dīkṣāvidhinānyakāraṇāt । (l.2)
yo śaivā nirmitā yena śivena paramātmanā ॥
sa śaivabrahmavaktre ca martyalokaṃ vrajet kramāt ।
kāśyapāditi nāmnā ca ṛṣirūpaiśca kīrtitāḥ ॥ (l.3)
ādau śivatvamastīti ādiśaiva idī(tī)ritaḥ ।
śivena dīkṣitāścete te śivabrāhmaṇā(ṇāḥ) smṛtāḥ ॥
sarvottamāḥ parācāryā viprottamāḥ śivottamāḥ । (l.4)
dvijottamottamāścaiva śivabhedāḥ śivātmakāḥ ॥
eṣāṃ paryāya nāmāni saṃkṣepāt kathitaṃ tava ।
kṛmikīṭapada(ta)ṅkebhyaḥ paśava*prājñayājñakāḥ ॥* (l.5)
paśubhyo'pi nārā śreṣṭhā teṣu śreṣṭhā dvijāta(da)yaḥ ।
dvijātiṣvadhikā viprā vipreṣu kṛtubuddhayaḥ ।
kṛtubuddhyeṣu kartārastebhyaḥ sanyāsayo'dhikāḥ ॥
sannyāsayo'dhikā śreṣṭhā teṣu śaṅkarapūjitaḥ ।

(caryāpādaḥ -)
athātassaṃpravakṣyāmi caryāpādavidhikramam । (p.110a, l.1)
(a)sya pādasya pūrve tu śaivānāmudbhavaṃ śruṇu ॥
śaivaṃ pāśupatasomaṃ lākulaṃ tu caturvidham ।
kṛ........vidhaṃ śāstrabhedabhāvamiti *smṛtam ॥* (l.2)
caturvidheṣu śāstreṣu yo bhaktyā dīkṣitaḥ kramāt ।
trayāṇāṃ śāstranāmnā tu procyate sakalāvadhi ॥
caturthe tu sthitānāṃ tu niṣkale *kalavarjitam ।* (l.3)
śaive tu vartate puṃsāṃ śaivāśceti prakīrtitaḥ ॥
pāśupatādiśāstreṣu tathā nāmni udīritāḥ ।
triṣu śāstreṣu bhedāni *bhidyante sva sva mārgakaiḥ ॥* (l.4)
idānīṃ śaivabhedāni vakṣyante tu samāsataḥ ।
śāstraṃ caturvidhaṃ jñeyaṃ vāmaṃ dakṣiṇamiśrakam ॥
siddhāntena samāyuktaṃ *caturthaivaṃ prakīrtitam ।* (l.5)
vāmādiśāstrabhedeṣu ye dīkṣāṃ vartayet kramāt ॥
vāmi ca dakṣiṇī miśrī siddhāntī ca kīrtitāḥ ।
vāmā śāmbhava*śāktāsya dakṣiṇaṃ bhairavātmakam ॥* (l.6)
miśrīyaṃmabhu(yamambhu)mityuktaṃ saptamātṛpradhānakam ।
siddhāntaṃ śuddhaśaivaṃ tu kevalaṃ śāmbhavaṃ sta(ta)thā ॥
siddhāntaṃ *sarvasāraṃ hi tattvaśuddhaśivaṃ smṛṃ(smṛtam ।)* (l.7)
vāmaṃ ca dakṣiṇa...................................... ॥
śaivabāhyāḥ samākhyātāste tu śaivepya*pūjitāḥ ।* (p.110b, f.2, l.1)
kāmikādiprabhedena cāṣṭāviṃśatisaṃhi......... ॥
....................ntaṃ teṣāṃ bhedopabhedakaiḥ ।
siddhāntaṃ miśramityuktaṃ navadhāma *bhu(bhū)saṃjñakam ॥* (l.2)
vāmācāryāstu vai pañca dakṣiṇe'pi tathaiva ca ।
tadvayormiśraguravaḥ pañca pañcaiva coditāḥ ॥
siddhāntaṃ miśraśaivānāṃ pañcānāṃ *lakṣaṇaṃ śruṇu ।* (l.3)
ācāryaḥ sādhakaścaiva samayiputrakastathā ॥
citrakeṭakaityukto mātṛyajñeṣu pūjitaḥ ।
śuddhasiddhāntaśaivakhyadīkṣayā dīkṣitaḥ *kramāt ॥* (l.4)
sādhakādicatuṣkāṇamācāryaḥ tvayi pā(vā) smṛtaḥ ।
mātṛṇāṃ sthāpanaṃ pūjānnāsāṃ vahnestu tarpaṇam ॥
snapanotsavaśāntyādinā cārye*ṇaiva kārayet ।* (l.5)
tanmiśroktāni karmāṇi kuryāttaiḥ sādhakādibhiḥ ॥
śivāgrajā dvadho bhūtvā ācāhcadicatuṣkakā ।
uktaḥ pāraśavaścaiva ci*citrakeṭakanāmataḥ ॥* (l.6)
samāsānmiśraśaivānāṃ yathātathyaṃ śruṇūdbhavam ।
anādiśevā prathamaṃ mahāpo dvitīyakaḥ ॥
ādiśāvāstritīyaśca caturthānuśaivakaḥ । (p.111a, l.1)
pañcamo'ntaraśaivastu śaivāḥ pañcavidhā smṛtāḥ ॥
śakto'dhiṣṭhitaśaktiśca nādo binduriti smi(smṛ)taḥ ।
sadāśiveśvaraścaiva ṣaṭsthāne ye ca *sā sthitāḥ ॥* (l.2)
anādiśaivāhaṃ prokto mahāśaivastata(taḥ) śruṇu ।
anā(na)ntādiśikhaṇyantāstathā mantramaheśvarāḥ ॥
mahāśaivā iti proktā ādiśaivātha *śruṇu ।* (l.3)
māyādyavaniparyantatatvajā teṣu saṃsthitāḥ ॥
ādiśaivā ime proktā anuśaivān tataśśruṇu ।
brahmā viṣṇvādidevāṃśca daityāḥ *kiṃpuruṣāstathā ॥* (l.4)
ṛṣayassiddhagandharvā yakṣarakṣoragādayaḥ ।
śrīkaṇṭhaśiṣyāstaiḥ sarve umādevyādiśaktayaḥ ॥
anuśaivā(vāḥ) smṛtāhyete yantarān *śruṇu sāmpratam ।* (l.5)
brāhmaṇādicaturvarṇā kāśyapādisamudbhavā ॥
parameśaprasādena adhikāri niyojitāḥ ।
śivadharmaprakāśāya *sarveṣāṃ bodhanāya ca ॥* (l.6)
sthāpanā prokṣaṇā dīkṣā vidhinā kṛtyakāraṇāt ।
ṛṣīṇā(ṇāṃ) mūrtimāsthāya śivena paramātmanā ॥

anugṛhītāśca ye sarve *tatra yorbhogavimukhaḥ saśarīrapurassaram ।* (p.111b, f.15?, l.1)
nivedaye daśārdhena prāṇaparyantakandhanaṃ yastu bhogaparādhīnastat sādhanaparigrahaḥ ॥

evaṃ grāmapatiṃ *kṣetramardhaṃ vānarthasañcayam ।* (l.2)
sambhūyāplāśanāścāsmai dadyurvā saṃkhabo(bho)janam ॥
ayaḥ punayarma? sarvasvamamuṃ kuraritavātmaṭham । ?
mahātmā svayamīśena *jñānadṛṣṭyāvilokitaḥ ॥* (l.3)
nādatantena kiñcit sthyāṃ yathāvadvidhipūrvakam ।
kalyāṇāya pravṛtte ........reṣāmātmano'pi hi ॥
yo'nugrahaṃ *karotyevaṃ kārayecca śiveritaḥ ।* (l.4)
na tasya śakyate vaktumidhattā puṇyasaṃhate ॥
etāvacchakyate vaktuṃ kulānāmekaviṃśati ।
pitro divi *vyavasthā svayaṃ gacchet śivaṃ padam ॥* (l.5)
somaśambhupaddhatyāṃ pañcavarṇasupadmeṣu sadyojātādipañcakam ।
pūjayedānupūrveṇa śaivānāṃ *vratamūrtaye ॥* (l.6)
vratāṅgasaṅkhyayā vahnau yajeddivaśataṃ śatam ।
brahmabhirvā hṛdādyaṅgairante vāsinyapoṣite ॥
tasya saṃyamakāpi na daṇḍabhasmajaṭāstataḥ । (l.7)
samarpayedṛdāṅgani sadyojātādiṣu kramāt ॥
caṇḍanāthamathābhyarcya vratāṅgāni visṛjya cā(ca) ।
vidhinānena pānīyapravāhe ca *pravāhayet ॥* (p.112a, l.1)
navavastrayugaṃ datvā brūyāditi deśikaḥ ।
anurūpāṃ kulīnāṃ ca pariṇeṣyasi kanyakām ॥
śaivānāṃ vratasantyāgaḥ sa...*peṇeti deśikaḥ ।* (l.2)

suprabhede –
ante vrateśvaraṃ yāgaṃ kṛtvā tadvratamokṣaṇam ।
vrataṃ vrateśvaraṃ yāgaṃ samāsācchṛṇu tattvataḥ ॥
caturaśramaṇḍalaṃ *kṛtvā gomayenānulipya vai ।* (l.3)
pañcapadmaṃ likhettatra madhye śvetābjamucyate ॥
pūrvābjaṃ pītavarṇaṃ tu kṛṣṇavarṇaṃ tu dakṣiṇe ।
pāṇḍaraṃ *paścime bhāge raktavarṇamathottare ॥* (l.4)
īśānamadhyapadme tu pūrvābje puruṣaṃ tathā ।
aghoraṃ dakṣiṇe padme sadyaṃ paścimapaṅkake ॥ (l.5)
saumye'bje vāmadevaṃ tu īśānādi supūjya vai ।
tattanmantreṇa gandhādyai(dyaiḥ) pūjayet susamāhitaḥ ॥
tattanmantre japaṃ kṛtvā śataṃ vārtha(rdha)kamevā(va vā) । (l.6)
vrateśvaraṃ namaskṛtvā vrapūrṇeśca(ca) yā punaḥ ॥
vratamīśasya vijñāpya pūrvāhne tu visarjayet ।
snapanaṃ tu tataḥ kṛtvā devadevaṃ viśeṣataḥ ॥ (l.7)
praṇamya vratanāthaṃ tu vrataḥ satyaṃ tu śrāvayet ।
vrateśapūjāṃ hyevaṃ vratasiddhikarāya vai ॥
tadabhāve jale liṅge vṛkṣe ..... । (incomplete, p.112b, l.1)

śivarātrinirṇayam –
trayodaśyāṃ bhagākāraḥ liṅgākāraṃ caturdaśī ।
tayoḥ sandhyogakāle *tu śivarātrivrataṃ caret ॥* (l.2)
kumbhasthe ca divānāthena kṛsthe ca *niśākare ।* (l.3), (l.4)
trayodaśī śivāmūrtiḥ śivamūrtī caturdaśī ॥ (p.113a, l.1)
kumbhaphālguṇayormāse yadā kṛṣṇacaturdaśī ।
tadrātri śivarātri syāt *śivenoktaṃ śivāgame ॥* (l.2)
kumbhasthe ca prakartavyaṃ na kartavyaṃ *mṛge ravau ।* (l.3)
pramādātpūjanaṃ kṛtvā rājā(ja)rāṣṭraṃ vinaśyati ॥ (l.4)
ghaṭasthe ca divānāthe mṛgasthe ca niśākare ।
caturdaśī yadā *rātriḥ śivenoktaṃ śivāgame ॥* (l.5)
॥ iti śivarātriḥ ॥ (p.113b, l.1)

(pañcākṣaravidhiḥ -)
ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi pañcākṣaravidhikramam ।
nakāraṃ ca makāraṃ ca śikāraṃ ca vakārakam ॥
yakāraṃ pra(pa)ñcamaṃ proktaṃ *praṇavādiṣaḍakṣaram ।* (l.2)
akṣarāt praṇavaṃ mukhyaṃ śroṇitaṃ prāṇahīnakam ॥
sarvamantreṣu mantrāṇāṃ prāṇāt *praṇavamucyate ।* (l.3)
praṇavaṃ nāmarūpaṃ ca oṅkāraṃ cākṣaraṃ dhṛvam ॥
yāntaṃ bhāntaṃ ca pāntaṃ ca tri(tṛ)tīyasvara*saṃyutam ।* (l.4)
lāntaṃ yugmasmaropetaṃ māntaṃ pañcākṣaraṃ varam ॥
nakārastrisamākhyātaṃ pulliṅgaṃ cākṣara*trayam ।* (l.5)
napuṃsakaṃ yakāraṃ tu liṅgaṃ pañcākṣarasya tu ॥
vakāraṃ sthāvaraṃ caiva yakāraṃ jaṅgamaṃ tathā । (l.6)
nakāraṃ caiva ṛgvedaṃ yajurvedamakārakam ॥
śikāraṃ sāmavedaṃ ca vakāro'tharvaṇaśrutiḥ ।
yakāraṃ praṇavaṃ *caiva sarvavedamayaṃ bhavet ॥* (l.7)
kṣīravarṇaṃ nakāraṃ ca raktavarṇaṃ makārakam ।
śikāraṃ kṛṣṇavarṇaṃ tu vakāraṃ *kuṅkumaprabham ॥* (p.116a, l.1)
yakāraṃ sphaṭikaṃ varṇaṃ pakṣmākṣaraṃ bhavet । ?
nakāraṃ sadyavaktraṃ tu makāraṃ vāmavaktrakam ॥
aghoraṃ ca śikāraṃ tu vakāraṃ *puruṣaṃ tathā ।* (l.2)
īśānaṃ tu yakāraṃ ca liṅgaṃ pañcākṣaraṃ bhavet ॥
nakāraṃ pṛthivīrūpaṃ makāraṃ vāmarūpakam ।
śikāramagnirūpaṃ *tu vakāraṃ vāyurūpakam ॥* (l.3)
yakāraṃ vyomarūpaṃ tu rūpaṃ pakṣaraṃ śivam ।
parāśaktisamāyuktam ādhāraṃ vyomameva ca ॥
jagatī *chandasaṃ yuktaṃ bhāradvājaṛṣistathā ।* (l.4)
sthānamūrdhnimidaṃ vidyāt īśānamiti kīrtitam ॥
sphaṭikābhamidaṃ caiva yākārākṣara*mucyate ।* (l.5)
nakāraṃ sṛṣṭirūpaṃ tu sthitirūpaṃ makārakam ॥
śikāraṃ saṃhṛtiṃ caiva tirobhāvaṃ vakārakam । (l.6)
anugrahaṃ yakāraṃ ca pañcākṣaramidaṃ jagat ॥
nakāre brahmadaivatyaṃ makāre viṣṇudaivatam ।
śikāre rudra*daivatyaṃ vakāre tvīśvaraṃ tathā ॥* (l.7)
sadāśivaṃ yakāre tu śya(hya)kṣareṣu ca bhāvayet ।
nakāre tu vasiṣṭhaṃ ca sāvitrī *śaktisaṃyutam ॥* (p.116b, f.102a?, l.1)
makāre vaiṣṇavīśakti viśvāmitraṛṣistathā ।
icchāśaktiḥ śikāre tu marīciṛṣirucyate ॥
bhogaśakti vakāre tu *ṛṣiḥ kāśyapa eva ca ।* (l.2)
yakāre tvambikāśakti bhāradvājaṛṣistathā ॥
praṇavaṃ tvātmarūpaṃ ca paramātmādhidaivatam ।
sthānaṃ *śivamayaṃ caiva tatkarṇaṃ gṛhyameva ca ॥* (l.3)
tasya bodhanasaṃyuktaṃ praṇavākṣaramucyate ।
yattā(tkā/kā)raṃ liṅgamityuktaṃ *ratnamāyasamanvitam ॥* (l.4)
mūlamantramidaṃ proktaṃ vakāraṃ nālameva ca ।
śikāramūrdhvapadmaṃ tu makāraṃ kaṇṭhamucyate ॥ (l.5)
adhopadmaṃ nakāraṃ tu akṣaraṃ liṅgarūpakam ।
pañcākṣaravidhiṃ proktaṃ tatphalaṃ śruṇu ṣaṇmukham ॥ (l.6)
pañcākṣaraṃ ca ṣaḍbhedaṃ tāraprasādamiśra(śri)tam ।
sthūlaṃ sūkṣmaṃ ca śuddhaṃ ca atroccārakraṃ(kramaṃ) śruṇu ॥
tadonnamaśśivāyeti tārapañcākṣaraṃ bhavet ।
yathā –
oṃ namaśśivāya ।
praṇavaṃ pūrvamuccārya sāntaṃ *dīrghasvarānvitam ।* (p.117a, l.1)
binduṃ sāntaṃ caropetaṃ bindunādasamanvitam ॥

	yathā – oṃ hāṃ hauṃ śivāya namaḥ । iti prāsādapañcākṣaraṃ (l.2) namaśśivāya śivāya namaḥ iti caret । kramāt yathā – namaśśivāya śivāya namaḥ । ityetat sthūlasūkṣmakam । śivāya namaśśivāya namaḥ । (l.3) ityucyate sadā । yathā śivāya namaśi(śśi)vāya namaḥ । iti miśrapañcākṣaram । sūkṣmaṃ namaḥ padam । pūrvaṃ śuddha(l.4)pañcākṣaraṃ bhavet । namaḥ padaṃ samuccārya । śivāya nama ityapi । namaśśivāya iti sūkṣmapañcākṣaram । śivāya (l.5) nama ityetat śuddhapañcākṣaraṃ sadā । yathā śivāya nama iti śuddhapañcākṣaram iti । śuddhapañcākṣaramidaṃ svānubhūtiprakārakaṃ pañcākṣarasya (l.6) tanmadhye pati pāśa paśūktimān dhyātvā patiṃ śivaṃ, pāśe'sya(syā)rtha(p.117b, f.103a?, l.1)pañcāpaśāvaṇūn ।
tanmadhye mūlamāyākhyā pṛthakkuryātma(ryādātma)darśane ।
tāraprāsādamādau tu pūjāṃ viprasya cocyate ॥ (l.2)
dīkṣitānāṃ ca viprāṇāṃ pūjādau tu viśiṣyate ।
prāsādapañcākṣaraṃ ca sthūlasūkṣma na ducyate ॥
dīkṣitānāṃ kṣatriyāṇāṃ pūjādau *tu vidhīyyate ।* (l.3)
dīkṣitānāṃ ca vaiśyānāṃ miśrapañcākṣaraṃ smṛtam ॥
yogīnāṃ jñānīnāṃ tatra sūkṣmapañcākṣaṃ *smṛtam ।* (l.4)
dīkṣitānāṃ ca śūdrāṇāṃ śuddhapañcākṣaraṃ smṛtam ॥
dīkṣitānāṃ naiṣṭhikānāṃ śuddhapañcākṣaraṃ *sadā ।* (l.5)
strīṇāmapi ca śūdrāṇāṃ tadvadeva samācaret ॥
viprāṇāmādiśaivānāṃ navādikalasaṃyutam । (l.6)
pañcākṣaraṃ japedanye pūrvoktabrāhmaṇe(ṇo)ktavat ॥
japeyurjapakālādau tadvidhānamihocyate ।
uktānāmapi varṇānāṃ kriyāṇāṃ *ca vidhānadaḥ(taḥ) ॥* (l.7)
śaivānāmapi viprāṇāṃ dīkṣitānāmihocyate ।
japakāle namaḥpūrvaṃ pūjāyāṃ ca namontakam । (p.118a, l.1)
svāhāntaṃ homakāle tu tvanyeṣūktaprakārataḥ ॥
dīkṣitānāṃ ca śūdrāṇāṃ vidhānamadhunocyate ।
jape home tu pūjāyā*manuṣṭhāne tu niṣkṛtau ॥* (l.2)
dīkṣāyāṃ carameṣā tu śrāddhe dāne'nyakālataḥ ।
namontenaiva mantreṇa sarvamevaṃ samācaret ॥ (l.3)
tadvat strīṇāṃ ca śūdrāṇāmevamevaṃ samācaret ।
yaḥ śāstravidhimutsṛjya vartate kāmacārataḥ ॥
gatāyurnarake ghore *vaset kalpaśatatrayam ।* (l.4)
iti samyak samīkṣya tu mantraṃ pañcākṣaraṃ mahat ॥
brāhmaṇādiṣu varṇeṣu yojayettu *gurūttamaḥ ।* (l.5)
pañcākṣaramahāmantraṃ pañcapātakanāśanam ॥
cintayenmanasā vācā śivasāyujyamāpnuyāt ।
vaṭabīja*vadatyalpamakṣaraṃ vedasūcitam ॥* (l.6)
pañcākṣarasya māhātmyaṃ mayā vaktuṃ na śakyate ।
namaḥpadaṃ tat khalu jīvayācī śivaḥ padam ॥
tatparamārthavāciḥ āyeti tadarthyaparā caturthī । so'haṃ parabrahmaśivāsmyakhaṇḍaṃ śikāraṃ sukhamityuktam । yīkāraṃ śaktirucyate । vakāramamṛtam । prāhuḥ śiva ityakṣaradvayam । (p.118b, pravaram, l.1) paraśivaparameśvara parāpara paraṃ jyoti paramātmaka pañcārṣayapravarānvita । paraśivatotrodbhavasya varṇapadatattvamantra(l.2)bhuvanakalātmikaṣaḍadhvātītavigrahasya sakalabhuvanasṛṣṭisthitisaṃhāratirobhāvā-nugrahākhyapañcakṛtyasaṃpannasya sakalaniṣkala(l.3)svarūpasya nirañjanadevasya naptre paraśivagotrodbhasya siddhāntaprasiddheśvaraṃ tantrādhiṣṭhitasya (l.4) aṇimādyaṣṭaiśvaryaṃ pradhānaikaparāśaktiramaṇīramaṇasya nirāmayākhyasya pautrāyā(ya) paraśivagotrodbhavasya (l.5) sakalabhuvanasṛṣṭisthitisaṃhāratirobhāvānugrahākhyakārakasya yogijanahṛtsaroruhanilayasya trimūrti(l.6)guṇārmakasya ādiśaktiramaṇīramasya niṣkalaṅkasya putrāya paraśivagotrodbhava(vā)ya sarvalokaśaranyā(ṇyā)ya sarvaloka(l.7)sukhaṅkarāya śrīhariprasādeśvarasvāmine varāya paraśaktiparāśaktiparāparaśaktikriyā ṛṣayapravarānvitaparaśakti (p.119a, l.1)gotrodbhavasya bhūrādisamastalokānāṃ vaikuṇṭhanagarasyopari vartamāna-kailāsādupavāsasya brahmaviṣṇvādināmādijanakasya (l.2) sūryamaṇḍalāntarvartamānavedāntavedyasya parañjyotiśarmaṇo naptri । paraśaktigotrodbhavasya satyalokanivāsibhiḥ । sanakādibhiḥ । deva(l.3)ṛṣibhiḥ brahmaṛṣibhiḥ sevyamānasarasvatī ramaṇīramaṇasya caturmukhabrahmaśarmaṇaḥ pautrīṃ paraśaktigotrodbhavasya gandhamādinā-maṣṭakula(l.4)parvatānāmadhīśvarasya menakā ramaṇīramaṇasya bhagavato hivataśarmaṇaḥ putriṃ paraśaktigotrodbhavāṃ (l.5) sarvamaṅgalarūpiṇī sakalasāṃbrā(mrā)jyapradāyinīṃ girijāṃ dharmavardhanābhyāṃ kanyāṃ vṛṇīdhvaṃ dāsyāmi ।
